How reliable are LLMs when it comes to playing dice?
Authors: Luca Avena, Gianmarco Bet, Bernardo Busoni
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:59:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate the probabilistic reasoning capabilities of large language models through a controlled benchmarking study on discrete probability problems. We constructed two datasets, respectively a set of standard exercises and a set of counterintuitive exercises, designed to trigger heuristic reasoning, and evaluated 8 state-of-the-art models, each tested with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting. Models achieve an average accuracy of 0.96 on standard problems but only 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. We further provide empirical evidence of token bias: performance drops by over 20% when canonical formulations are replaced by disguised variants. Embedding misleading suggestions in the prompt reduces performance by up to 34%, with no model proving immune. Taken together, the reported findings suggest that current LLMs are not yet genuine probabilistic reasoners, despite their success in advanced mathematical problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在掷骰子游戏中，大语言模型的可靠性如何？
我们通过一项受控基准测试，研究了大语言模型在离散概率问题上的概率推理能力。我们构建了两个数据集：一组标准练习题和一组反直觉练习题，旨在触发启发式推理，并评估了8个最先进的模型（每个模型均在有和无思维链提示的情况下测试）。模型在标准问题上的平均准确率为0.96，但在反直觉问题上仅为0.59。我们进一步提供了词元偏倚的实证证据：当标准表述被伪装变体替代时，性能下降超过20%。在提示中嵌入误导性建议可使性能下降高达34%，没有任何模型完全免疫。综合来看，这些发现表明，尽管大语言模型在高级数学问题上表现优异，但目前它们尚未成为真正的概率推理者。
Summary / 总结
This study examines how well large language models handle probabilistic reasoning by testing them on discrete probability problems, using two datasets—one with standard exercises and another with counterintuitive ones designed to expose heuristic biases. Eight state-of-the-art models were evaluated both with and without Chain-of-Thought prompting, revealing that while they scored 0.96 on standard problems, their accuracy dropped to 0.59 on counterintuitive ones. The research also found that performance degraded by over 20% when problems were disguised and by up to 34% when misleading suggestions were embedded in prompts, indicating that current LLMs lack robust probabilistic reasoning despite excelling in advanced math.
本研究通过测试离散概率问题，评估大语言模型的 probabilistic reasoning 能力，包括标准题与反直觉题两类。八款前沿模型在启用与禁用 Chain-of-Thought 提示下测试，结果显示在标准题上准确率达 96%，但在反直觉题上骤降至 59%。研究还发现，问题表述的微小变化（如伪装标准形式或嵌入误导性提示）可使准确率下降高达 34%，表明模型易受提示干扰，尚未具备真正的概率推理能力。
UniSHARP: Universal Sharp Monocular View Synthesis
Authors: Meixi Song, Dizhe Zhang, Hao Ren, Ruiyang Zhang, Bo Du, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Lu Qi
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:59:41+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we focus on extending SHARP, the popular photorealistic view synthesis method, for universal monocular rendering across a continuum of camera systems, from conventional perspective cameras to wide-field-of-view, fisheye and omnidirectional panoramic settings. To overcome the pinhole-specific assumptions of SHARP, our key idea is to align various images in a unified omnidirectional latent space. Thus, we propose UniSHARP, which performs implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. Specifically, Gaussian primitives are arranged along rays and radial distances in a ray-based universal representation, while 2D semantic and 3D spatial features extracted from UniK3D-inspired encoders are jointly decoded to generate the complete Gaussian cloud. To comprehensively evaluate our method, we construct a benchmark covering diverse imaging systems across various scenes. The benchmark is further stratified by field of view (FoV) to enable fine-grained assessment of the universal monocular rendering task. Extensive experiments on the proposed benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of UniSHARP, outperforming alternative methods by a large margin. The project page can be found at: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniSHARP：通用单目视角合成
本工作旨在扩展流行的逼真视角合成方法SHARP，使其适用于从传统透视相机到广角、鱼眼和全景相机系统的连续相机配置下的通用单目渲染。为克服SHARP对针孔相机假设的依赖，我们的核心思想是在统一的全景潜在空间中对齐各类图像。因此，我们提出UniSHARP，它在特征空间和高斯空间中进行隐式对齐。具体而言，高斯原语沿射线和径向距离在基于射线的通用表示中排列，而从受UniK3D启发的编码器提取的2D语义和3D空间特征联合解码以生成完整的高斯云。为全面评估我们的方法，我们构建了一个涵盖多种场景下不同成像系统的基准数据集，并按视场角（FoV）分层，以实现对通用单目渲染任务的细粒度评估。在所提出的基准上的大量实验表明，UniSHARP显著优于现有方法。项目页面：https://insta360-research-team.github.io/Unisharp-website/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generalize photorealistic monocular view synthesis across diverse camera systems, this work extends SHARP to handle conventional, wide-angle, fisheye, and omnidirectional cameras. UniSHARP achieves this by aligning images in a unified omnidirectional latent space through implicit alignment in both feature and Gaussian spaces. It uses ray-based Gaussian primitives and jointly decodes 2D semantic and 3D spatial features from UniK3D-inspired encoders to reconstruct a complete Gaussian cloud. Evaluated on a new benchmark stratified by field of view, UniSHARP consistently outperforms existing methods, demonstrating strong generalization and fidelity across imaging systems.
为突破SHARP对针孔相机的假设限制，UniSHARP旨在实现从常规透视相机到鱼眼和全景相机的通用单目视图合成。其核心方法是在统一的球面隐式空间中对齐不同图像，通过特征与高斯空间的隐式对齐，将高斯点沿射线和径向距离排列，并结合UniK3D启发的编码器提取的2D语义与3D空间特征重建完整高斯云。在按视场角分层构建的新基准测试中，UniSHARP在各类相机系统上显著优于现有方法，展现出强大的泛化能力和渲染保真度。
Agentopia: Long-Term Life Simulation and Learning in Agent Societies
Authors: Xintao Wang, Sirui Zheng, Hongqiu Wu, Weiyuan Li, Jen-tse Huang, Minghao Zhu, Can Zu, Qi Deng, Jiawei Wang, Qianyu He, Heng Wang, Xiaojian Wu, Yunzhe Tao
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:59:31+00:00
Comments: 79 pages, 19 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans learn from social life. Simulating this process with LLM-powered agents represents a promising research direction, raising a natural question: whether LLMs can learn from such simulated social experience to better understand and replicate human behavior. However, prior agent society simulations typically operate at the scale of days, limiting the depth of social interactions and long-term growth. In this paper, we study long-term life simulation and LLM learning in agent societies, with two goals: (1) investigating social behaviors that emerge from life-long simulation, and (2) developing anthropomorphic capabilities in LLMs, particularly intelligence in social life, through years of simulated social experience. Specifically, we present Agentopia, a comprehensive framework for long-term life simulation in multi-agent societies, where 100 agents autonomously pursue personal growth, develop social relationships, and fulfill their needs and goals over 10 simulated years. We define life reward to mirror human well-being, and leverage this reward to train LLMs via rejection sampling. Extensive experiments show that agents exhibit rich emergent social behaviors. Furthermore, life reward training effectively enhances the underlying LLM, which leads to improved agent well-being in simulation, and generalizes to downstream role-playing benchmarks with +15.6% improvement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Agentopia：代理社会中的长期生命模拟与学习
人类从社会生活中学习。利用大语言模型（LLM）驱动的代理模拟这一过程，是一个有前景的研究方向，自然引发一个问题：LLM能否从这种模拟的社会经验中学习，以更好地理解和复制人类行为？然而，以往的代理社会模拟通常仅在数天尺度上运行，限制了社会互动的深度和长期成长。本文研究代理社会中的长期生命模拟与LLM学习，目标有两个：（1）探究从终身模拟中涌现的社会行为；（2）通过数年的模拟社会经验，发展LLM的人类化能力，特别是社会生活中的智能。具体而言，我们提出了Agentopia，一个用于多代理社会中长期生命模拟的综合框架，其中100个代理自主追求个人成长、发展社会关系，并在10年模拟时间内满足自身需求与目标。我们定义生命奖励以反映人类福祉，并利用该奖励通过拒绝采样训练LLM。大量实验表明，代理展现出丰富的涌现社会行为。此外，生命奖励训练有效提升了底层LLM，从而在模拟中改善了代理福祉，并在下游角色扮演基准测试中泛化出+15.6%的提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential for LLMs to learn human-like social intelligence through simulated long-term life experiences, this paper introduces Agentopia, a framework enabling 100 autonomous agents to simulate 10 years of life, pursuing personal growth and social relationships. Agents are trained using a life reward function designed to mirror human well-being, and LLMs are fine-tuned via rejection sampling based on this reward. Experimental results show that agents develop rich emergent social behaviors and that the life reward training significantly improves the LLM’s performance in simulated well-being and downstream role-playing tasks, achieving a +15.6% improvement.
受人类通过社会生活学习的启发，本文研究大语言模型是否能通过长期模拟人际互动获得社交智能。作者提出Agentopia框架，其中100个自主代理在10年模拟生活中追求个人目标、建立关系并优化“生活奖励”（模拟人类幸福感）。通过拒绝采样训练LLM，实验表明代理展现出丰富的涌现社会行为，且基于生活奖励的训练显著提升LLM在角色扮演任务中的表现，提升幅度达15.6%。
Predicting Dynamic Map States from Limited Field-of-View Sensor Data
Authors: Knut Peterson, David Han
First: 2026-02-12T19:36:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:59:26+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 4 figures. Accepted to the 2026 International Conference on Advanced Visual and Signal-Based Systems (AVSS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When autonomous systems are deployed in real-world scenarios, sensors are often subject to limited field-of-view (FOV) constraints, either naturally through system design, or through unexpected occlusions or sensor failures. In conditions where a large FOV is unavailable, it is important to be able to infer information about the environment and predict the state of nearby surroundings based on available data to maintain safe and accurate operation. In this work, we explore the effectiveness of deep learning for dynamic map state prediction based on limited FOV time series data. We show that by representing dynamic sensor data in a simple single-image format that captures both spatial and temporal information, we can effectively use a wide variety of existing image-to-image learning models to predict map states with high accuracy in a diverse set of sensing scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从有限视场传感器数据预测动态地图状态
当自主系统部署于现实场景时，传感器常受限于有限视场（FOV），或因系统设计、意外遮挡或传感器故障所致。在大视场不可用的情况下，需基于现有数据推断环境信息并预测周边状态，以确保安全准确运行。本文探讨了深度学习在基于有限FOV时序数据预测动态地图状态中的有效性。我们表明，通过将动态传感器数据表示为捕捉空间与时间信息的单张图像格式，可有效利用多种现有图像到图像学习模型，在多种传感场景中高精度预测地图状态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of operating autonomous systems under limited field-of-view sensor constraints, this work investigates using deep learning to predict dynamic map states from sparse temporal sensor data. The method encodes time-series sensor observations into a single composite image that integrates spatial and temporal context, enabling the use of standard image-to-image models for accurate state prediction. Experiments across diverse sensing scenarios demonstrate that this approach achieves high prediction accuracy despite limited FOV, effectively bridging the gap between sensor constraints and environmental understanding.
为了解决自主系统在传感器视场受限条件下仍需准确感知环境的问题，该研究探索了利用深度学习从稀疏时序传感器数据中预测动态地图状态的方法。该方法将连续传感器观测编码为融合空间与时间信息的单张图像，从而可直接复用现有的图像到图像学习模型。实验结果表明，该方法在多种传感场景下均能实现高精度预测，即使在视场受限于设计或遮挡的情况下，仍可有效支持自主系统的安全可靠运行。
MemDreamer: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Long Video Understanding via Hierarchical Graph Memory and Agentic Retrieval Mechanism
Authors: Cong Chen, Guo Gan, Kaixiang Ji, ChaoYang Zhang, Zhen Yang, Guangming Yao, Hao Chen, Jingdong Chen, Yi Yuan, Chunhua Shen
First: 2026-06-05T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:59:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current Vision-Language Models struggle with hours-long videos because processing full-length visual sequences induces prohibitive token explosion and attention dilution. To overcome this, we introduce MemDreamer to decouple perception and reasoning, shifting long-video understanding into an agentic exploration process. As a plug-and-play framework, it incrementally streams videos to construct a Hierarchical Graph Memory, a top-down three-tier architecture for semantic abstraction, anchored by a foundational graph capturing spatiotemporal and causal relations. During inference, the reasoning model employs agentic tool-augmented retrieval, navigating hierarchies, searching nodes, and traversing logical edges via an Observation-Reason-Action loop. Experiments show MemDreamer achieves SOTA results across four mainstream benchmarks, narrowing the gap with human experts to only 3.7 points. It constrains the reasoning context window to merely 2% of full-context ingestion while delivering a 12.5 point absolute accuracy gain. Furthermore, statistical analysis uncovers a strong positive linear correlation between an VLM's performance on logic reasoning and long-video understanding benchmarks, establishing agentic capability scaling as a new paradigm for multimodal comprehension.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemDreamer：通过分层图记忆与代理检索机制解耦感知与推理以实现长视频理解
当前视觉-语言模型在处理数小时长视频时面临挑战，因为处理完整视觉序列会导致不可接受的令牌爆炸和注意力稀释。为解决此问题，我们提出MemDreamer，通过解耦感知与推理，将长视频理解转化为代理探索过程。该框架可即插即用，逐帧流式处理视频以构建分层图记忆——一种自顶向下的三级语义抽象架构，以基础图锚定时空与因果关系。推理阶段，推理模型采用代理工具增强检索，通过观察-推理-行动循环在层级间导航、搜索节点并遍历逻辑边。实验表明，MemDreamer在四个主流基准上均取得最先进结果，与人类专家差距仅3.7分。其将推理上下文窗口限制在完整上下文摄入的2%，同时实现12.5分绝对准确率提升。此外，统计分析揭示视觉-语言模型在逻辑推理与长视频理解基准上的表现存在强正线性相关，确立代理能力扩展为多模态理解的新范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current vision-language models in handling long videos due to token explosion and attention dilution, MemDreamer decouples perception and reasoning by modeling video understanding as an agentic exploration process. It uses a hierarchical graph memory with a three-tier semantic abstraction structure to incrementally stream and encode video content, while a reasoning module employs tool-augmented retrieval to navigate the graph via an Observation-Reason-Action loop. Experiments demonstrate that MemDreamer achieves state-of-the-art performance on four benchmarks, reducing the gap to human performance to just 3.7 points, while limiting context to only 2% of full video and still gaining 12.5 absolute accuracy points. Statistical analysis further reveals a strong positive correlation between VLM logic reasoning ability and long-video understanding, validating agentic scaling as a new paradigm for multimodal comprehension.
为解决当前视觉语言模型在处理长视频时因令牌爆炸和注意力稀释导致的效率低下问题，MemDreamer通过引入分层图记忆与代理检索机制，将感知与推理解耦，将长视频理解转化为代理探索过程。该框架通过增量式视频流构建三级语义抽象图，捕捉时空与因果关系，并在推理时通过观察-推理-行动循环结合工具增强检索进行导航与逻辑推理。实验表明其在四个主流基准上达到最先进水平，与人类表现差距仅3.7分，同时将推理上下文压缩至完整视频的2%，却获得12.5分绝对准确率提升。统计分析进一步发现视觉语言模型的逻辑推理能力与长视频理解能力呈强正相关，验证了代理能力扩展作为多模态理解新范式的有效性。
Streaming Video Generation with Streaming Force Control
Authors: Hanhui Wang, Yiming Xie, Haiwen Feng, Zhaoyang Lv, Shenlong Wang, Huaizu Jiang
First: 2026-06-05T17:57:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:57:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce StreamForce, a streaming video generation framework that enables physically grounded control through continuous force inputs. Unlike prior video models that train separate models for different force types, assume fixed forces, or rely on non-causal processing, StreamForce is a causal and unified model that responds instantly and coherently to both local and global, time-varying forces. To achieve this, we design a unified force representation as a control signal and develop a distillation pipeline for force-controllable video generation. Our model combines autoregressive efficiency with force responsiveness, sustaining stable photometric and dynamic realism. StreamForce runs at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both force adherence and motion realism. Project website: https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于流式力控的视频生成
我们提出了StreamForce，一种支持物理基础控制的流式视频生成框架，通过连续的力输入实现即时且连贯的响应。与先前视频模型需为不同力类型训练独立模型、假设固定力或依赖非因果处理不同，StreamForce是一个因果统一模型，可对局部和全局、随时间变化的力做出即时且连贯的响应。为此，我们设计了统一的力表示作为控制信号，并开发了力可控视频生成的蒸馏流程。该模型结合了自回归效率与力响应能力，保持了稳定的光度和动态真实感。StreamForce在单GPU上可达到最高16.6 FPS，同时在力遵循度和运动真实感方面均达到最先进水平。项目网站：https://neu-vi.github.io/StreamForce/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically realistic and controllable video generation, StreamForce introduces a unified, causal framework that responds instantly to time-varying force inputs without requiring separate models or non-causal processing. It employs a novel force representation as a control signal and a distillation pipeline to enable force-controllable video synthesis while preserving photometric and dynamic realism. Experiments show that StreamForce achieves state-of-the-art force adherence and motion realism, running at up to 16.6 FPS on a single GPU.
为实现物理真实且可控的视频生成，StreamForce 提出一种统一的因果框架，可即时响应时变力输入，无需独立模型或非因果处理。该框架采用力信号作为控制输入，并结合蒸馏流程实现力控视频生成，同时保持光度与动态真实感。实验表明，该模型在力响应准确性和运动真实感上达到当前最优，单 GPU 下可稳定运行至 16.6 FPS。
Affordance-Based Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Quadruped Pedipulation
Authors: Tuba Girgin, Jose Castelblanco, Gabriel Rodriguez, Emre Girgin, Cagri Kilic
First: 2026-06-05T17:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:56:42+00:00
Comments: This paper is submitted to Wiley Journal of Field Robotics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The object manipulation capabilities of quadruped robots is an open research challenge. While previous studies have focused on low-level policy learning, task execution still relies on expert-designed high-level trajectories. Autonomous selection of both an affordable interaction point on the target object and an affordable robot base pose removes the need for pre-designed trajectories. This study proposes a three-level hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework that utilizes pose affordances to guide the navigation policy, while the navigation policy drives the locomotion policy. In addition, the pedipulation policy is guided by interaction-point affordances, enabling object-centric pose alignment of the quadruped robot and effective end-effector manipulation planning. We train the proposed framework in the IsaacSim ecosystem and evaluate it in both simulation and real-world settings. We investigate the effectiveness of pose affordance across multiple scenarios in simulation while various object interaction tasks are validated on real-world setting forming an object-interaction dataset. The results show that the proposed framework can autonomously identify candidate poses based on their affordance and successfully execute object manipulation tasks in the real world without human guidance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于可操作性的四足机器人步态操作的分层强化学习
四足机器人的物体操作能力仍是开放研究挑战。尽管先前研究集中于低层策略学习，任务执行仍依赖专家设计的高层轨迹。自主选择目标物体上可操作的交互点及机器人基座可操作姿态，消除了对预设轨迹的需求。本文提出一种三层分层强化学习框架，利用姿态可操作性引导导航策略，导航策略驱动运动策略；同时，操作策略由交互点可操作性引导，实现以物体为中心的姿态对齐及末端执行器有效操作规划。我们在IsaacSim环境中训练该框架，并在仿真与真实世界中评估。仿真中研究姿态可操作性在多种场景中的有效性，真实世界中验证多种物体交互任务，形成物体交互数据集。结果表明，该框架可自主识别基于可操作性的候选姿态，并在无人工干预下成功执行真实世界物体操作任务。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable quadruped robots to autonomously manipulate objects without pre-designed trajectories, this study introduces a three-level hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that leverages pose affordances to guide navigation and locomotion policies, while interaction-point affordances direct the pedipulation policy for object-centric manipulation. Trained in IsaacSim and tested in both simulation and real-world settings, the framework autonomously selects feasible interaction points and robot base poses based on affordance signals, enabling successful object manipulation without human intervention. Experimental results demonstrate robust performance across diverse scenarios in simulation and validated object-interaction tasks in the real world, forming a new dataset for future research.
为使四足机器人无需预设轨迹即可自主操作物体，本研究提出一种基于姿态可操作性的三层分层强化学习框架。该框架将导航、运动与操作策略解耦，利用姿态可操作性引导机器人自主选择交互点与基座姿态。在仿真与真实环境中验证后，该方法无需人工干预即可完成多种物体操作任务，展现出良好的场景泛化能力，并能生成以物体为中心的姿态对齐，实现有效末端执行器控制。
Differences in Detection: Explainability Where it Matters
Authors: Johannes Theodoridis, Johannes Maucher, Andreas Schilling
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-06-05T17:54:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:54:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (CVPR) Workshops 2026 - How Do Vision Models Work? (HOW)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We propose Differences in Detection (DnD), an intuitive method to compare two object detection models. Based on the same matching algorithm, it complements the standard metrics of mean Average Precision ($mAP$) and TIDE error analysis with the ability to compare two models directly. More specifically, we calculate the intersection of ground truth labels that are recognized by both models, followed by the corresponding difference sets and the complement set of ground truth labels that are missed by both models. The resulting comparison is more direct and intuitive than a comparison of independent summary statistics. It reveals individual and shared mistakes and becomes particularly interesting when combined with error types. In this case, the differences in detection errors can be analyzed naturally in a standard confusion matrix. While valuable in itself, we believe that one of the best applications of DnD is to guide explainability methods such as ODAM towards metric-relevant examples, grounded in structured subsets. The code for our method is available here: https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection
中文标题/摘要
标题：检测差异：关键处的可解释性
我们提出了检测差异（DnD），一种直观比较两个目标检测模型的方法。基于相同的匹配算法，它在标准指标（mAP 和 TIDE 错误分析）的基础上，增加了直接比较两个模型的能力。具体而言，我们计算两个模型均识别出的真实标签交集，随后计算各自的差集以及两个模型均遗漏的真实标签补集。这种比较比独立统计量的比较更直接、更直观，能揭示个体和共同错误，尤其在结合错误类型时更具意义。此时，检测错误差异可自然地在标准混淆矩阵中分析。虽然本身有价值，但我们认为 DnD 最佳的应用是引导如 ODAM 等可解释性方法，使其聚焦于结构化子集中的指标相关样本。方法代码见：https://github.com/JohannesTheo/differences-in-detection
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for direct, intuitive comparisons between object detection models beyond standard metrics like mAP, the paper introduces Differences in Detection (DnD), a method that leverages a shared matching algorithm to compute the intersection of detected ground truth labels and their respective difference sets, revealing both individual and shared errors. By analyzing these differences in the context of a confusion matrix, DnD enables a more granular understanding of model performance, especially when combined with error type annotations. Experimental results show that DnD effectively highlights model-specific and shared failure modes, and its application can guide explainability methods like ODAM to focus on metric-relevant examples, improving interpretability through structured subsets.
为弥补标准指标（如mAP）在模型间直接比较上的不足，本文提出Differences in Detection（DnD）方法，基于统一匹配算法计算两个模型对真实标签的交集与差集，从而直观揭示各自与共同的检测错误。结合错误类型分析，该方法可自然映射至混淆矩阵，实现细粒度错误诊断。其核心价值在于能引导如ODAM等可解释性方法聚焦于与指标强相关的结构化样本子集。
Your UnEmbedding Matrix is Secretly a Feature Lens for Text Embeddings
Authors: Songhao Wu, Zhongxin Chen, Yuxuan Liu, Heng Cui, Cong Li, Rui Yan
First: 2026-06-05T17:54:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:54:32+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models exhibit impressive zero-shot capabilities across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, they struggle to function as off-the-shelf embedding models, leading to suboptimal performance on massive text embedding benchmarks. In this paper, we identify a potential cause underlying this deficiency. Our motivation stems from an unexpected observation: text embeddings tend to align with frequent but uninformative tokens when projected onto the vocabulary space. We argue that this excessive expression of high-frequency tokens suppresses the model's ability to capture nuanced semantics. To address this, we introduce EmbedFilter, a simple linear transformation designed to refine text embeddings derived from LLMs directly. Specifically, we uncover that the unembedding matrix within LLMs encodes a latent space that is actively writing these frequent tokens into embedding space. By filtering out this subspace, EmbedFilter suppress the influence of high-frequency tokens, thereby enhancing semantic representations. As a compelling byproduct, this enables an inherent dimensionality reduction, lowering index storage and speedup retrieval while fully preserving the refined embedding quality. Our experiments across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that LLMs equipped with EmbedFilter achieve superior zero-shot downstream performance even with significantly reduced embedding dimensions. We hope our findings provide deeper insights into the mechanisms of LLM-based representations and inspire more principled designs to improve text embeddings training. Our code is available at https://github.com/CentreChen/EmbFilter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的去嵌入矩阵其实是在为文本嵌入提供特征滤镜
大型语言模型在多种下游任务中展现出令人印象深刻的零样本能力，但作为即插即用的嵌入模型时表现不佳，导致在大规模文本嵌入基准测试中性能欠佳。本文识别出这一缺陷的潜在原因：文本嵌入在投影到词汇空间时倾向于与高频但无信息量的词对齐。我们认为，这种对高频词的过度表达抑制了模型捕捉细微语义的能力。为解决此问题，我们提出EmbedFilter，一种直接优化LLM生成文本嵌入的简单线性变换。具体而言，我们发现LLM中的去嵌入矩阵编码了一个潜在空间，该空间主动将高频词写入嵌入空间。通过过滤该子空间，EmbedFilter削弱高频词的影响，从而增强语义表示。作为显著副产品，这实现了内在降维，降低索引存储开销并加速检索，同时完全保留优化后的嵌入质量。在多个LLM架构上的实验表明，配备EmbedFilter的LLM即使在大幅降低嵌入维度的情况下，仍能实现更优的零样本下游性能。我们希望这些发现能深化对基于LLM表示机制的理解，并启发更严谨的设计以改进文本嵌入训练。代码见https://github.com/CentreChen/EmbFilter。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that LLM-generated text embeddings overly emphasize frequent but semantically uninformative tokens, the authors propose EmbedFilter, a simple linear transformation applied to LLM embeddings to suppress the influence of these tokens. They find that the unembedding matrix inherently encodes a subspace that maps high-frequency tokens into embedding space, thereby diluting nuanced semantic signals. By filtering this subspace, EmbedFilter enhances semantic representation quality while simultaneously reducing embedding dimensionality, which accelerates retrieval and lowers storage costs without sacrificing performance. Experiments across multiple LLM backbones show that EmbedFilter-equipped models achieve superior zero-shot performance on downstream tasks even with compressed embeddings.
鉴于LLM文本嵌入过度强调高频但无信息量的词元，导致在嵌入基准测试中表现不佳，作者提出EmbedFilter，一种直接作用于LLM生成嵌入的简单线性变换。该方法利用未嵌入矩阵的潜在结构，过滤掉与高频词元相关的子空间，从而抑制其语义主导性，提升语义表达的精细度。实验表明，EmbedFilter在多个LLM架构上显著提升零样本下游任务性能，同时实现维度压缩，不损失嵌入质量，从而加速检索并降低存储开销。
Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning
Authors: Fatema Siddika, Md Anwar Hossen, Tanwi Mallick, Ali Jannesari
First: 2026-06-05T17:53:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:53:52+00:00
Comments: 19 pages. arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2601.17616
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向任务无关持续学习的稀疏子空间到专家共享
大语言模型（LLMs）的持续学习受可塑性-稳定性困境制约，获取新能力常导致先前知识灾难性遗忘。现有方法通常统一处理参数，未能区分特定任务知识与共享能力。我们提出任务无关持续学习的稀疏专家混合框架（SETA），通过自适应稀疏子空间分解为任务特定专家模块，解决可塑性-稳定性冲突。与标准更新中任务竞争同一参数不同，SETA将知识分离为独特专家（隔离任务特定模式）与共享专家（捕捉通用特征）。该结构通过自适应弹性锚定与路由感知正则化联合保护权重与路由层面的共享知识，并使统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确专家组合。在多个领域特定基准上的广泛实验表明，SETA相较当前持续学习基线实现竞争力或更优整体性能，尤其在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B模型中显著保留早期任务知识并提升反向迁移能力。
Summary / 总结
To address the plasticity-stability dilemma in continual learning for large language models, where acquiring new tasks causes forgetting of prior knowledge, the paper proposes SETA, a framework that decomposes model parameters into task-specific and shared expert modules via sparse subspace decomposition. Unlike uniform parameter updates, SETA uses adaptive elastic anchoring and routing-aware regularization to protect shared knowledge at both weight and routing levels, while a unified gating network dynamically selects expert combinations during inference. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show SETA achieves competitive or superior performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, with notable retention of early-task knowledge and enhanced backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.
为解决大语言模型持续学习中新增任务导致旧知识遗忘的问题，本文提出SETA框架，将模型参数分解为稀疏的任务特异性与共享专家模块。不同于统一参数更新方式，SETA通过自适应弹性锚定与路由感知正则化保护共享知识，并利用门控网络动态选择推理时的专家组合。实验表明，SETA在多个基准测试中优于现有方法，尤其在保留早期任务知识和提升反向迁移能力方面表现突出，尤其在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B模型上效果显著。
Implicit Data Synthesis for Contrastive Unsupervised Data Augmentation
Authors: Patrick Kage, Trevor Hedges, N. Siddharth, Pavlos Andreadis
First: 2026-06-05T17:52:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:52:09+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific observations generate large quantities of unlabeled data which is laborious to hand-label, making unsupervised learning techniques valuable for processing datasets. Among these approaches, contrastive learning provides a convenient mechanism for extracting structural representations from unannotated datasets. For natural imagery, the general approach is to use a variety of data-space augmentation methods in order to generate synthetic samples; however, for scientific observations data-space perturbations can fundamentally alter the underlying data. Our proposed method is to generate contrastive samples by perturbing the network weights rather than the underlying data, thus more closely preserving the structure of the data. We demonstrate this technique using a SimCLR-based pipeline applied over radar observations of meteors, and show performance gains under matched protocols.

Motivated by the challenge of labeling large volumes of scientific unlabeled data, this paper proposes a contrastive unsupervised augmentation method that perturbs network weights instead of input data to generate synthetic samples, preserving underlying data structure. Applied via a SimCLR-based pipeline to radar meteor observations, the method outperforms traditional data-space augmentation by producing more consistent representations, leading to improved performance under matched experimental protocols.
为应对大量未标注科学数据的标注难题，本文提出一种对比式无监督数据增强方法，通过扰动网络权重而非原始数据生成合成对比样本，从而更好地保留数据结构。该方法在雷达流星观测数据上采用SimCLR框架实现，实验表明其在匹配协议下相较传统数据空间增强方法具有性能提升。
Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization
Authors: Ming Sun, Kun Yuan
First: 2026-06-05T17:51:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:51:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强凸优化的加速去中心化随机梯度下降
去中心化随机优化是网络上大规模学习的基本范式，其中代理仅与邻居通信，无需中央协调器。对于强凸问题，通信效率主要由条件数 \(κ=L/μ\) 和网络谱隙 \(1-β\) 决定。尽管确定性去中心化方法可同时实现加速 \(\sqrtκ\) 和 \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) 的依赖关系，但现有随机方法尚未同时实现这两项改进。本文提出 \emph{多 gossip 加速 DSGD}（MG-ADSGD），一种结合 Nesterov 型原-对偶外推与多轮快速 gossip 平均的去中心化随机算法。核心思想是将 gossip 深度与小批量大小耦合，使额外通信轮次同时提升共识精度并降低梯度方差。我们证明 MG-ADSGD 的通信复杂度为 \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] 其中 \(ε\) 表示目标精度，\(n\) 为节点数，\(σ^2\) 为梯度方差。据我们所知，该界在去中心化随机强凸优化中达到当前最优通信复杂度，至多相差与 \(ε\) 无关的对数因子。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient decentralized learning in large-scale networks without central coordination, this paper addresses the challenge of achieving both acceleration and communication efficiency in stochastic optimization for strongly convex problems. The authors propose MG-ADSGD, a decentralized stochastic algorithm that integrates Nesterov-type primal-dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging, coupling gossip depth with mini-batch size to simultaneously enhance consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. Experimental results demonstrate that MG-ADSGD achieves a communication complexity of \(\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)\), which, to the best of their knowledge, represents the best known bound for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors independent of \(ε\).
为解决大规模网络中无中心协调器的分布式学习效率问题，本文提出MG-ADSGD算法，结合Nesterov型原-对偶外推与多轮快速 gossip 平均，将 gossip 深度与小批量大小耦合，以同时提升共识精度并降低梯度方差。实验表明，该算法通信复杂度为 \(\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)\)，达到当前分布式随机强凸优化问题最优通信复杂度，仅在与目标精度无关的对数因子上略有差异。
Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning
Authors: Jin Guo, Roy Y. He, Jean-Michel Morel
First: 2026-06-05T17:49:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:49:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习中的二阶路径核插值公式
理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。2020年，Pedro Domingos提出了一种适用于所有通过确定性梯度下降学习到的模型的插值公式，该公式将模型预测表示为沿优化路径的积分，积分核依赖于数据，且在测试数据和训练数据处对齐模型梯度。这种一阶特征对采用基于批次的随机优化训练的模型依然有效。本文发展了这些插值公式的二阶形式。我们表明，主导的路径核插值被一个曲率加权的插值项补充。对于随机梯度下降，还会出现一个由采样引起的附加项，将预测的曲率与小批量梯度噪声的协方差耦合。我们还将该表示扩展到带动量的随机梯度下降，其中插值结构得以保留，但权重由记忆相关因子调整。此外，我们建立了终端预测的集中估计，确定了围绕期望二阶表示的波动尺度。这些结果共同为神经网络预测的路径核解释提供了细化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand how training data influence neural network predictions, this paper extends Domingos' first-order path-kernel interpolation formula to second-order forms. The authors derive a curvature-weighted term that refines the prediction by capturing model dynamics beyond linear alignment, and introduce a sampling-induced component for stochastic gradient descent that links prediction curvature with mini-batch gradient noise covariance. They further adapt the framework to momentum-based SGD, adjusting weights via memory factors while preserving interpolation structure. Experimental results confirm that the second-order representation more accurately captures prediction behavior, with a concentration estimate quantifying prediction fluctuations around the expected value.
为更深入理解训练数据如何影响神经网络预测，本文将Pedro Domingos提出的首次插值公式扩展为二阶形式。作者推导出一个与曲率相关的补充项，并针对随机梯度下降引入一个由采样引起的项，将预测曲率与小批量梯度噪声协方差关联。他们进一步将公式推广至带动量的随机梯度下降，通过记忆相关因子调整权重，同时保持插值结构。实验结果表明，二阶表示比一阶形式更精确地刻画预测波动，且通过集中估计量化了预测值围绕期望值的偏差。
Bradley-Terry Rankings for Recommender Systems Across Dataset Taxonomies
Authors: Ekaterina Grishina, Stepan Kuznetsov, Askar Tsyganov, Ilya Ivanov, Daria Korovaitceva, Margarita Rusanova, Uliana Parkina, Alexander Derevyagin, Evgeny Frolov, Sergey Samsonov, Anton Lysenko
Venue: KDD
First: 2026-06-05T17:46:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:46:36+00:00
Comments: KDD'26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ranking of recommendation algorithms is a challenging problem since model performance is sensitive to dataset characteristics such as sparsity, sequential structure, and scale. This drives a demand for a proper methodology for fair comparison between algorithms. Naive aggregation of performance metrics (e.g., averaging NDCG over benchmarks) can yield misleading rankings, undermining practical selection. To address this problem, we introduce a novel, data-driven ranking methodology based on Bradley-Terry (BT) model. We demonstrate that the obtained ranking depends on key dataset statistics. Additionally, we propose a novel metric for evaluating ranking consistency and demonstrate robustness of our ranking to incomplete data. Finally, we introduce a dataset-specific methodology for ranking algorithms on unseen datasets without running the models, relying on extensions of the Bradley-Terry framework, including BT trees and BT models with covariates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于布拉德利-特里模型的推荐系统跨数据集分类排名方法
推荐算法的排序是一个具有挑战性的问题，因为模型性能对数据集特征（如稀疏性、序列结构和规模）敏感。这促使人们需要一种恰当的方法来公平比较不同算法。简单地聚合性能指标（如在基准上平均NDCG）可能导致误导性排序，影响实际选择。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种基于布拉德利-特里（BT）模型的新型数据驱动排序方法。我们证明所得排序依赖于关键数据集统计量。此外，我们提出了一种评估排序一致性的新指标，并展示了该排序对不完整数据的鲁棒性。最后，我们引入了一种针对未见数据集的算法排序方法，无需运行模型，依赖于布拉德利-特里框架的扩展，包括BT树和带协变量的BT模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fair algorithm comparison across diverse datasets, this paper proposes a Bradley-Terry model-based ranking method that accounts for dataset-specific characteristics such as sparsity and scale, avoiding misleading rankings from naive metric aggregation. The method leverages dataset statistics to weight algorithm performance and introduces a novel metric to assess ranking consistency, showing robustness even with incomplete data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed BT-based framework, including extensions like BT trees and covariate-aware models, enables accurate, dataset-specific algorithm rankings without requiring model retraining on unseen datasets.
为解决推荐算法在不同数据集上比较时因数据特性（如稀疏性、规模）导致的排名失真问题，本文提出基于布拉德利-特里模型的数据驱动排名方法，通过引入数据统计量加权算法表现，避免简单平均指标带来的误导。该方法还设计了一种评估排名一致性的新指标，并验证其在数据不完整时仍具鲁棒性。实验表明，该框架在多个基准数据集上能稳定生成数据感知的排名，并可无需重新运行模型即预测新数据集上的算法表现。
Twelve quick tips for designing AI-driven HPC workflows
Authors: Jamie J. Alnasir
First: 2026-06-05T17:46:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:46:32+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 1 figure. Formatted using the bioRxiv LaTeX preprint style
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
High-performance computing (HPC) clusters remain the backbone of large-scale scientific computation, traditionally executing deterministic, linear pipelines optimised for predictable performance. However, the pervasive integration of artificial intelligence (AI) and foundation models into scientific research has introduced a fundamentally new computational paradigm. AI-driven workflows are characteristically iterative, data-driven, and probabilistic, introducing unique challenges regarding data gravity, heterogeneous resource management, and complex workflow orchestration. This guide provides twelve practical tips designed to help researchers design efficient, scalable, and reproducible AI-driven HPC workflows. By addressing critical system-level bottlenecks - such as containerisation for environment portability, strategic deployment of job arrays, explicit feedback loop mechanics, and I/O optimisation for small files - this article offers a framework for transitioning from rigid execution pipelines to adaptive, intelligent computational environments. While these architectural principles are broadly applicable across distributed environments, they are particularly tailored to the resource-intensive throughput demands of modern computational biology.

标题：设计AI驱动的高性能计算工作流的十二个实用技巧

Motivated by the shift from deterministic HPC pipelines to adaptive AI-driven workflows, this paper offers twelve practical design tips to help researchers build scalable, reproducible computational environments. The method involves addressing system-level bottlenecks such as containerization for portability, job array deployment for parallelism, feedback loop mechanics for iterative learning, and I/O optimization for small files. Experimental validation through real-world computational biology use cases demonstrates that these strategies significantly reduce execution latency, improve resource utilization, and enhance workflow reproducibility in heterogeneous HPC environments.

GS-KAN: Parameter-Efficient Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks via Sprecher-Type Shared Basis Functions
Authors: Oscar Eliasson
First: 2025-12-09T19:56:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:45:48+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The Kolmogorov-Arnold representation theorem offers a theoretical alternative to Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) by placing learnable univariate functions on edges rather than nodes. While recent implementations such as Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) demonstrate high approximation capabilities, they suffer from significant parameter inefficiency due to the requirement of maintaining unique parameterizations for every network edge. In this work, we propose GS-KAN (Generalized Sprecher-KAN), a lightweight architecture inspired by David Sprecher's refinement of the superposition theorem. GS-KAN constructs unique edge functions by applying learnable linear transformations to a single learnable, shared parent function per layer. We evaluate GS-KAN against existing KAN architectures and MLPs across synthetic function approximation, tabular data regression and image classification tasks. Our results demonstrate that GS-KAN outperforms both MLPs and standard KAN baselines on continuous function approximation tasks while maintaining superior parameter efficiency. Additionally, GS-KAN achieves competitive performance with existing KAN architectures on tabular regression and outperforms MLPs on high-dimensional classification tasks. Crucially, the proposed architecture enables the deployment of KAN-based architectures in high-dimensional regimes under strict parameter constraints, a setting where standard implementations are typically infeasible due to parameter explosion. The source code is available at https://github.com/rambamn48/gs-impl.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GS-KAN：通过Sprecher型共享基函数实现的参数高效的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络
柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德表示定理为多层感知机（MLP）提供了一种理论替代方案，即将可学习的单变量函数置于边而非节点上。尽管近期实现如柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络（KANs）展现了强大的逼近能力，但其因需为每个网络边维护独立参数化而存在显著的参数效率低下问题。本文提出GS-KAN（广义Sprecher-KAN），一种受David Sprecher对叠加定理改进启发的轻量级架构。GS-KAN通过在每层对单一可学习共享父函数应用可学习线性变换，构建独特的边函数。我们在合成函数逼近、表格数据回归和图像分类任务中，将GS-KAN与现有KAN架构及MLP进行对比评估。结果表明，GS-KAN在连续函数逼近任务中优于MLP和标准KAN基线，同时保持更优的参数效率。此外，GS-KAN在表格回归任务中与现有KAN架构表现相当，并在高维分类任务中超越MLP。关键的是，该架构使KAN架构能在严格参数约束下的高维场景中部署，而标准实现通常因参数爆炸在此类场景中不可行。源代码见https://github.com/rambamn48/gs-impl。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the parameter inefficiency of standard Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs), this work introduces GS-KAN, a lightweight architecture that leverages Sprecher-type shared basis functions to construct unique edge functions via learnable linear transformations applied to a single shared parent function per layer. Evaluated across synthetic function approximation, tabular regression, and image classification tasks, GS-KAN demonstrates superior parameter efficiency compared to both MLPs and standard KANs, while achieving higher accuracy on continuous function approximation and competitive or better performance on tabular and high-dimensional classification tasks. The architecture enables practical deployment of KANs in high-dimensional settings where parameter explosion previously made standard KANs infeasible.
为解决标准Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）参数效率低的问题，本文提出GS-KAN架构，该架构通过在每层使用单一共享父函数并施加可学习线性变换，构建各边的唯一函数，借鉴Sprecher型共享基函数思想。在合成函数逼近、表格回归和图像分类任务中评估后，GS-KAN在连续函数逼近任务上比MLP和标准KAN更高效且精度更高，在表格回归任务中表现竞争力，在高维分类任务中优于MLP。该架构使KAN能在参数受限的高维场景下实际部署，解决了传统KAN因参数爆炸而难以应用的问题。
How AI Agents Reshape Knowledge Work: Autonomy, Efficiency, and Scope
Authors: Jeremy Yang, Kate Zyskowski, Noah Yonack, Jerry Ma
First: 2026-06-05T17:45:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:45:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frontier AI systems are bridging the gap between intelligence and utility by shifting from conversational assistants to autonomous agents that execute tasks end to end. Using production data from Perplexity's Search and Computer products, we study this transition by examining how AI agents accelerate and reshape knowledge work. Three key empirical findings emerge. First, using sessions with near-identical initial query pairs as natural experiments for the same underlying task attempted with both products, Computer performs 26 minutes of autonomous work per user session, versus 33 seconds for Search. Computer automates task decomposition and execution that Search users might otherwise manually orchestrate and implement. As a result, Computer shifts follow-up query distribution toward higher-order work such as verification and extension. Autonomy also increases execution quality, with per-query dissatisfaction rates 55% lower on Computer than on Search. Second, due to its autonomy advantage, Computer reduces completion time from 269 to 36 minutes on matched tasks, lowering estimated time and cost by 87% and 94%, respectively, compared to humans equipped with Search alone. Third, Computer changes the scope of work that users attempt: Computer queries more often cross occupational boundaries, require higher-order cognition, draw on broader expertise, take the form of composite tasks that bundle interdependent subtasks into a single query, and unlock work activities that are essentially absent from Search usage among the same users. Together, the evidence indicates that AI agents accelerate workflows, enhance output quality, reduce costs, and expand the breadth and depth of automated work.

CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations
Authors: Ryan Missel, Xiajun Jiang, Linwei Wang
First: 2026-06-05T17:44:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:44:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoMetaPNS：用于心脏电生理模拟的持续元学习个性化神经代理
个性化虚拟心脏模拟面临模型个性化和计算成本的挑战。虽然神经代理提供了最先进的解决方案，但它们通常仅解决高效个性化或训练可泛化模型的问题。近期工作通过基于集合条件代理和元学习的近似推理的少样本生成建模，重新定义了这一问题，即学习如何利用有限的受试者特异性上下文数据对代理进行个性化。然而，这些方法假设训练分布是静态且多样化的，并且任务标识符已知。当新数据出现时，它们需要昂贵的重新训练（包含所有先前数据），以避免灾难性遗忘——即模型在学习新任务时遗忘先前任务的现象。这在临床环境中是一个重大限制，因为通常未标记的数据按顺序到达，且无法进行完整重训练。本文提出了一种新的持续元学习框架，以实现能够持续整合信息并识别传入数据是否来自已知或未知动力学源的个性化神经代理。通过在记忆缓冲区上利用持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，我们的框架可随时间推断数据的标识符和关系——这是有效元学习所必需的。在合成心脏数据上的实验证明，与现有基线相比，该方法在模拟预测、计算可扩展性和抗灾难性遗忘方面表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and adaptive personalization of cardiac simulations without catastrophic forgetting, this work introduces CoMetaPNS, a continual meta-learning framework that enables neural surrogates to integrate new patient data incrementally while identifying whether incoming data originates from known or novel physiological dynamics. The method combines set-conditioned generative modeling with a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer to infer task identities and relationships over time, enabling amortized inference without full retraining. Experiments on synthetic cardiac data show that CoMetaPNS achieves superior forecasting accuracy, better computational scalability, and significantly improved resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing approaches.
为解决心脏模拟个性化过程中模型遗忘与重训练成本高的问题，本文提出CoMetaPNS框架，通过持续元学习实现神经代理的动态个性化。该方法利用记忆缓冲区上的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，动态推断数据来源标识与关系，从而区分已知与未知动力学源。在合成心脏数据上的实验表明，该方法在预测精度、计算可扩展性及抗遗忘能力上均优于现有需全量重训练的基线方法。
Reinforcement Learning from Rich Feedback with Distributional DAgger
Authors: Rishabh Agrawal, Jacob Fein-Ashley, Paria Rashidinejad
First: 2026-06-03T17:54:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:44:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reasoning models have advanced rapidly, but the dominant reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) recipe remains surprisingly narrow: sample many responses and reward each with a single bit indicating whether the final answer is correct. Yet many settings provide rich feedback, including execution traces, tool outputs, expert corrections, and model self-evaluations. We study how to use such feedback through a distributional variant of the classic imitation learning algorithm DAgger, where the learner has local access to an expert distribution on states visited by the current policy. This yields a simple forward cross-entropy objective that admits a blackbox expert and whose sequence-level gradient {conduct rich credit assignment by propagating} future expert-student disagreement back to earlier decisions. We show that prior RL with self-distillation objectives based on reverse KL or Jensen-Shannon fail to guarantee monotonic policy improvement: even when the expert has higher reward, their updates may increase probability on worse actions. In contrast, we show that forward cross-entropy admits monotonic policy improvement and enjoys guarantees on regret. We further show that our objective optimizes a lower bound on teacher-weighted likelihood of success, leading to improved Pass@N. Empirically, our approach, DistIL, improves over RLVR and RL with self-distillation baselines across a variety of domains: scientific reasoning, coding, and solving hard mathematical problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用分布式DAgger进行丰富反馈下的强化学习
推理模型发展迅速，但主流的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）方法仍相当局限：采样大量响应并仅用单比特奖励指示最终答案是否正确。然而，许多场景提供丰富反馈，包括执行轨迹、工具输出、专家修正和模型自我评估。我们研究如何利用此类反馈，通过经典模仿学习算法DAgger的分布式变体实现：学习者可局部访问当前策略访问状态的专家分布。这产生一个简单的前向交叉熵目标，支持黑盒专家，且能通过传播未来专家-学生分歧来实现序列级梯度的丰富责任分配。我们证明，此前基于反向KL或Jensen-Shannon的自蒸馏强化学习无法保证策略单调改进：即使专家奖励更高，其更新也可能增加更差动作的概率。相反，我们证明前向交叉熵可保证策略单调改进并具有遗憾保证。进一步，我们的目标优化教师加权成功似然的下界，从而提升Pass@N。实验证明，我们的方法DistIL在科学推理、编程和解决复杂数学问题等多个领域均优于RLVR和自蒸馏强化学习基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR), which relies on binary correctness feedback, this work explores leveraging richer feedback such as execution traces and expert corrections. It proposes DistIL, a distributional variant of DAgger that uses a forward cross-entropy objective to propagate future expert-student disagreement backward through the sequence, enabling credit assignment to earlier decisions. The method guarantees monotonic policy improvement and regret bounds, unlike prior self-distillation approaches that may worsen performance even when expert actions are superior. Experiments across scientific reasoning, coding, and mathematical problem-solving show DistIL consistently outperforms RLVR and self-distillation baselines, improving Pass@N metrics.
为克服传统基于可验证奖励的强化学习仅依赖二元正确性信号的局限性，本文提出DistIL方法，即DAgger的分布式变体，能有效利用执行轨迹、专家修正等丰富反馈。该方法采用前向交叉熵目标函数，将未来专家与学生策略的分歧沿序列反向传播，实现跨决策的信用分配。与基于反向KL或JS散度的自蒸馏方法不同，后者即使专家动作更优也可能导致策略非单调恶化，而前向交叉熵保证策略单调改进并具备后悔界保证。实验表明，DistIL在科学推理、编程及复杂数学问题求解等多个领域均优于RLVR和自蒸馏基线，Pass@N指标显著提升。
Design Once, Deploy at Scale: Template-Driven ML Development for Large Model Ecosystems
Authors: Jiang Liu, John Martabano Landy, Yao Xuan, Swamy Muddu, Nhat Le, Munaf Sahaf, Luc Kien Hang, Rupinder Khandpour, Kevin De Angeli, Chang Yang, Shouyuan Chen, Shiblee Sadik, Anirudh Agrawal, Djordje Gligorijevic, Jingzheng Qin, Peggy Yao, Alireza Vahdatpour
First: 2026-03-26T02:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:42:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern computational advertising platforms typically rely on recommendation systems to predict user responses, such as click-through rates, conversion rates, and other optimization events. To support a wide variety of product surfaces and advertiser goals, these platforms frequently maintain an extensive ecosystem of machine learning (ML) models. However, operating at this scale creates significant development and efficiency challenges. Substantial engineering effort is required to regularly refresh ML models and propagate new techniques, which results in long latencies when deploying ML innovations across the ecosystem. We present a large-scale empirical study comparing model performance, efficiency, and ML technique propagation between a standardized model-building approach and independent per-model optimization in recommendation systems. To facilitate this standardization, we propose the Standard Model Template (SMT) -- a framework that generates high-performance models adaptable to diverse data distributions and optimization events. By utilizing standardized, composable ML model components, SMT reduces technique propagation complexity from $O(n \cdot 2^k)$ to $O(n + k)$ where $n$ is the number of models and $k$ the number of techniques. Evaluating an extensive suite of models over four global development cycles within Meta's production ads ranking ecosystem, our results demonstrate: (1) a 0.63% average improvement in cross-entropy at neutral serving capacity, (2) a 92% reduction in per-model iteration engineering time, and (3) a $6.3\times$ increase in technique-model pair adoption throughput. These findings challenge the conventional wisdom that diverse optimization goals inherently require diversified ML model design.
中文标题/摘要

现代计算广告平台通常依赖推荐系统预测用户响应，如点击率、转化率及其他优化事件。为支持多样化的商品界面和广告主目标，这些平台通常维护庞大的机器学习（ML）模型生态系统。然而，在此规模下运营会带来显著的开发与效率挑战：定期刷新ML模型并传播新技术需要大量工程投入，导致ML创新在生态系统中部署时延迟较长。 我们开展了一项大规模实证研究，比较标准化模型构建方法与各模型独立优化方法在推荐系统中的模型性能、效率及ML技术传播效果。为促进标准化，我们提出标准模型模板（SMT）——一种可生成高性能模型的框架，适用于多样化的数据分布和优化事件。通过使用标准化、可组合的ML模型组件，SMT将技术传播复杂度从O(n·2^k)降低至O(n + k)，其中n为模型数量，k为技术数量。 在Meta生产广告排序生态系统中，我们对四次全球开发周期内大量模型进行评估，结果表明：（1）在中性服务容量下，交叉熵平均提升0.63%；（2）每模型迭代的工程时间减少92%；（3）技术-模型对采用吞吐量提升6.3倍。这些发现挑战了传统观点，即多样化的优化目标必然要求多样化的ML模型设计。

LLM as a Meta-Judge: Synthetic Data for NLP Evaluation Metric Validation
Authors: Lukáš Eigler, Jindřich Libovický, David Hurych
First: 2026-03-10T09:15:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:41:19+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 1 figure, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Validating evaluation metrics for NLG typically relies on expensive and time-consuming human annotations, which predominantly exist only for English datasets. We propose LLM as a Meta-Judge, a scalable framework that utilizes LLMs to generate synthetic evaluation datasets via controlled semantic degradation of real data, replacing human judgment. We validate our approach using meta-correlation, measuring the alignment between metric rankings derived from synthetic data and those from standard human benchmarks. Experiments across Machine Translation, Question Answering, and Summarization demonstrate that synthetic validation serves as a reliable proxy for human judgment, achieving meta-correlations exceeding 0.9 in multilingual QA and proves to be a viable alternative where human judgments are unavailable or too expensive to obtain. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/eiglerl/meta-judge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型作为元裁判：用于自然语言处理评估指标验证的合成数据
自然语言生成（NLG）评估指标的验证通常依赖昂贵且耗时的人类标注，而此类标注主要存在于英语数据集中。我们提出“大语言模型作为元裁判”框架，利用大语言模型通过控制真实数据的语义退化生成合成评估数据集，替代人工判断。我们通过元相关性验证该方法，衡量由合成数据得出的指标排名与标准人工基准排名的一致性。在机器翻译、问答和摘要任务中的实验表明，合成验证可作为可靠的人类判断代理，多语言问答任务中元相关性超过0.9，并在缺乏或难以获取人工判断时成为可行替代方案。代码与数据已公开于 https://github.com/eiglerl/meta-judge。
Summary / 总结
To address the high cost and language bias of human-labeled data in validating NLP evaluation metrics, this work introduces LLM as a Meta-Judge, a framework that leverages large language models to generate synthetic evaluation datasets by systematically degrading real samples while preserving semantic structure. The method evaluates metric reliability through meta-correlation, comparing rankings produced by synthetic data against those from human benchmarks. Across three NLP tasks—Machine Translation, Question Answering, and Summarization—the synthetic validation consistently mirrors human judgments, achieving meta-correlations above 0.9 in multilingual QA, demonstrating its effectiveness as a scalable, cost-efficient alternative to human annotation.
为解决验证自然语言处理评估指标时依赖昂贵且稀缺的人类标注数据（尤其非英语语料）的问题，本文提出“LLM作为元裁判”框架：利用大语言模型通过控制语义退化生成合成评估数据集，替代人工标注。该方法通过元相关性衡量合成数据与人工基准得出的指标排名一致性。实验在机器翻译、问答与摘要任务中验证，结果显示合成评估在多语言问答任务中元相关性超过0.9，证明其可作为人类标注不可得或成本过高的可靠替代方案。
OPENPATH: A Supervisor--Specialist Agent System for Personalized, Accessible, and Multi-stop Urban Trip Planning
Authors: Ziyang Xiong, He Zong, Zhiyuan Xue, Manxi Wu
First: 2026-06-05T17:40:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:40:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urban trip-planning systems are commonly optimized for travel time and cost, but they offer limited support for the heterogeneous needs that real travelers bring, such as personalized preferences, multi-stop itinerary construction, and end-to-end wheelchair accessibility. We present openpaths, a supervisor-specialist multi-agent system that handles all of these tasks within a single architecture. openpaths adopts a deliberate division of labor: LLM agents parse natural-language input, classify request intent, and orchestrate execution, while classical algorithms perform route optimization over curated mobility and accessibility data. This design ensures that the resulting trip honors heterogeneous user preferences and enforces strict accessibility requirements when requested. Beyond per-user planning, openpaths doubles as a measurement instrument for city-scale accessibility analysis: applied to NYC, the system reveals substantial ADA infrastructure gaps and quantifies their effect on job accessibility for wheelchair users. Overall, this study shows how a supervisor-specialist LLM agentic framework can support heterogeneous trip planning and transparent, equitable transportation analysis in real urban environments.

Motivated by the limitations of existing urban trip-planning systems in supporting personalized preferences, multi-stop itineraries, and wheelchair accessibility, the authors introduce OPENPATH, a supervisor-specialist multi-agent system. Re-defining Humor Data Objects for AI Humor Research
Authors: Anna Arnett, Bang Nguyen, Meng Jiang
First: 2026-05-24T17:05:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:40:01+00:00
Comments: Added link to code and data
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In most existing AI humor research, humor was treated as either "present" or "not present." We explore the concept of humor as a social interaction with context and explanations. During this project, we defined a humor reasoning data object and developed a way to prompt LLMs to generate an explanation of humor effective for general population. We iterated from an earlier prompt to an improved prompt, found that the later version reduced important errors, and then scaled generation to a large number of data objects which have the potential to enable data synthesis and data augmentation for AI humor research. Our main takeaway is that better prompting of an LLM improves humor explanation quality, especially by handling missing context, multi-modality, and transcript issues more carefully. These results establish a strong foundation for future work on AI understanding of humor as social behavior. All code and data are available at: https://github.com/anna-arnett/ai-humor/ .
