Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach
Authors: Lei Huang
First: 2026-06-05T17:38:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:38:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从级联数据中恢复网络：一种基于去偏雅可比矩阵的机器学习方法
许多重要结果以动态级联形式展开，包括产品采用、疾病传播、财务困境和信息扩散。核心挑战在于恢复这些级联背后的隐藏影响网络。现有方法通常假设特定的扩散模型，当该假设不成立时，其性能会显著下降。我们提出CascadeNet，这是一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，用于网络恢复，无需指定扩散机制。核心思想是，底层影响结构可通过一步转移函数的雅可比矩阵表征。CascadeNet首先构建转移函数的灵活估计器，再通过里斯代表元应用 Neyman 正交去偏，使去偏雅可比矩阵达到 $\sqrt{n}$ 一致性和渐近正态性，从而支持对网络结构的正式推断。我们在模拟实验和现实世界实证应用中验证了 CascadeNet。在模拟中，当数据生成过程已知时，CascadeNet 在九种常见数据生成过程中均取得最高的网络恢复精度。在对西班牙52个省份COVID-19传播的实证应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实省际流动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络则与真实情况无显著一致性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to recover hidden influence networks behind dynamic cascades such as disease spread or product adoption without assuming specific diffusion models, the authors propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning method that constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function and applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer to achieve $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal estimation of the influence network. In simulations across nine data-generating processes, CascadeNet outperformed baseline methods in network recovery accuracy. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain’s 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovered networks significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, while baseline methods showed no such alignment.
为了解决在不假设特定扩散模型的情况下从动态级联数据中恢复隐藏影响网络的问题，作者提出了CascadeNet方法，该方法通过构建一步转移函数的灵活估计器，并结合Neyman正交去偏技术（基于Riesz表示）来实现影响网络的$\sqrt{n}$一致且渐近正态估计。在九种数据生成过程的模拟实验中，CascadeNet在恢复网络准确性上优于基线方法。在西班牙52个省份的COVID-19传播实证研究中，CascadeNet恢复的网络与真实省际流动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络则无显著一致性。
MIST: Mutual Information Estimation Via Supervised Training
Authors: German Gritsai, Megan Richards, Maxime Méloux, Kyunghyun Cho, Maxime Peyrard
First: 2025-11-24T09:55:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:37:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose a fully data-driven approach to designing mutual information (MI) estimators. Since any MI estimator is a function of the observed sample from two random variables, we parameterize this function with a neural network (MIST) and train it end-to-end to predict MI values. Training is performed on a large meta-dataset of 625,000 synthetic joint distributions with known ground-truth MI. To handle variable sample sizes and dimensions, we employ a two-dimensional attention scheme ensuring permutation invariance across input samples. To quantify uncertainty, we optimize a quantile regression loss, enabling the estimator to approximate the sampling distribution of MI rather than return a single point estimate. This research program departs from prior work by taking a fully empirical route, trading universal theoretical guarantees for flexibility and efficiency. Empirically, the learned estimators largely outperform classical baselines across sample sizes and dimensions, including on joint distributions unseen during training. The resulting quantile-based intervals are well-calibrated and more reliable than bootstrap-based confidence intervals, while inference is orders of magnitude faster than existing neural baselines. Beyond immediate empirical gains, this framework yields trainable, fully differentiable estimators that can be embedded into larger learning pipelines. Moreover, exploiting MI's invariance to invertible transformations, meta-datasets can be adapted to arbitrary data modalities via normalizing flows, enabling flexible training for diverse target meta-distributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIST：通过监督训练估计互信息
我们提出了一种完全数据驱动的方法来设计互信息（MI）估计器。由于任何MI估计器都是两个随机变量观测样本的函数，我们用神经网络参数化该函数（MIST），并端到端训练其预测MI值。训练基于包含62.5万种已知真实MI的合成联合分布元数据集。为处理可变样本量和维度，我们采用二维注意力机制，确保输入样本的排列不变性。为量化不确定性，我们优化分位数回归损失，使估计器能逼近MI的抽样分布，而非仅返回单点估计。本研究与以往工作不同，采取完全经验路线，以灵活性和效率换取通用理论保证。实证表明，所学估计器在样本量和维度上普遍优于经典基线，包括在训练中未见过的联合分布上。所得基于分位数的置信区间校准良好，比基于自助法的置信区间更可靠，且推理速度比现有神经基线快几个数量级。除即时实证收益外，该框架还生成可训练、完全可微的估计器，可嵌入更大规模学习流程。此外，利用MI对可逆变换的不变性，可通过归一化流将元数据集适配至任意数据模态，从而支持针对多样化目标元分布的灵活训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible, data-driven mutual information estimators without theoretical constraints, the authors propose MIST, a neural network that learns to estimate mutual information directly from samples. Trained end-to-end on a large synthetic meta-dataset with known ground-truth MI, the model uses a two-dimensional attention mechanism to handle variable sample sizes and dimensions while ensuring permutation invariance. It employs quantile regression loss to output calibrated uncertainty intervals rather than point estimates. Experimental results show that MIST significantly outperforms classical methods across diverse sample sizes and dimensions, including on unseen distributions, and provides faster inference than existing neural baselines while producing more reliable confidence intervals than bootstrap methods. The framework also supports integration into larger learning systems and can be adapted to new data modalities via normalizing flows.
为解决传统互信息估计器缺乏灵活性和效率的问题，作者提出MIST方法，通过神经网络直接从数据中学习互信息估计。该模型在包含62.5万组已知真实互信息的合成联合分布元数据集上端到端训练，采用二维注意力机制处理不同样本量和维度，确保输入样本的排列不变性。模型使用分位数回归损失输出置信区间而非点估计，从而实现校准良好的不确定性量化。实验表明，MIST在不同样本量和维度下均显著优于传统基线方法，甚至在训练未见分布上表现优异，其置信区间比Bootstrap方法更可靠，推理速度也快数个数量级。该框架支持嵌入到更大规模学习系统中，并可通过归一化流适配不同数据模态，实现对任意目标元分布的灵活训练。
Generalization of Diffusion Models Arises with a Balanced Representation Space
Authors: Zekai Zhang, Xiao Li, Xiang Li, Lianghe Shi, Meng Wu, Molei Tao, Qing Qu
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-12-24T05:40:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:37:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICLR 2026. 40 pages, 19 figures. The first two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models excel at generating high-quality, diverse samples, yet they risk memorizing training data when overfit to the training objective. We analyze the distinctions between memorization and generalization in diffusion models through the lens of representation learning. By investigating a two-layer ReLU denoising autoencoder (DAE), we prove that (i) memorization corresponds to the model storing raw training samples in the learned weights for encoding and decoding, yielding localized spiky representations, whereas (ii) generalization arises when the model captures local data statistics, producing balanced representations. Furthermore, we validate these theoretical findings on real-world unconditional and text-to-image diffusion models, demonstrating that the same representation structures emerge in deep generative models with significant practical implications. Building on these insights, we propose a representation-based method for detecting memorization and a training-free editing technique that allows precise control via representation steering. Together, our results highlight that learning good representations is central to novel and meaningful generative modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型的泛化能力源于平衡的表示空间
扩散模型擅长生成高质量、多样化的样本，但若过度拟合训练目标，可能记忆训练数据。我们通过表示学习的视角分析扩散模型中记忆与泛化的区别。通过研究两层ReLU去噪自编码器（DAE），我们证明：(i) 记忆对应模型在编码和解码的学得权重中存储原始训练样本，产生局部尖锐的表示；(ii) 泛化则源于模型捕捉局部数据统计，生成平衡的表示。此外，我们在现实世界中的无条件及文本到图像扩散模型上验证了这些理论发现，表明深度生成模型中同样出现此类表示结构，具有重要实际意义。基于这些见解，我们提出一种基于表示的记忆检测方法，以及一种无需训练的编辑技术，可通过表示引导实现精确控制。我们的结果共同表明，学习良好的表示是实现新颖且有意义生成建模的核心。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of memorization in diffusion models despite their generative strength, this work investigates the relationship between memorization and generalization through representation learning. Using a two-layer ReLU denoising autoencoder, it theoretically demonstrates that memorization manifests as localized, spiky representations storing raw training data, while generalization emerges from balanced representations capturing local data statistics. Experimental validation on real-world diffusion models confirms these patterns, and the study further proposes a representation-based memorization detector and a training-free editing method via representation steering, underscoring that balanced representations are essential for meaningful generative modeling.
鉴于扩散模型虽能生成高质量多样样本，但易在训练目标过拟合时记忆训练数据，本文从表征学习角度分析记忆与泛化差异。通过两层ReLU去噪自编码器理论证明，记忆表现为编码解码权重中存储原始训练样本，导致局部尖锐表征；而泛化则源于模型捕捉局部数据统计，形成平衡表征。在真实无条件及文生图扩散模型中验证了该理论，同时提出基于表征的记忆检测方法与无需训练的编辑技术（通过表征引导），表明学习良好平衡表征是实现有意义生成建模的核心。
Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling
Authors: Chris R. Jung, Markus Dörr, Natalie Jüngling, Jennifer Niessner, Adam T. Müller, Nicolaj C. Stache
First: 2026-06-05T17:35:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:35:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2nd International Symposium AI and Fluid Mechanics 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于代理流建模的漂移模型
尽管计算流体力学（CFD）可为优化室内环境提供高保真流场，但其计算成本限制了快速探索。为解决此问题，生成式代理优于确定性网络在分布建模方面，但迭代采样速度慢。为实现高质量单次生成，我们适配了新颖的生成漂移框架至流体力学领域。我们引入一种条件架构，在学习到的VAE潜在空间中执行漂移，并使用标签感知掩码使生成样本与边界条件对齐。我们的标签条件模型在准确性和流场一致性上与迭代扩散相当，但运行速度快两个数量级。此外，我们提出了一种空间条件变体，为推广至未见几何形状开辟了有前景的路径。最终，条件漂移成为基于扩散方法的高效替代方案，解锁了推理速度至关重要的实时CFD代理。
Summary / 总结
To address the high computational cost of CFD for real-time indoor environment optimization, this work introduces a conditional drifting framework adapted for fluid mechanics, enabling single-pass generation of high-fidelity flow fields. The method leverages a learned VAE latent space and label-aware masking to align generated samples with boundary conditions, avoiding iterative sampling. Experimental results show the model achieves accuracy and flow consistency comparable to iterative diffusion models while running two orders of magnitude faster, and a spatial-conditioning variant demonstrates potential for generalizing to unseen geometries, making it a viable real-time surrogate for CFD.
为解决CFD计算成本高、难以实时优化室内流场的问题，本文提出一种面向流体力学的条件漂移框架，支持单次生成高质量流场。该方法在学习到的VAE潜在空间中进行漂移，并通过标签感知掩码对齐生成样本与边界条件，避免迭代采样。实验表明，该模型在精度与流场一致性上可媲美迭代扩散模型，但速度提升两个数量级，且空间条件变体展现出对未见几何的良好泛化潜力，为实时CFD代理模型提供了高效替代方案。
Supervision versus Demonstration-Based In-Context Learning for Multiword Expression Classification
Authors: Sercan Karakaş, Yusuf Şimşek
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-06-05T17:34:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:34:07+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL SRW 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Turkish idiomatic light verb constructions (LVCs) are challenging for multiword expression processing because they often share the same surface form as fully literal verb-object combinations while functioning as a single, partially idiomatic predicate. We frame Turkish LVC detection as a binary classification task (literal meaning vs. idiomatic meaning) and evaluate on a manually created controlled set (N=147) with matched negatives: out-of-domain random sentences and in-domain literal controls (NLVC), alongside LVC positives. We compare a supervised Turkish encoder baseline (BERTurk with a classifier head) to three instruction-tuned LLMs from different families under zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot prompting, and analyze how demonstrations shift error profiles. In zero-shot, LLMs perform well on negatives but show very low LVC recall. One-shot prompting sharply improves LVC detection but can induce strong, model-specific biases, leading models to overpredict or underpredict LVCs. A richer few-shot prompt improves calibration and yields robust overall performance for GPT-OSS-20B and Qwen 2.5-14B. Overall, the results highlight substantial prompt sensitivity in Turkish metalinguistic classification: the supervised baseline remains competitive, while prompted LLMs can match or exceed it on LVCs with carefully constructed demonstrations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：监督学习与基于演示的上下文学习在多词表达分类中的对比
土耳其语轻动词结构（LVCs）对多词表达处理具有挑战性，因为它们常与完全字面意义的动宾结构共享表层形式，但作为单一的、部分习语化的谓语功能。我们将土耳其LVC检测建模为二分类任务（字面意义 vs. 习语意义），并在人工构建的控制数据集（N=147）上评估，该数据集包含匹配的负样本：跨领域随机句子和领域内字面控制样本（NLVC），以及LVC正样本。我们比较了监督土耳其编码器基线（BERTurk加分类器头）与三种不同家族的指令调优大语言模型在零样本、一样本和少样本提示下的表现，并分析演示如何改变错误分布。零样本下，大语言模型在负样本上表现良好，但LVC召回率极低；一样本提示显著提升LVC检测，但可能引发模型特异性偏差，导致模型对LVC过度或不足预测；更丰富的少样本提示提升校准效果，使GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B整体表现稳健。总体而言，结果凸显了土耳其元语言分类中提示的显著敏感性：监督基线仍具竞争力，而经精心构造演示提示的大语言模型在LVC任务上可匹配甚至超越其表现。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of classifying Turkish idiomatic light verb constructions (LVCs), which often resemble literal verb-object pairs but carry idiomatic meaning. The authors frame this as a binary classification task and evaluate models on a manually curated dataset of 147 LVCs with matched negative controls. They compare a supervised BERTurk baseline with three instruction-tuned LLMs under zero-shot, one-shot, and few-shot prompting. Results show that while zero-shot LLMs perform well on negatives but poorly on LVC recall, one-shot prompting improves detection but introduces model-specific biases. Few-shot prompting, especially with GPT-OSS-20B and Qwen 2.5-14B, yields calibrated and robust performance, often matching or surpassing the supervised baseline, demonstrating that prompt design critically affects classification accuracy in Turkish metalinguistic tasks.
该研究旨在解决区分土耳其语习语轻动词结构（LVCs）与字面动宾结构的难题，将其建模为二分类任务。作者对比了监督式BERTurk基线模型与三种指令微调的大语言模型，在零样本、单样本和少样本提示下进行评估，使用147个手工标注的LVC样本及匹配负样本。结果表明，LLM在零样本设置下对非LVC样本表现良好，但对LVC的召回率极低；单样本提示虽显著提升检测能力，却引入模型特异性偏差，导致过预测或欠预测；少样本提示（尤其GPT-OSS-20B和Qwen 2.5-14B）能改善校准并实现稳健性能，有时可媲美甚至超越监督基线，凸显LLM在语言学分类任务中对提示设计的高度敏感性。
MACD: Model-Aware Contrastive Decoding via Counterfactual Data
Authors: Qixin Xiao, Kun Zhou
First: 2026-02-02T07:21:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:33:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video language models (Video-LLMs) are prone to hallucinations, generating plausible but ungrounded content when visual evidence is weak, ambiguous, or biased. Existing methods, such as contrastive decoding (CD), rely on random perturbations to construct contrastive data for hallucination mitigation, but often fail to target the visual cues that drive hallucination or align with model weaknesses. We propose Model-Aware Counterfactual Data based Contrastive Decoding (MACD), an inference strategy that combines model-guided counterfactual construction with contrastive decoding. MACD uses the Video-LLM's own feedback to identify object regions most responsible for hallucination, generating targeted object-level counterfactual inputs rather than arbitrary frame or temporal modifications. These counterfactual inputs are integrated into CD to enforce evidence-grounded token selection during decoding. Experiments on EventHallusion, MVBench, Perception-test, and Video-MME show that MACD consistently reduces hallucination while maintaining or improving task accuracy across diverse Video-LLMs, including Qwen and InternVL, with especially strong gains in scenarios involving small, occluded, or co-occurring objects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MACD：基于反事实数据的模型感知对比解码
视频语言模型（Video-LLMs）易产生幻觉，在视觉证据薄弱、模糊或有偏时生成看似合理但无依据的内容。现有方法如对比解码（CD）依赖随机扰动构建对比数据以缓解幻觉，但往往未能针对驱动幻觉的视觉线索或与模型弱点对齐。我们提出模型感知反事实数据对比解码（MACD），一种结合模型引导的反事实构造与对比解码的推理策略。MACD利用Video-LLM自身的反馈识别最易导致幻觉的物体区域，生成针对物体级别的反事实输入，而非任意帧或时间修改。这些反事实输入整合进CD中，以在解码过程中强制执行证据驱动的token选择。在EventHallusion、MVBench、Perception-test和Video-MME上的实验表明，MACD在多种Video-LLMs（包括Qwen和InternVL）上持续减少幻觉，同时保持或提升任务准确率，尤其在涉及小物体、遮挡物体或共现物体的场景中效果显著。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of video language models to hallucinate when visual evidence is weak or ambiguous, the paper introduces MACD, a model-aware contrastive decoding method that leverages the model's own feedback to identify key object regions responsible for hallucinations. Instead of random perturbations, MACD constructs targeted counterfactual inputs at the object level, which are then used to guide contrastive decoding and enforce grounding during token selection. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MACD consistently reduces hallucinations while preserving or enhancing task accuracy, particularly in challenging scenarios involving small, occluded, or co-occurring objects.
为解决视频语言模型在视觉证据不足或模糊时易产生幻觉的问题，本文提出MACD方法，通过模型自身反馈识别导致幻觉的关键物体区域，构建针对性的物体级反事实输入，并将其融入对比解码以强化解码过程中的证据约束。实验表明，MACD在多个基准测试中持续降低幻觉率，同时保持或提升任务准确率，尤其在小物体、遮挡或共现物体等困难场景下表现突出。
Physiologically Constrained Musculoskeletal Neural Network for Multi-DoF Joint Kinematics Estimation from Partially Observed sEMG
Authors: Wending Heng, Mingming Zhang, Glen Cooper, Zhenhong Li
First: 2026-06-05T17:30:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:30:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper investigates multi-degrees of freedom (DoF) joint kinematics estimation under partially observed surface electromyography (sEMG), where only a subset of task-relevant muscles can be measured due to anatomical inaccessibility or sensor constraints. A novel musculoskeletal neural network (MSK-NN) is proposed to estimate multi-DoF joint angles while simultaneously inferring activations for both measured and unmeasured muscles. MSK-NN consists of a CNN-based muscle activation estimator and an embedded MSK forward dynamics module, forming a fully differentiable architecture. Unlike existing hybrid neural frameworks that require additional biomechanical labels (e.g., muscle-tendon forces, joint torques), MSK-NN is trained without direct supervision of internal biomechanical variables. A composite physics-physiology loss is designed by incorporating a joint kinematics loss, a data-driven muscle synergy loss, and an anatomy-guided trend loss. The proposed method is evaluated on two-DoF wrist kinematics estimation across three rhythmic motions with unconstrained speed and amplitude, and one random motion. Compared with CNN, Bi-LSTM, CNN-LSTM, and PET baselines, MSK-NN achieves lower normalized root mean square error (NRMSE) and higher coefficient of determination (R2), especially for the random motion. More importantly, the optimized MSK parameters remain within physiological limits, and the estimated activation of an input-excluded muscle exhibits strong temporal agreement with its recorded sEMG envelope, demonstrating the capability of musculoskeletal (MSK)-NN to recover physiologically plausible activations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于生理约束的肌肉骨骼神经网络，用于从部分观测的表面肌电图估计多自由度关节运动学
本文研究在部分观测表面肌电图（sEMG）条件下多自由度（DoF）关节运动学估计问题，由于解剖结构不可达或传感器限制，仅能测量任务相关肌肉的子集。提出一种新型肌肉骨骼神经网络（MSK-NN），在估计多DoF关节角度的同时，推断测量与未测量肌肉的激活情况。MSK-NN由基于CNN的肌肉激活估计器和嵌入的MSK前向动力学模块组成，形成完全可微分架构。与现有需额外生物力学标签（如肌腱力、关节力矩）的混合神经框架不同，MSK-NN无需直接监督内部生物力学变量进行训练。设计一种复合物理-生理损失函数，结合关节运动学损失、数据驱动的肌肉协同损失和解剖学引导趋势损失。该方法在三种节奏性运动（速度与幅度无约束）及一种随机运动中评估了两DoF手腕运动学估计性能。相比CNN、Bi-LSTM、CNN-LSTM和PET基线方法，MSK-NN在归一化均方根误差（NRMSE）上更低、决定系数（R²）更高，尤其在随机运动中表现更优。更重要的是，优化后的MSK参数保持在生理合理范围内，且输入排除肌肉的估计激活与记录sEMG包络在时间上高度一致，证明了MSK-NN恢复生理合理激活的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of estimating multi-degree-of-freedom joint movements from limited surface electromyography (sEMG) signals, this study proposes a musculoskeletal neural network (MSK-NN) that jointly estimates joint angles and infers activations for both measured and unmeasured muscles. The architecture combines a CNN-based activation predictor with a differentiable musculoskeletal forward dynamics module, trained without direct supervision of biomechanical variables. A composite loss function integrates joint kinematics, muscle synergy, and anatomy-guided trend terms. Evaluated on wrist motion tasks with varying speeds and amplitudes, MSK-NN outperformed CNN, Bi-LSTM, CNN-LSTM, and PET baselines in accuracy (lower NRMSE, higher R²), particularly for random motions. Crucially, optimized parameters remained physiologically valid, and inferred activations for unmeasured muscles matched recorded sEMG envelopes temporally, validating the model’s ability to generate biologically plausible muscle activity patterns.
为了解决因解剖结构或传感器限制导致部分肌肉sEMG信号无法获取时，如何准确估计多自由度关节运动的问题，本文提出了一种新型的肌骨神经网络（MSK-NN），该网络可同时估计关节角度并推断测量与未测量肌肉的激活状态。该架构结合了基于CNN的肌肉激活预测器与嵌入式肌骨动力学模块，形成可端到端训练的完全可微分系统，无需直接监督内部生物力学变量。通过联合关节运动、数据驱动肌肉协同和解剖学引导趋势的复合损失函数，确保生理合理性。在包含多种节奏性与随机运动的腕部运动任务中，MSK-NN在NRMSE和R²指标上显著优于CNN、Bi-LSTM、CNN-LSTM和PET基线模型，尤其在随机运动中表现突出。更重要的是，未测量肌肉的推断激活与实际sEMG包络在时序上高度一致，验证了MSK-NN生成生理合理肌肉激活的能力。
Trace Reconstruction with Language Models
Authors: Franziska Weindel, Michael Girsch, Reinhard Heckel
First: 2025-07-17T09:08:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:29:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The general trace reconstruction problem seeks to recover an original sequence from its noisy copies independently corrupted by insertions, deletions, and substitutions. This problem arises in applications such as DNA data storage, a promising storage medium due to its high information density and longevity. However, errors introduced during DNA synthesis, storage, and sequencing require correction through algorithms and codes, with trace reconstruction often used as part of data retrieval. In this work, we propose TReconLM, a decoder-only transformer that solves trace reconstruction as a next-token prediction task. TReconLM outperforms state-of-the-art trace reconstruction algorithms, including prior deep-learning approaches, recovering a substantially higher fraction of sequences without error. We pretrain on synthetic data generated from a simple error model and fine-tune on real-world data to adapt to technology-specific error patterns. Code is available at https://github.com/MLI-lab/TReconLM.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用语言模型进行轨迹重建
轨迹重建问题旨在从独立遭受插入、删除和替换噪声干扰的副本中恢复原始序列。该问题在DNA数据存储中尤为关键，DNA因其高信息密度和长寿命而成为有前景的存储介质。然而，DNA合成、存储和测序过程中引入的错误需要通过算法和编码进行校正，轨迹重建常用于数据检索。本文提出TReconLM，一种仅解码器的Transformer模型，将轨迹重建视为下一个词预测任务。TReconLM在性能上超越现有最先进的轨迹重建算法，包括先前的深度学习方法，能更准确地恢复更高比例的序列。我们使用基于简单错误模型生成的合成数据进行预训练，并在真实数据上微调，以适应特定技术的错误模式。代码可在https://github.com/MLI-lab/TReconLM获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to recover original sequences from noisy DNA data in storage applications, this work introduces TReconLM, a decoder-only transformer that frames trace reconstruction as a next-token prediction task. The model is pretrained on synthetic data under a simple error model and fine-tuned on real-world DNA error patterns to improve performance. Experimental results show TReconLM significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art algorithms, including prior deep-learning methods, by recovering a substantially higher fraction of input sequences without error.
为了解决DNA数据存储中因插入、删除和替换导致的序列噪声问题，本文提出TReconLM模型，将序列恢复任务转化为下一个词预测问题，采用解码器-only的Transformer架构。模型先在合成数据上预训练，再在真实DNA错误数据上微调以适应特定技术噪声。实验表明，TReconLM显著优于现有最先进方法（包括先前的深度学习方法），能更准确地恢复原始序列，错误率更低。
Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Mohammadreza Sadeghi, Sareh Soleimani, Zihan Wang, Narges Armanfard
First: 2026-06-05T17:28:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:28:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECML PKDD 2026 (Research Track). arXiv admin note: substantial text overlap with arXiv:2405.19234
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过前向-后向知识蒸馏实现无监督持续聚类
无监督持续学习（UCL）旨在使神经网络在无标签且无法访问历史数据的情况下学习序列任务。该场景的主要挑战是灾难性遗忘，即模型在学习新任务时会遗忘先前学习的任务。由于缺乏标签来引导学习和记忆保留，这一挑战在UCL中更为严重。现有缓解策略（如知识蒸馏和重放缓冲区）常引发内存和隐私问题，且当前UCL方法大多忽视聚类特定目标。为填补这一空白，我们提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）及持续聚类前向-后向知识蒸馏（FBCC）。FBCC采用具备聚类投影器的持续教师网络与轻量级任务特定学生网络，通过双阶段前向-后向蒸馏过程，教师在学习新聚类的同时保留先前发现的聚类结构，且无需存储历史数据。FBCC是UCC领域的开创性方法，在序列任务中展现出更优的聚类性能。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，同时显著减少灾难性遗忘。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of catastrophic forgetting in unsupervised continual learning, where models forget prior tasks without labels or past data access, this work introduces Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and proposes Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students, using a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process to learn new clusters while preserving old ones without storing past data. Experimental results across four benchmarks show FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing forgetting.
为解决无监督持续学习中模型因缺乏标签和历史数据而遗忘先前任务的问题，本文提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）及前向-后向知识蒸馏方法（FBCC）。FBCC通过持续教师网络与轻量级任务学生网络，采用双阶段前向-后向蒸馏机制，在学习新聚类的同时保留旧聚类结构，无需存储历史数据。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，同时显著降低遗忘现象。
Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs
Authors: Takuto Takahashi, Itsuki Nakayama, Takahiro Mitani, Ryosuke Kikuchi, Yuya Sasaki, Makoto Onizuka
First: 2026-06-05T17:28:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:28:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用高阶类别标签连通性的图神经网络用于异质图
图神经网络（GNN）中的节点分类在图分析的各个领域得到了广泛应用。GNN在同质图中表现优异，其中具有相同类别标签的节点倾向于相互连接。然而，在异质图中，其性能仍受限，因为不同类别标签的节点更可能相互连接。特别是，当前基于图卷积网络的GNN无法捕捉到在现实世界异质图中频繁出现的高阶类别标签连通性。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种新颖的分类器——标签上下文分类器（LCC），专门设计用于在有向图中捕捉高阶类别标签连通性。LCC通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入，以此估计目标节点的类别标签。此外，我们的方法允许将LCC与任何GNN结合，并自适应学习其重要性。实验结果表明，集成LCC的GNN优于现有最先进方法，且标签上下文嵌入显著提升了异质有向图中的节点分类性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of existing GNNs on heterophilous graphs where nodes of different classes are densely connected, this work introduces the Label Context Classifier (LCC), a novel method that captures higher-order class label connectivity via four distinct types of walks to generate label context embeddings. LCC is designed for directed graphs and can be adaptively integrated with any GNN to enhance its classification capability. Experimental results show that integrating LCC with GNNs significantly improves node classification accuracy on heterophilous directed graphs, outperforming state-of-the-art methods.
为了解决现有图神经网络在异质图（节点不同类别间高度连接）中表现不佳的问题，本文提出了一种名为标签上下文分类器（LCC）的新方法，通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入，以捕捉高阶类别标签关联性。LCC适用于有向图，并可自适应地与任意GNN结合以提升性能。实验表明，将LCC与GNN结合后，在异质有向图上的节点分类准确率显著提升，优于当前最先进方法。
Certified Robustness to Data Poisoning in Gradient-Based Training
Authors: Philip Sosnin, Mark N. Müller, Maximilian Baader, Calvin Tsay, Matthew Wicker
First: 2024-06-09T06:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:27:51+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern machine learning pipelines leverage large amounts of public data, making it infeasible to guarantee data quality and leaving models open to poisoning and backdoor attacks. Provably bounding model behavior under such attacks remains an open problem. In this work, we address this challenge by developing the first framework providing provable guarantees on the behavior of models trained with potentially manipulated data without modifying the model or learning algorithm. In particular, our framework certifies robustness against untargeted and targeted poisoning, as well as backdoor attacks, for bounded and unbounded manipulations of the training inputs and labels. Our method leverages convex relaxations to over-approximate the set of all possible parameter updates for a given poisoning threat model, allowing us to bound the set of all reachable parameters for any gradient-based learning algorithm. Given this set of parameters, we provide bounds on worst-case behavior, including model performance and backdoor success rate. We demonstrate our approach on multiple real-world datasets from applications including energy consumption, medical imaging, and autonomous driving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于梯度训练的对抗数据投毒认证鲁棒性
现代机器学习流程依赖大量公开数据，难以保证数据质量，使模型易受投毒和后门攻击。在这些攻击下对模型行为进行可证明的界限仍属开放性问题。本文通过构建首个框架，无需修改模型或学习算法，即可对使用潜在被操纵数据训练的模型行为提供可证明的保障。具体而言，我们的框架可认证针对无目标和有目标投毒，以及后门攻击的鲁棒性，适用于训练输入和标签的有界与无界操纵。该方法利用凸松弛技术，对给定投毒威胁模型下所有可能的参数更新进行上界近似，从而界定任何梯度学习算法所能达到的所有参数集合。基于此参数集合，我们提供最坏情况下的行为界限，包括模型性能和后门成功率。我们在多个现实世界数据集上验证了该方法，涵盖能源消耗、医学影像和自动驾驶等应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of gradient-based machine learning models to data poisoning and backdoor attacks in real-world settings where data quality cannot be guaranteed, this work introduces the first framework offering provable robustness guarantees without altering the model or training algorithm. The method employs convex relaxations to over-approximate the set of possible parameter updates under various poisoning threat models, enabling bounding of all reachable parameters during gradient-based training. Experimental results across real-world datasets—covering energy consumption, medical imaging, and autonomous driving—show that the framework successfully certifies robustness against untargeted and targeted poisoning as well as backdoor attacks, providing tight bounds on worst-case model performance and backdoor success rates.
鉴于现代机器学习模型在使用公共数据时易受数据投毒和后门攻击，本文提出首个无需修改模型或学习算法即可提供可证明鲁棒性保障的框架。该方法通过凸松弛技术，对给定投毒威胁模型下的参数更新集合进行上界近似，从而界定所有可达模型参数。在能源、医学影像和自动驾驶等真实数据集上的实验表明，该框架可有效证明模型对无目标和有目标投毒以及后门攻击的鲁棒性，并量化最坏情况下的模型性能与攻击成功率。
Focus-then-Context: Subject-Centric Progressive Visual Token Reduction for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Yulin Zhao, Zheng Zhang
First: 2026-05-20T09:37:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:26:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) face a bottleneck of prohibitive computational costs arising from massive visual token sequences during inference. Existing vision token reduction methods alleviate this burden, but they unintentionally preserve the isolated visual subject strictly aligned with the user's query, which fails to substantially explore salient subjects and their contextual relationships. In this paper, we propose SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction paradigm that emulates the \textit{Focus-then-Context} mechanism of the human visual perception system. Specifically, we first construct a focus identification module to explicitly model the interplay between visual saliency and semantic relevance. Herein, it can excavate the comprehensive visual subject spectrum to ensure a high-fidelity representation of visual input. Subsequently, a context-aware structural scanning module is developed to aggregate contextual cues from neighboring regions. As such, it can effectively restore global relational dependencies to uphold the structural integrity of the preserved subjects. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our paradigm consistently outperforms SOTA methods, achieving up to 2.53 times speedup with only 22.2% of visual tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA with a negligible 0.6% accuracy drop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：聚焦后上下文：面向视觉-语言模型的以主体为中心的渐进式视觉令牌缩减
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在推理过程中因大量视觉令牌序列而面临计算成本过高的瓶颈。现有视觉令牌缩减方法虽能缓解此负担，但无意中严格保留与用户查询对齐的孤立视觉主体，未能充分探索显著主体及其上下文关系。本文提出SPpruner，一种以主体为中心的渐进式缩减范式，模拟人类视觉感知系统的“聚焦后上下文”机制。具体而言，我们首先构建聚焦识别模块，显式建模视觉显著性与语义相关性的交互，从而挖掘全面的视觉主体谱系，确保视觉输入的高保真表示；随后开发上下文感知结构扫描模块，从邻近区域聚合上下文线索，有效恢复全局关系依赖，维持保留主体的结构完整性。大量实验表明，我们的范式持续优于现有最先进方法，在Qwen2.5-VL中仅保留22.2%视觉令牌即可实现最高2.53倍加速，且在LLaVA上实现67%的FLOPs降低，准确率仅下降0.6%。
Summary / 总结
To address the high computational cost of processing long visual token sequences in Vision-Language Models, this paper introduces SPpruner, a subject-centric progressive reduction method inspired by human visual perception’s 'Focus-then-Context' mechanism. It first identifies the primary visual subject using a focus module that combines saliency and semantic relevance to capture a comprehensive subject spectrum. Then, a context-aware scanning module aggregates neighboring contextual cues to preserve global structural relationships. Experiments show SPpruner achieves up to 2.53x speedup with only 22.2% of tokens retained in Qwen2.5-VL and a 67% FLOPs reduction on LLaVA, with less than 0.6% accuracy loss.
为缓解视觉语言模型推理时因长视觉token序列导致的高计算开销，作者提出SPpruner方法，该方法以人类视觉感知的“聚焦-上下文”机制为灵感，采用主体中心的渐进式视觉token削减策略。首先通过聚焦模块结合视觉显著性与语义相关性，挖掘全面的视觉主体表示；随后通过上下文感知扫描模块聚合邻域上下文线索，恢复全局关系依赖并保持主体结构完整性。实验表明，该方法在Qwen2.5-VL中仅保留22.2%视觉token即可实现2.53倍加速，在LLaVA上降低67% FLOPs，准确率仅下降0.6%。
Whisper Hallucination Detection and Mitigation via Hidden Representation Steering and Sparse AutoEncoders
Authors: Georgii Aparin, Vadim Popov, Tasnima Sadekova, Assel Yermekova
First: 2026-06-05T17:26:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:26:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whisper, a widely adopted ASR model, is known to suffer from hallucinations - coherent transcriptions generated for non-speech audio entirely disconnected from the input. We investigate whether hallucinations can be detected and mitigated through Whisper's internal representations. We extract audio encoder activations and evaluate two representation spaces: raw Whisper activations and Sparse AutoEncoder (SAE) latents. We show that both spaces encode linearly separable hallucination-related information, with discriminative power concentrated in a sparse feature subset and increasing toward deeper encoder layers. We propose two steering strategies: activation-space steering and SAE latent-space steering. SAE-based steering reduces hallucination rate from 72.63% to 14.11% for Whisper small and from 86.88% to 27.33% for Whisper large-v3 on the full non-speech test set, with small WER degradation on speech data, approaching the performance of fine-tuning-based methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过隐藏表示引导与稀疏自编码器检测与缓解Whisper幻觉
Whisper是一种广泛采用的语音识别模型，其已知会生成与输入音频无关的连贯转录结果（幻觉）。我们研究是否可通过Whisper内部表示检测并缓解幻觉。我们提取音频编码器激活值，并评估两种表示空间：原始Whisper激活值与稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜变量。结果显示，两种空间均能线性分离与幻觉相关的特征，判别力集中在稀疏特征子集，并随编码器层数加深而增强。我们提出两种引导策略：激活空间引导与SAE潜空间引导。基于SAE的引导方法将Whisper small在全非语音测试集上的幻觉率从72.63%降至14.11%，Whisper large-v3从86.88%降至27.33%，语音数据的WER轻微下降，性能接近基于微调的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the prevalence of hallucinations in Whisper, an ASR model that generates coherent but incorrect transcriptions for non-speech audio, this study explores detection and mitigation via internal representation steering. It evaluates two spaces—raw activations and Sparse AutoEncoder latents—and finds both contain linearly separable hallucination signals, concentrated in sparse features and becoming more discriminative in deeper layers. Two steering methods are proposed: steering in activation space and steering in SAE latent space. Experimental results show SAE-based steering reduces hallucination rates from 72.63% to 14.11% for Whisper small and from 86.88% to 27.33% for Whisper large-v3 on non-speech audio, with minimal WER degradation on speech data, nearly matching fine-tuning performance.
鉴于Whisper模型在语音识别中普遍存在幻觉现象，本研究探索通过操控其内部表征来检测与缓解此类错误。作者提取编码器激活值，分析原始激活空间与稀疏自编码器（SAE）潜空间，发现两者均包含可线性分离的幻觉相关特征，且在深层编码器中更集中于稀疏特征。提出两种引导策略：激活空间引导与SAE潜空间引导。实验表明，基于SAE的引导可显著降低幻觉率——Whisper small降至14.11%，Whisper large-v3降至27.33%（非语音测试集），对语音WER影响极小，性能接近微调方法。
SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents
Authors: Matheus Kunzler Maldaner, Adam Fourney, Amanda Swearngin, Hussein Mozannar, Gagan Bansal, Maya Murad, Rafah Hosn, Saleema Amershi
First: 2026-06-03T18:32:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:19:56+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents are increasingly asked to carry out work that spans minutes, hours, or longer. Yet the default model of agent behavior is continuous action: issuing tool calls, refreshing pages, searching for alternatives, or otherwise trying to force progress. This is the wrong approach for many long-running tasks, which are better served by a strategy of sustained attention. Instead, agents should monitor an environment, notice when an external event makes progress possible, then respond promptly without wasting resources while waiting. To measure progress on this class of tasks, we introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark for time-evolving monitoring tasks. SentinelBench contains 100 tasks across 10 synthetic web environments, including email, calendars, finance, professional networking, and entertainment. Each environment exposes a live web interface and replays a scripted sequence of events, requiring agents to navigate and reason about web pages whose state shifts underfoot. SentinelBench measures task completion, reaction time, and resource use, exposing the tradeoff between responsiveness and cost. We report results across three models and two browser-agent harnesses, establishing performance baselines for future comparison and demonstrating how agent design choices can dramatically impact key metrics. Together, these results show that SentinelBench distinguishes meaningful differences in agent behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SentinelBench：长期监控代理的基准测试
AI代理越来越多地被要求执行跨越数分钟、数小时甚至更长时间的任务。然而，代理行为的默认模型是连续行动：调用工具、刷新页面、搜索替代方案，或试图强制推动进展。但对于许多长期任务而言，这种做法并不合适，更有效的策略是持续关注。代理应监控环境，当外部事件使进展成为可能时及时响应，同时在等待期间不浪费资源。为衡量此类任务的进展，我们引入了SentinelBench，这是一个开源的时间演化监控任务基准。 SentinelBench包含10个合成网络环境中的100个任务，涵盖电子邮件、日历、金融、专业社交和娱乐等领域。每个环境提供实时网页界面，并重放预设事件序列，要求代理在网页状态不断变化的情况下进行导航和推理。SentinelBench衡量任务完成率、反应时间和资源使用情况，揭示响应速度与成本之间的权衡。我们在三个模型和两个浏览器代理框架上报告了结果，为未来比较建立了性能基准，并展示了代理设计选择如何显著影响关键指标。这些结果共同表明，SentinelBench能够区分代理行为中的实质性差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI agents performing long-running tasks that require sustained attention rather than continuous action, the authors introduce SentinelBench, an open-source benchmark featuring 100 monitoring tasks across 10 synthetic web environments. The benchmark requires agents to observe live web interfaces and respond only when external events enable progress, measuring completion, reaction time, and resource usage. Experiments with three models and two browser-agent frameworks reveal significant performance differences based on agent design, demonstrating that responsiveness and efficiency are trade-offs that can be optimized through architecture choices.
为评估需要持续关注而非连续行动的长时任务中AI代理的表现，作者提出了SentinelBench，一个包含100个监控任务的开源基准，覆盖10个合成网页环境。代理需观察动态网页界面，识别相关外部事件并及时响应，避免资源浪费。该基准衡量任务完成率、反应时间和资源消耗，揭示响应速度与效率之间的权衡。在三个模型和两种浏览器代理框架上的实验显示，代理设计显著影响关键指标，确立了性能基线，并证明监控策略优于传统连续行动方法。
LLM-Guided Search for Deletion-Correcting Codes
Authors: Franziska Weindel, Reinhard Heckel
First: 2025-04-01T10:11:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:17:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finding deletion-correcting codes of maximum size has been an open problem for over 70 years, even for a single deletion. We adapt FunSearch, a large language model (LLM)-guided evolutionary search, to discover functions that construct deletion-correcting codes at short code lengths. For a single deletion, our search finds a function that we prove constructs the conjectured-optimal Varshamov-Tenengolts code. For multiple deletions and quaternary edit codes, the discovered functions improve on prior explicit, search-based, and neural constructions but remain empirical heuristics without new theoretical insights. We study design choices for LLM-guided evolutionary search and find that, for our problem, compute is better allocated to sampling more functions than to longer reasoning traces per function, and that co-evolving natural language descriptions with code hurts search quality. We propose deduplicating logically identical functions during evolution, which we find critical for search diversity. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLM-guided evolutionary search for information theory and code design and represent the first application of such methods for constructing error-correcting codes. However, in our current formulation, evaluating a function scales exponentially with code length, limiting the approach to short codes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型引导的删除纠错码搜索
寻找最大规模的删除纠错码已是一个悬而未决的问题超过70年，即使仅针对单次删除。我们适应了FunSearch方法——一种基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的进化搜索——以发现能够构造短码长删除纠错码的函数。对于单次删除，我们的搜索发现了一个函数，我们证明其构造的是推测最优的Varshamov-Tenengolts码。对于多次删除和四进制编辑码，所发现的函数优于先前的显式、搜索式和神经网络构造，但仍是缺乏新理论见解的经验启发式方法。我们研究了LLM引导进化搜索的设计选择，发现对于本问题，计算资源更应分配于采样更多函数而非每个函数的更长推理轨迹，且与代码共同进化自然语言描述会损害搜索质量。我们提出在进化过程中对逻辑上相同的函数去重，发现这对其搜索多样性至关重要。我们的结果展示了LLM引导进化搜索在信息论和码设计中的潜力，并代表了此类方法首次应用于构造纠错码。然而，在当前形式下，评估函数的复杂度随码长呈指数增长，限制了该方法仅适用于短码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the longstanding challenge of finding maximum-size deletion-correcting codes, this work adapts FunSearch—an LLM-guided evolutionary search—to discover functions that construct such codes at short lengths. For single deletions, the method finds a function proven to generate the conjectured-optimal Varshamov-Tenengolts code; for multiple deletions and quaternary edit codes, it outperforms prior explicit, search-based, and neural constructions, though without theoretical guarantees. The study evaluates design choices and finds that sampling more functions is more effective than deep reasoning per function, co-evolving natural language descriptions harms performance, and deduplicating logically identical functions is essential for maintaining search diversity. The approach demonstrates LLM-guided search’s potential in coding theory but is limited to short codes due to exponential evaluation cost.
为解决长期悬而未决的删除纠错码最大规模问题，该研究将基于大语言模型的FunSearch进化搜索方法应用于短码长下构造删除纠错码函数。针对单删除情形，方法发现并证明了可生成最优猜想Varshamov-Tenengolts码的函数；对多删除及四进制编辑码，其性能优于现有显式、搜索和神经构造方法，但缺乏理论支撑。研究发现，计算资源应优先用于采样更多函数而非单函数深度推理，且代码与自然语言描述共进化会降低搜索质量。进化过程中去重逻辑等价函数对维持多样性至关重要。该方法虽在信息论编码设计中展现潜力，但因评估成本随码长指数增长，目前仅适用于短码。
Planning-aligned Token Compression for Long-Context Autonomous Driving
Authors: Zhixuan Liang, Yuxiao Chen, Yurong You, Peter Karkus, Wenhao Ding, Boyi Li, Alexander Popov, Yan Wang, Maximilian Igl, Yiming Li, Danfei Xu, Nikolai Smolyanskiy, Boris Ivanovic, Ping Luo, Marco Pavone
First: 2026-06-05T17:16:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:16:21+00:00
Comments: 9 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monolithic vision-action models represent an emerging paradigm in autonomous driving. However, this architecture produces token sequences that quickly exceed real-time computational budgets when encoding extended temporal context for complex interactions. While approaches like linear transformers and external memory try to make the context lightweight, token compression is most compatible with the architecture as it requires no backbone modifications. Yet existing compression adopts rule-based heuristics like temporal decay, decoupled from planning, risking loss of decision-critical information. We propose COMPACT-VA, a planning-aligned working memory framework built on conditional VQ-VAE, compressing extended context into bounded representations. Compression is conditioned on both historical trajectory and a learned planning intent that the posterior encoder distills from future trajectories during training, while the prior encoder learns to predict it from compressed observations. The compressed memory, concatenated with the predicted latent, feeds the policy for end-to-end optimization, planning with retained decision-critical information. We evaluate on high-signal dynamic scenarios where historical context is most critical for behavior correctness (e.g., stop, yield, or proceed), and accordingly design behavioral metrics. Under comparable token budgets, we achieve $>$6% improvement (68.3%) on success rates with consistent gains across metrics. Ablations validate planning-aligned coupling effectiveness. Closed-loop evaluation confirms that COMPACT-VA maintained general driving performance with 3.3* speedup and 2.7* memory reduction over uncompressed processing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向长上下文自动驾驶的规划对齐令牌压缩
单体视觉-动作模型是自动驾驶领域新兴范式。然而，该架构在编码复杂交互的扩展时序上下文时，生成的令牌序列会迅速超出实时计算预算。虽然线性变换器和外部记忆等方法试图使上下文轻量化，但令牌压缩与架构最兼容，无需修改骨干网络。但现有压缩方法采用基于规则的启发式策略（如时间衰减），与规划脱钩，可能丢失决策关键信息。我们提出COMPACT-VA，一种基于条件VQ-VAE的规划对齐工作记忆框架，将扩展上下文压缩为有界表示。压缩过程同时依赖历史轨迹与从未来轨迹中训练后编码器提炼的规划意图，而先验编码器则从压缩观测中学习预测该意图。压缩后的记忆与预测的潜在表示拼接后输入策略，实现端到端优化，保留决策关键信息进行规划。我们在历史上下文对行为正确性至关重要的高信号动态场景（如停车、让行或通行）中评估，并相应设计行为指标。在相同令牌预算下，成功率提升>6%（68.3%），各项指标均保持稳定增长。消融实验验证了规划对齐耦合的有效性。闭环评估表明，COMPACT-VA在未压缩处理基础上实现3.3倍加速和2.7倍内存缩减，同时保持通用驾驶性能。

To address the computational burden of encoding long temporal context in monolithic vision-action models for autonomous driving, the authors propose COMPACT-VA, a planning-aligned token compression framework based on conditional VQ-VAE. Amortized Neural Optimization for Pre-Layout Signal Integrity Design Space Exploration using Differentiable Surrogates
Authors: Julian Withöft, Werner John, Emre Ecik, Ralf Brüning, Jürgen Götze
First: 2026-06-05T17:13:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:13:44+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 20 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-layout design space exploration (DSE) for high-speed signal integrity (SI) analysis is often limited by the computational cost of simulations and iterative optimization algorithms within modern electronic design automation (EDA) workflows. While machine learning surrogate models accelerate the simulation step, optimizing designs still requires utilizing iterative black-box search methods. This iterative nature scales poorly, making multi-corner sweeps computationally expensive. As a solution, this paper proposes amortized neural optimization (ANO) for pre-layout SI design. ANO entirely eliminates iterative black-box inference by utilizing fully differentiable neural network surrogate models. ANO extracts analytical gradients from the surrogate to train a global optimization policy. Instead of solving the optimization problem repeatedly at inference, the optimization process is learned offline and therefore amortized. Once the ANO policy is trained, it maps different channel contexts directly to near-optimal design parameters in a single deterministic forward pass. The efficiency and accuracy of the ANO framework are demonstrated based on three complex SI design scenarios, including DDR5 decision feedback equalization (DFE), 9-dimensional SerDes Tx/Rx co-equalization, and DDR3 DQS differential pair routing to optimize eye diagram metrics under intra-pair skew constraints. By trading roughly 10% in optimality compared to instance-specific black-box algorithms, it realizes speedups of three to four orders of magnitude. For a large-scale 320,000-instance multi-corner SerDes sweep optimization, ANO collapses what would have taken days of computation using iterative search algorithms into a single batched forward pass that completes in milliseconds. This transforms computationally expensive SI optimization into real-time and interactive pre-layout DSE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用可微代理模型的预布局信号完整性设计空间探索的均摊神经优化
高速信号完整性（SI）分析的预布局设计空间探索（DSE）常受限于现代电子设计自动化（EDA）工作流中仿真与迭代优化算法的计算成本。虽然机器学习代理模型加速了仿真步骤，但设计优化仍需依赖迭代黑盒搜索方法，其迭代特性导致多角扫描计算成本高昂。为此，本文提出预布局SI设计的均摊神经优化（ANO），通过使用完全可微的神经网络代理模型，彻底消除迭代黑盒推断。ANO从代理模型中提取分析梯度以训练全局优化策略。优化过程在推理时无需重复求解，而是离线学习并实现均摊。训练完成后，ANO策略可直接将不同通道上下文映射至近最优设计参数，单次确定性前向传播即可完成。基于三个复杂SI设计场景（包括DDR5决策反馈均衡（DFE）、9维SerDes收发共均衡、DDR3 DQS差分对布线以优化眼图指标并满足对内偏斜约束），验证了ANO框架的效率与准确性。相比实例特定黑盒算法，ANO牺牲约10%的最优性，实现三至四数量级加速。对于大规模32万实例多角SerDes优化，ANO将原本需数日计算的迭代搜索算法压缩为毫秒级单次批量前向传播，将计算密集型SI优化转变为实时交互式预布局DSE。

Motivated by the high computational cost of iterative optimization in pre-layout signal integrity design space exploration, this paper introduces amortized neural optimization (ANO) using differentiable neural surrogates to replace black-box search. ANO trains a global optimization policy offline using analytical gradients from the surrogate model, enabling direct mapping of channel contexts to near-optimal design parameters in a single forward pass. Experimental results across three complex SI scenarios—DDR5 DFE, 9D SerDes co-equalization, and DDR3 DQS routing—show ANO achieves 3–4 orders of magnitude speedup over iterative methods with only ~10% loss in optimality. 为解决预布局信号完整性设计空间探索中迭代优化计算成本高的问题，本文提出一种称为“ amortized neural optimization (ANO) ”的方法，利用可微分神经网络代理模型替代传统黑盒迭代搜索，通过分析梯度训练全局优化策略，实现一次前向传播直接输出近最优设计参数。在DDR5 DFE、9维SerDes共均衡及DDR3 DQS布线等三个复杂场景中，ANO相较实例特定黑盒算法仅损失约10%的最优性，但速度提升3至4个数量级。在32万实例的多角扫描优化中，ANO将原本需数天的计算压缩至毫秒级，使预布局设计空间探索实现实时交互。
Act As a Real Researcher: A Suite of Benchmarks Evaluating Frontier LLMs and Agentic Harnesses in Research Lifecycle
Authors: Jiayu Wang, Weijiang Lv, Bowen Fu, Jing Fu, Jiayi Song, Lingyu Zhang, Lanxuan Xue, Luodi Chen, Zepeng Xin, Kaiyu Li, Xiangyong Cao
First: 2026-06-05T17:13:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:13:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As foundation models advance and agent scaffolding becomes increasingly sophisticated, agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in complex, long-horizon coding tasks and even autonomous experiment execution. Despite their evolution from research assistants into autonomous research agents, these systems still exhibit significant limitations in field sensitivity, research ethics, and nuanced scientific judgment. Consequently, frontier agents remain unable to fully replace human researchers. To bridge this gap, we conceptualize the AARR (Act As a Real Researcher) benchmark series. Unlike existing benchmarks that primarily assess macro-level execution capabilities, AARR focuses on whether agents can emulate the professionalism, thoroughness, and nuanced reasoning that characterize human researchers in granular research scenarios. In this work, we propose AARRI-Bench (Act As a Real Research Intern), the first benchmark in this series. We conduct extensive experiments across frontier models and agentic systems, revealing that even the best-performing configuration (Mini-SWE-Agent with Claude Opus 4.7) achieves only 68.3\% success rate, frequently overlooking subtle yet critical details that are obvious to real human researchers. Our results indicate that developing researcher-like AI requires further exploration of research behavior, rather than merely complex scaffolding. Our data is released at https://github.com/AARR-bench/AARRI-bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扮演真实研究员：一套评估前沿大语言模型与代理框架在研究生命周期中表现的基准测试

Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting
Authors: Lorenzo Longarini, Alessandro Rongoni, Simone Silenzi, Emanuele Frontoni, Riccardo Rosati
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-06-05T17:05:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:05:19+00:00
Comments: To be published in the 2nd ICML Workshop on Foundation Models for Structured Data
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

To address the cold-start challenge in photovoltaic forecasting—where operators must predict output before site-specific data is available—the paper proposes a zero-shot pipeline that synthesizes historical production data from plant metadata and weather covariates, enabling physics-informed time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to generate forecasts without prior site observations. 为解决光伏系统冷启动阶段（无历史观测数据时）的预测难题，本文提出一种零样本方法，利用电站元数据与气象协变量合成历史发电序列，使物理信息引导的时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）能在推理时进行条件预测。在四个数据集的440个光伏站点上，五种TSFMs在三种反馈策略下与传统方法对比，结果表明协变量感知的TSFMs性能提升1.7–2倍，其中TabPFN-TS在真实反馈下MAE最低（0.514 kWh/kWp/天），Chronos-2在自预测反馈下表现最稳健，说明模型性能主要取决于时间序列上下文的合理性，而非合成历史的具体来源。
Modeling AdaGrad, RMSProp, and Adam with Integro-Differential Equations
Authors: Carlos Heredia
First: 2024-11-14T19:00:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:03:31+00:00
Comments: 60 pages, 15 figures; v3 - Section 4 corrected
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a continuous-time formulation for the AdaGrad, RMSProp, and Adam optimization algorithms by modeling them as first-order integro-differential equations. We perform numerical simulations of these equations, along with stability and convergence analyses, to demonstrate their validity as accurate approximations of the original algorithms. Our results indicate a strong agreement between the behavior of the continuous-time models and the discrete implementations, thus providing a new perspective on the theoretical understanding of adaptive optimization methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用积分微分方程建模AdaGrad、RMSProp和Adam
本文提出了一种连续时间形式，将AdaGrad、RMSProp和Adam优化算法建模为一阶积分微分方程。我们对这些方程进行了数值模拟，并进行了稳定性与收敛性分析，以证明其作为原始算法准确近似的有效性。结果表明，连续时间模型与离散实现的行为高度一致，从而为自适应优化方法的理论理解提供了新视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a continuous-time theoretical framework to understand adaptive optimization algorithms, this paper formulates AdaGrad, RMSProp, and Adam as first-order integro-differential equations. Through numerical simulations and stability analysis, the authors demonstrate that these continuous models closely approximate the discrete-time behavior of the original algorithms, validating their use as accurate theoretical proxies. The results confirm strong alignment between continuous dynamics and discrete implementations, offering a novel analytical lens for studying adaptive optimization.
为理解自适应优化算法，本文将AdaGrad、RMSProp和Adam建模为一阶积分微分方程。通过数值模拟与稳定性收敛分析，作者证明这些连续模型能准确逼近离散实现，行为高度一致，为研究自适应方法提供了新的理论视角。
CORE: Contrastive Reflection Enables Rapid Improvements in Reasoning
Authors: Linas Nasvytis, Simon Jerome Han, Ben Prystawski, Satchel Grant, Noah D. Goodman, Judith E. Fan
First: 2026-05-27T17:01:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:02:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models can use verifiable rewards to improve at a wide variety of reasoning tasks. However, both parametric (e.g. RLVR) and non-parametric (e.g. prompt optimization) approaches to doing so typically require hundreds of training samples and thousands of model rollouts, making them expensive in the best case and intractable in the worst. To address this challenge, we introduce Contrastive Reflection (CORE), a non-parametric learning algorithm that compares past reasoning traces to generate insights: short natural-language descriptions of reasoning strategies and constraints that capture differences between successful and unsuccessful problem attempts. Across four reasoning tasks, we demonstrate that CORE enables more rapid improvement than both parametric (GRPO) and non-parametric (GEPA, episodic RAG, and MemRL) methods, while using fewer rollouts. Under fixed rollout budgets with as few as five training samples, CORE achieves the strongest performance in most task-data regimes. Finally, we highlight how CORE is substantially more context-efficient than non-parametric baselines, requiring fewer prompt tokens while storing learned knowledge as compact, interpretable natural-language insights. Our results therefore suggest that distilling contrasts between successful and unsuccessful reasoning traces into abstract and useful insights can provide a more efficient and interpretable route to model self-improvement than weight updates, prompt optimization, or direct reuse of stored reasoning traces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CORE：对比反思可快速提升推理能力
语言模型可通过可验证奖励在多种推理任务中提升表现。然而，无论是参数化（如RLVR）还是非参数化（如提示优化）方法，通常都需要数百个训练样本和数千次模型运行，最理想情况下也十分昂贵，最坏情况下则不可行。为解决此问题，我们提出了对比反思（CORE），这是一种非参数化学习算法，通过比较过往推理轨迹生成洞察：即简短的自然语言描述，概括成功与失败尝试之间的推理策略与约束差异。在四个推理任务中，我们证明CORE比参数化（GRPO）和非参数化（GEPA、分段RAG、MemRL）方法实现更快提升，且使用更少的运行次数。在固定运行预算下，仅需五个训练样本，CORE在多数任务-数据场景中表现最佳。最后，我们指出CORE比非参数化基线方法显著更节省上下文，所需提示词更少，同时以紧凑、可解释的自然语言洞察形式存储所学知识。因此，我们的结果表明，将成功与失败推理轨迹之间的对比提炼为抽象且有用的洞察，比权重更新、提示优化或直接重用存储推理轨迹，能更高效且可解释地实现模型自我提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of training samples and model rollouts in existing reasoning improvement methods, the authors propose Contrastive Reflection (CORE), a non-parametric algorithm that compares successful and unsuccessful reasoning traces to extract natural-language insights about effective strategies and constraints. CORE uses these insights to guide model improvement without updating weights or optimizing prompts, achieving faster convergence than parametric (GRPO) and non-parametric (GEPA, episodic RAG, MemRL) baselines across four reasoning tasks. Under tight rollout budgets and as few as five training samples, CORE consistently outperforms alternatives and demonstrates superior context efficiency by requiring fewer prompt tokens and storing knowledge in compact, interpretable language.
为降低语言模型推理能力提升的成本，本文提出对比反思（CORE）算法，通过对比过往推理轨迹，提炼出描述成功与失败策略差异的自然语言洞察。该方法无需参数更新或大量推理采样，仅通过生成简洁、可解释的反思指导后续推理。在四个推理任务中，CORE在样本和采样预算极低的情况下（如仅5个训练样本）超越了参数化（GRPO）和非参数化（GEPA、episodic RAG、MemRL）基线方法，表现最优。同时，CORE在上下文效率上显著优于非参数基线，使用更少的提示词并以自然语言形式存储知识，表明通过对比成功与失败推理轨迹提炼抽象洞察，是比权重更新、提示优化或直接复用推理轨迹更高效、可解释的自我提升路径。
PaperFlow: Profiling, Recommending, and Adapting Across Daily Paper Streams
Authors: Fuqiang Wang, Song Tan, Zheng Guo, Jiaohao Fu, Xinglong Xu, Bihui Yu, Jie Dong, Zheng Sun, Siyuan Li, Jingxuan Wei, Cheng Tan
First: 2026-06-05T17:00:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T17:00:20+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 13 figures, 22 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific paper recommendation is typically evaluated as static ranking over a fixed candidate set, yet real scientific reading unfolds as a daily, longitudinal process in which interests shift and feedback accumulates. We introduce PaperFlow, a framework that organizes it into three coupled stages: Profiling, which constructs and maintains a structured, inspectable scholarly profile from heterogeneous cold-start evidence; Recommending, which ranks each date-specific paper stream through multi-signal aggregation under a fixed display budget; and Adapting, which updates user state from semantically distinct feedback signals and models interest drift across days. We further define a longitudinal user-day benchmark that fixes users, dates, candidate pools, visible inputs, and hidden simulated relevance labels under a shared temporal information boundary. The benchmark contains 24 simulated research users, 50 daily paper streams, 1,200 user-day episodes, 20,727 unique papers, and 497,448 episode-paper records. We additionally specify a blind human-evaluation protocol to validate alignment between automatic metrics and expert judgments. Experiments against five scientific recommendation baselines show that PaperFlow achieves the strongest oracle-based ranking, the highest behavioral alignment with simulated reading selections, and the best blind human-evaluation score.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PaperFlow：跨每日论文流的画像、推荐与适应
科学论文推荐通常被评估为在固定候选集上的静态排序，但真实的科学阅读是一个每日纵向过程，兴趣会变化且反馈会累积。我们提出了PaperFlow框架，将其划分为三个耦合阶段：画像阶段，从异构冷启动证据构建并维护结构化、可检查的学术画像；推荐阶段，在固定展示预算下，通过多信号聚合对每日论文流进行排序；适应阶段，从语义不同的反馈信号更新用户状态，并建模跨日兴趣漂移。我们进一步定义了一个纵向用户-日基准，固定用户、日期、候选池、可见输入和隐藏模拟相关性标签，在共享时间信息边界下进行评估。该基准包含24个模拟研究用户、50个每日论文流、1200个用户-日会话、20727篇独特论文和497448个会话-论文记录。我们还制定了盲人评估协议，以验证自动指标与专家判断的一致性。与五个科学推荐基线方法的实验表明，PaperFlow实现了最强的基于oracle的排序、最高的模拟阅读选择行为一致性，以及最佳的盲人评估得分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model scientific reading as a dynamic, longitudinal process rather than a static ranking task, PaperFlow introduces a three-stage framework: Profiling constructs a structured scholarly profile from sparse initial data; Recommending ranks daily paper streams under a fixed display budget using multi-signal aggregation; and Adapting updates user state and models interest drift using feedback. Evaluated on a novel longitudinal user-day benchmark with 24 simulated users, 50 daily streams, and 1.2M episode-paper records, PaperFlow outperforms five baselines by achieving the best oracle-based ranking, highest behavioral alignment with simulated reading choices, and top blind human-evaluation scores.
为应对科学阅读作为动态纵向过程而非静态排序任务的需求，PaperFlow提出三阶段框架：Profiling从稀疏初始数据构建结构化学术画像；Recommending在固定展示预算下，通过多信号聚合对每日论文流进行排序；Adapting利用语义不同的反馈信号更新用户状态并建模兴趣漂移。在包含24个模拟用户、50个每日流、120万篇论文记录的纵向基准上，PaperFlow优于五个基线方法，在Oracle排序、模拟阅读行为对齐度及盲人评估得分三方面均表现最优。
Unregistered Spectral Image Fusion: Unmixing, Adversarial Learning, and Recoverability
Authors: Jiahui Song, Sagar Shrestha, Xiao Fu
First: 2026-03-23T02:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:55:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses the fusion of a pair of spatially unregistered hyperspectral image (HSI) and multispectral image (MSI) covering roughly overlapping regions. HSIs offer high spectral but low spatial resolution, while MSIs provide the opposite. The goal is to integrate their complementary information to enhance both HSI spatial resolution and MSI spectral resolution. While hyperspectral-multispectral fusion (HMF) has been widely studied, the unregistered setting remains challenging. Many existing methods focus solely on MSI super-resolution, leaving HSI unchanged. Supervised deep learning approaches were proposed for HSI super-resolution, but rely on accurate training data, which is often unavailable. Moreover, theoretical analyses largely address the co-registered case, leaving unregistered HMF poorly understood. In this work, an unsupervised framework is proposed to simultaneously super-resolve both MSI and HSI. The method integrates coupled spectral unmixing for MSI super-resolution with latent-space adversarial learning for HSI super-resolution. Theoretical guarantees on the recoverability of the super-resolution MSI and HSI are established under reasonable generative models -- providing, to our best knowledge, the first such insights for unregistered HMF. The approach is validated on semi-real and real HSI-MSI pairs across diverse conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未配准光谱图像融合：解混、对抗学习与可恢复性
本文研究空间未配准的高光谱图像（HSI）与多光谱图像（MSI）的融合问题，二者覆盖大致重叠区域。HSI具有高光谱分辨率但低空间分辨率，MSI则相反。目标是整合二者互补信息，提升HSI的空间分辨率和MSI的光谱分辨率。尽管高光谱-多光谱融合（HMF）已广泛研究，但未配准情形仍具挑战性。许多现有方法仅关注MSI超分辨率，而忽略HSI。监督深度学习方法虽被用于HSI超分辨率，但依赖精确训练数据，常难以获取。此外，理论分析多针对配准情形，未配准HMF尚缺乏深入理解。本文提出一种无监督框架，同时实现MSI与HSI的超分辨率。该方法结合耦合光谱解混用于MSI超分辨率，与潜在空间对抗学习用于HSI超分辨率。在合理生成模型下，建立了超分辨率MSI与HSI的可恢复性理论保证——据我们所知，这是首次针对未配准HMF的此类理论见解。该方法在多种条件下，经半真实与真实HSI-MSI对验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance both spatial and spectral resolution in unregistered hyperspectral and multispectral image pairs, this work proposes an unsupervised framework that simultaneously super-resolves both modalities. It combines coupled spectral unmixing for MSI resolution enhancement with latent-space adversarial learning for HSI resolution recovery, avoiding reliance on precise training data. Theoretical guarantees for recoverability are established under plausible generative models, marking the first such analysis for unregistered HMF. Experimental results on semi-real and real datasets confirm the method’s effectiveness across diverse conditions, outperforming existing approaches that handle only one modality or assume registration.
为解决空间未对齐的高光谱与多光谱图像融合问题，本文提出一种无监督框架，同时提升两类图像的分辨率。该方法结合耦合光谱解混实现多光谱图像超分辨率，与潜在空间对抗学习实现高光谱图像超分辨率，解决了标注数据缺失与理论研究空白。实验在半真实与真实图像对上验证，结果表明该方法能有效恢复高分辨率图像，且在合理生成模型下具备理论可恢复性保障。
TEVI: Text-Conditioned Editing of Visual Representations via Sparse Autoencoders for Improved Vision-Language Alignment
Authors: Sweta Mahajan, Sukrut Rao, Jiahao Xie, Alexander Koller, Bernt Schiele
First: 2026-06-05T16:54:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:54:40+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 13 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models such as CLIP are highly useful for diverse tasks due to their shared image-text embedding space. Despite this, the image and text embeddings are often poorly aligned, affecting downstream performance. Recent work has shown that this can be attributed to an information imbalance: images contain more information than their captions describe. In this work, we propose TEVI, a framework that uses captions as a signal for what to retain from image embeddings. Specifically, we use sparse autoencoders to disentangle image embeddings and train a masking module to selectively reconstruct the embedding based on a given caption. In a controlled setup with synthetic captions, we show that TEVI is effective at preserving caption-described attributes while discarding others. By applying TEVI to CLIP models trained on natural images, we further achieve improved retrieval performance across coarse-grained short-caption (MS COCO, Flickr) and fine-grained long-caption (IIW, DOCCI) benchmarks, with stronger gains on richer captions, and improved robustness on the RoCOCO benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TEVI：通过稀疏自编码器实现文本条件视觉表示编辑以提升视觉-语言对齐
视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）因其共享的图像-文本嵌入空间，在多种任务中具有高度实用性。然而，图像与文本嵌入往往对齐不佳，影响下游性能。近期研究表明，这源于信息失衡：图像包含的信息多于其描述性标题。本文提出TEVI框架，利用标题作为信号，指导从图像嵌入中保留哪些信息。具体而言，我们使用稀疏自编码器解耦图像嵌入，并训练一个掩码模块，根据给定标题选择性地重建嵌入。在合成标题的受控实验中，我们证明TEVI能有效保留标题描述的属性并丢弃其他属性。将TEVI应用于在自然图像上训练的CLIP模型后，我们在粗粒度短标题（MS COCO、Flickr）和细粒度长标题（IIW、DOCCI）基准上均提升了检索性能，对丰富标题的提升更显著，并在RoCOCO基准上增强了鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor alignment between image and text embeddings in vision-language models like CLIP, which hinders downstream performance, this work proposes TEVI, a framework that leverages captions to guide the retention of relevant image features. TEVI employs sparse autoencoders to disentangle image embeddings and trains a masking module to selectively reconstruct them based on given captions. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that TEVI effectively preserves caption-described attributes while discarding irrelevant ones, leading to improved retrieval performance on both coarse-grained (MS COCO, Flickr) and fine-grained (IIW, DOCCI) benchmarks, with more significant gains on richer captions and enhanced robustness on RoCOCO.
为解决图像与文本描述间信息失衡导致的视觉-语言对齐不足问题，TEVI提出一种基于稀疏自编码器的框架，通过文本提示选择性保留图像嵌入中与描述相关的信息。该框架训练一个掩码模块，根据给定的文本描述重构图像嵌入，从而过滤无关视觉特征。实验表明，TEVI在合成和真实数据上能有效保留描述相关属性并剔除无关部分，在MS COCO、Flickr等粗粒度和IIW、DOCCI等细粒度基准上均提升检索性能，尤其在长描述和RoCOCO鲁棒性测试中表现更优。
On orbital stabilization of a circular motion primitive for a dynamic extension of the Dubins car model
Authors: Artem Angelchev-Shiryaev, Pavel E. Aleshin, Anton S. Shiriaev, Pavel A. Shamanaev, Leonid B. Freidovich
First: 2026-06-05T16:51:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:51:55+00:00
Comments: 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper addresses orbital stabilization of a circular motion primitive for a dynamic extension of the Dubins car model within a transverse-linearization framework. We show that the corresponding transverse linearization is unstable and not stabilizable by linear state feedback. Therefore, the standard linearization-based approach to orbital stabilization cannot be applied directly. The main contribution is a set of explicit and verifiable conditions that characterize when a controller design based on transverse linearization remains applicable. These conditions rely on the specific structure of the dynamics in a neighborhood of the motion and on the use of non-standard transverse coordinates for controller design and analysis. Numerical simulations illustrate the proposed design procedure.

标题：关于Dubins汽车模型动态扩展中圆形运动原语的轨道稳定化
本文在横向线性化框架下研究Dubins汽车模型动态扩展中圆形运动原语的轨道稳定化问题。我们证明相应的横向线性化是不稳定的，且无法通过线性状态反馈实现稳定化，因此标准的基于线性化的轨道稳定化方法不能直接应用。主要贡献是提出一组明确且可验证的条件，用于刻画在何种情况下基于横向线性化的控制器设计仍适用。这些条件依赖于运动在邻域内的动力学结构，以及在控制器设计与分析中使用非标准横向坐标。数值仿真展示了所提出的设计方法。

Motivated by the need to stabilize circular motion in a dynamic extension of the Dubins car model, this paper investigates orbital stabilization using transverse linearization.
