Numerical simulations confirm the validity of the proposed controller design framework.
为解决动态扩展的Dubins汽车模型中圆形运动的轨道稳定问题，本文在横向线性化框架下研究轨道稳定控制。研究发现，横向线性化本身不稳定，标准线性反馈无法实现稳定控制。为此，作者提出一组明确且可验证的条件，确保基于横向线性化的控制器设计在局部动力学结构下仍有效，关键在于采用非常规横向坐标。数值仿真验证了该方法在标准方法失效时仍能实现轨道稳定。
Twin: Tuning Learning Rate and Weight Decay of Deep Homogeneous Classifiers without Validation
Authors: Lorenzo Brigato, Stavroula Mougiakakou
First: 2024-03-08T18:57:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:51:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted at TMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce Tune without Validation (Twin), a simple and effective pipeline for tuning learning rate and weight decay of homogeneous classifiers without validation sets, eliminating the need to hold out data and avoiding the two-step process. Twin leverages the margin-maximization dynamics of homogeneous networks and an empirical scaling law that links training and test losses across hyper-parameter configurations. This mathematical modeling yields a regime-dependent, validation-free selection rule: in the non-separable regime, training loss is monotonic in test loss and therefore predictive of generalization, whereas in the separable regime, the parameters' norm becomes a reliable indicator of generalization due to margin maximization. Across 37 dataset-architecture configurations for image classification, we demonstrate that Twin achieves a mean absolute error of 1.28% compared to an Oracle baseline that selects HPs using test accuracy. We demonstrate Twin's benefits in scenarios where validation data is scarce, such as small-data regimes, or difficult and costly to collect, as in medical imaging. Code available at https://github.com/lorenzobrigato/twin.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Twin：无需验证集即可调优深度同质分类器的学习率与权重衰减
我们提出了无需验证集（Twin）的简单高效流程，用于在无验证集的情况下调优同质分类器的学习率与权重衰减，无需预留数据，避免两阶段过程。Twin利用同质网络的边际最大化动态及一个经验缩放定律，将不同超参数配置下的训练损失与测试损失关联起来。该数学建模得出一种依赖于训练阶段的、无需验证集的选择规则：在不可分区域，训练损失与测试损失单调相关，因而可预测泛化能力；在可分区域，由于边际最大化，参数范数成为泛化能力的可靠指标。在37种图像分类数据集-架构配置中，Twin相比使用测试准确率选择超参数的Oracle基线，平均绝对误差为1.28%。我们展示了Twin在验证数据稀缺（如小数据场景）或难以收集（如医学影像）时的优势。代码见https://github.com/lorenzobrigato/twin。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to tune learning rate and weight decay without validation sets to avoid data waste and simplify training, Twin leverages margin-maximization dynamics in homogeneous classifiers and an empirical scaling law linking training and test losses to derive a validation-free selection rule. In non-separable regimes, training loss reliably predicts test performance due to monotonicity; in separable regimes, parameter norms indicate generalization via margin maximization. Across 37 image classification dataset-architecture pairs, Twin achieved a 1.28% mean absolute error relative to an oracle that uses test accuracy, demonstrating effectiveness especially in data-scarce or costly-to-collect settings like medical imaging.
为避免数据保留和简化训练流程，Twin提出无需验证集即可调优学习率与权重衰减的方法，利用同质分类器的边际最大化特性及训练与测试损失间的经验缩放规律，推导出依赖训练阶段的无验证选择规则：在非可分区域，训练损失因单调性可预测泛化能力；在可分区域，参数范数成为可靠泛化指标。在37组图像分类数据集-架构组合中，Twin相较使用测试准确率的Oracle基线，平均绝对误差仅为1.28%，在数据稀缺或验证成本高的场景（如医学影像）中表现优异。
COMPOSE: Hypergraph Cover Optimization for Multi-view 3D Human Pose Estimation
Authors: Tony Danjun Wang, Tolga Birdal, Nassir Navab, Lennart Bastian
First: 2026-01-14T18:50:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:45:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D human pose estimation from sparse multi-view camera rigs is an essential task for numerous applications, including action recognition, sports analysis, and human-robot interaction. While learned methods dominate the field on benchmarks, they require large annotated datasets; training-free optimization-based methods remain promising as they circumvent 3D supervision by solving a correspondence problem across views from 2D detections. Existing combinatorial formulations rely on pairwise associations to model this correspondence problem and enforce global consistency across views only as a downstream constraint. However, reconciling locally plausible pairwise matches becomes brittle under occlusion and noisy detections, where local errors propagate globally. We propose COMPOSE, which recasts multi-view 3D human pose estimation as a weighted exact-cover optimization over a hypergraph of person hypotheses. Our formulation replaces pairwise association and post-hoc consistency enforcement with a single global combinatorial objective. To address the exponentially large candidate space, we introduce a geometric pruning strategy alongside two complementary solvers: an exact Integer Linear Programming formulation and a scalable relaxation via Belief Propagation. Without any 3D supervision, COMPOSE improves average precision by up to 31 points over the best optimization-based method and 13 points over self-supervised learned methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of higher-order combinatorial association for training-free multi-view 3D human pose estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：COMPOSE：面向多视角3D人体姿态估计的超图覆盖优化
从稀疏多视角相机阵列中估计3D人体姿态是动作识别、体育分析和人机交互等众多应用的关键任务。虽然学习方法在基准测试中占据主导地位，但它们需要大量标注数据；而无需训练的优化方法仍具潜力，因其通过解决跨视角的2D检测对应问题，避免了3D监督。现有组合式方法依赖成对关联建模该对应问题，并仅将全局一致性作为下游约束。然而，在遮挡和噪声检测下，局部合理成对匹配难以协调，局部误差会全局传播。我们提出COMPOSE，将多视角3D人体姿态估计重新表述为对人物假设超图的加权精确覆盖优化问题。我们的方法用单一全局组合目标替代成对关联与事后一致性约束。为应对指数级庞大的候选空间，我们引入几何剪枝策略，并结合两种互补求解器：精确整数线性规划形式与通过信念传播实现的可扩展松弛方法。无需3D监督，COMPOSE在最佳优化方法基础上平均精度提升31点，在自监督学习方法基础上提升13点，证明了高阶组合关联在无训练多视角3D人体姿态估计中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for training-free 3D human pose estimation from sparse multi-view cameras without 3D supervision, COMPOSE reformulates the problem as a weighted exact-cover optimization over a hypergraph of person hypotheses, replacing pairwise associations with a global combinatorial objective to enforce consistency. To handle the exponential search space, it combines geometric pruning with two solvers: an exact Integer Linear Programming approach and a scalable Belief Propagation relaxation. Experiments show that COMPOSE achieves up to 31-point higher average precision than the best optimization-based method and 13 points over self-supervised learned methods, demonstrating the efficacy of higher-order combinatorial modeling in this setting.
为实现无需3D监督的稀疏多视角相机下的人体3D姿态估计，COMPOSE将问题重构为在人体假设超图上的加权精确覆盖优化，用全局组合目标替代成对关联与事后一致性约束。为应对组合爆炸，引入几何剪枝及两种求解器：精确整数线性规划与可扩展的信念传播松弛。实验表明，无需3D监督下，COMPOSE比最优优化方法提升31点平均精度，比自监督学习方法高13点，验证了高阶组合建模的有效性。
Sycophantic Praise: Evaluating Excessive Praise in Language Models
Authors: Daniel Vennemeyer, Phan Anh Duong, Meryl Ye, Ruihong Huang, Tianyu Jiang
First: 2026-06-05T16:38:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:38:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sycophancy in language models is typically studied as excessive agreement or validation, while explicit praise and flattery have received comparatively little attention. We argue that sycophantic praise is a distinct alignment problem that cannot be reliably measured using current methods. We introduce a parameterized framework that measures whether praise is excessive relative to contribution quality and expected user ability. We show that our framework substantially outperforms generic LLM judges in agreement with human annotations, and that sycophantic praise occurs far more frequently in social and interpretive domains than in objective reasoning settings. Together, these findings position praise calibration as a distinct alignment challenge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：阿谀奉承式赞美：评估语言模型中的过度赞美
语言模型中的阿谀奉承通常被研究为过度同意或验证，而明确的赞美和奉承则受到相对较少的关注。我们认为，阿谀奉承式赞美是一个独特的对齐问题，无法通过当前方法可靠衡量。我们引入了一个参数化框架，用于衡量赞美是否相对于贡献质量及用户预期能力而言过度。我们表明，该框架在与人工标注的一致性上显著优于通用大语言模型评判者，且阿谀奉承式赞美在社交与解释性领域远比在客观推理场景中更为频繁。这些发现将赞美校准定位为一个独特的对齐挑战。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the under-studied phenomenon of sycophantic praise in language models, distinguishing it from general excessive agreement by focusing on explicit flattery that may be disproportionate to the user’s actual contribution or ability. The authors propose a parameterized framework that evaluates whether praise is excessive relative to both the quality of the contribution and the expected capability of the user, offering a more nuanced metric than existing methods. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework significantly outperforms generic LLM-based judges in aligning with human annotations, and that sycophantic praise is notably more prevalent in social and interpretive contexts compared to objective reasoning tasks, highlighting praise calibration as a unique alignment challenge.
该研究关注语言模型中常被忽视的阿谀奉承式赞美现象，将其与单纯的过度认同区分开来，聚焦于与用户实际贡献或能力不匹配的明显奉承。作者提出一种参数化框架，用于衡量赞美是否超出输入质量与用户预期能力所应得的合理水平。实验表明，该框架在与人类标注的一致性上显著优于通用大模型判断器，且阿谀式赞美在社交与解释性领域远多于客观推理场景，凸显了赞美校准作为独立对齐挑战的重要性。
Re-imagining ISO 26262 in the Age of Autonomous Vehicles: Enhancing Controllability through Transferability and Predictability
Authors: Chaitanya Shinde, Hadi Hajieghrary, Paul Schmitt, Adam Shoemaker, Bodo Seifert, Steve Kenner
First: 2026-06-05T16:35:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:35:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The ISO 26262 standard defines functional safety for road vehicles through risk assessments based on Severity, Exposure, and Controllability, grounded in a human-driven vehicle paradigm. In the context of autonomous vehicles (AVs), the absence of a human driver necessitates revisiting these principles. This paper decomposes the Controllability placeholder into two auditable evidence dimensions of ISO 26262 by introducing two measurable sub-concepts: Transferability and Predictability. Transferability extends Controllability to capture AV systems' ability to hand off control to dedicated fallback safety mechanisms, while Predictability captures how easily external agents can anticipate AV behavior. Predictability is formally defined from human-robot interaction-inspired principles, and a mathematical framework is provided to quantify it. A designed-versus-achievable gap is introduced to distinguish architectural fallback claims from scene-conditioned achievable fallback capability. The proposed metrics align with ISO 26262 and ISO/PAS 21448 (SOTIF), rendering fallback and interaction claims falsifiable and traceable across ODD slices. These dimensions complement rather than replace existing standards, and the enhancements preserve the structure of ISO 26262 while extending its applicability to driverless automated systems operating at SAE Levels 4 and 5.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在自动驾驶时代重新构想ISO 26262：通过可转移性和可预测性增强可控性
ISO 26262标准通过基于严重性、暴露度和可控性的风险评估，定义了道路车辆的功能安全，其基础是人类驾驶车辆的范式。在自动驾驶汽车（AV）背景下，由于缺乏人类驾驶员，有必要重新审视这些原则。本文将可控性概念分解为ISO 26262中两个可审计的证据维度，并引入两个可测量的子概念：可转移性和可预测性。可转移性扩展了可控性，以捕捉AV系统将控制权移交至专用备用安全机制的能力；可预测性则衡量外部代理预测AV行为的难易程度。可预测性基于人机交互原理正式定义，并提供数学框架以量化其值。引入设计与可实现能力之间的差距，以区分架构层面的备用声明与场景条件下的可实现备用能力。所提出的指标与ISO 26262及ISO/PAS 21448（SOTIF）保持一致，使备用和交互声明可在ODD切片间被证伪和追溯。这些维度补充而非替代现有标准，同时在保持ISO 26262结构的基础上，扩展其在SAE L4和L5级无人驾驶自动化系统中的适用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of ISO 26262’s Controllability concept in autonomous vehicles where human drivers are absent, this paper redefines Controllability into two measurable dimensions: Transferability and Predictability. Transferability assesses an AV’s ability to safely transfer control to fallback systems, while Predictability quantifies how predictable AV behavior is to external agents, using a formal mathematical framework derived from human-robot interaction principles. The study introduces a designed-versus-achievable gap to distinguish theoretical fallback capabilities from real-world scene-dependent performance, enabling falsifiable and traceable safety claims across operational design domains. These enhancements align with ISO 26262 and SOTIF, extending the standard’s applicability to SAE Levels 4 and 5 without replacing existing safety structures.
鉴于ISO 26262标准中‘可控性’概念在自动驾驶车辆中缺乏人类驾驶员时的适用性不足，本文将可控性分解为两个可度量维度：可转移性与可预测性。可转移性衡量自动驾驶系统在必要时能否安全移交控制权至备用安全机制，可预测性则量化外部主体对自动驾驶行为的可预见程度，其数学框架源自人机交互原理。研究引入‘设计能力’与‘实际达成能力’之间的差距，以区分理论上的备用机制能力与实际场景中的可实现能力，使安全声明可证伪、可追溯，覆盖不同操作设计域。该方法与ISO 26262及SOTIF标准兼容，扩展了标准在SAE L4/L5级自动驾驶中的适用性，而不替代现有框架。
Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Haoyuan Li, Zhengdong Hu, Jun Wang, Hehe Fan, Yi Yang
First: 2026-06-05T16:33:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:33:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Skill-3D：演化场景感知技能以实现自主3D空间推理
本文探讨了自主3D空间理解，即通过工具使用进行3D推理的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）代理。现有方法常误用工具，且在3D场景下表现出工具偏好偏差，导致自主范式仅比非自主策略略有优势。我们发现，3D空间推理任务在不同场景间具有异质性，而这些代理对所有场景采用统一的工具使用策略，而非根据具体场景和任务选择工具。为解决此问题，我们提出Skill-3D框架，该框架学习自演化场景感知技能。具体而言，Skill-3D识别任务场景，并将代理的工具使用轨迹记录至场景记忆库中，其中相似场景的成功轨迹被聚合并蒸馏为可复用的场景感知技能，失败轨迹则作为经验附加于技能中。训练过程中，当相似场景重现时，对应技能被注入以引导代理生成新轨迹，其成功与失败进一步优化技能，形成记忆库与技能库协同演化的循环。实验表明，Skill-3D显著提升了3D空间推理中的工具利用率（在VSI-Bench上从39%提升至78%），推动代理实现正确且充分的工具使用。例如，在MMSI-Bench上，其使Gemini-3-Flash性能提升67%。此外，我们对技能引导轨迹进行自主后训练，使Qwen3-VL-8B在VSI-Bench上性能提升43%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current MLLM agents in 3D spatial reasoning due to rigid, scene-agnostic tool usage, this paper introduces Skill-3D, a framework that enables agents to evolve scene-aware skills through self-reflection and memory. It identifies the current scene and logs tool-use trajectories into a Scene Memory, where successful paths from similar scenes are distilled into reusable skills while failures serve as corrective lessons. During training, recurring scenes trigger the injection of relevant skills, guiding new trajectories that further refine the skill library in a co-evolutionary loop. Experiments demonstrate that Skill-3D significantly enhances tool utilization, boosting performance from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench and improving Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench, with agentic post-training further elevating Qwen3-VL-8B by 43% on VSI-Bench.
为了解决当前多模态大模型在3D空间推理中因工具使用缺乏场景适应性而导致效率低下的问题，本文提出Skill-3D框架，使代理能通过自我反思与记忆进化出场景感知技能。该框架识别任务场景，将工具使用轨迹记录到场景记忆中，并从相似场景的成功轨迹中提炼可复用技能，同时保留失败案例作为经验。训练过程中，当遇到相似场景时，系统注入对应技能引导新轨迹，新轨迹的成功与失败进一步优化技能库，形成记忆与技能库协同进化的循环。实验表明，Skill-3D将工具利用率从39%提升至78%（VSI-Bench），并在MMSI-Bench和VSI-Bench上分别使模型性能提升67%和43%。
The Lipreading Gap: Do VSR Models Perceive Visual Speech Like Human Lipreaders?
Authors: Rishabh Jain, Naomi Harte
First: 2026-06-05T16:33:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:33:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted at INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual speech recognition (VSR) models now surpass human lipreaders on benchmarks, but do such gains establish human-like visual speech perception? To explore this, we compare three VSR systems with human baselines on the MaFI word-level lipreading dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme-level metrics. Although models achieve higher overall accuracy, they succeed and fail on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline given only a few initial phonemes rivals human lipreading. VSR word-level errors are consistently better explained by training word frequency than by the visual informativeness of words. Viseme accuracies, confusion matrices and human-model correlations further show that models gain most on visemes humans find hardest, and show much weaker dependence on visual clarity. Our work demonstrates that VSR systems rely primarily on language cues from training data rather than visual perception, failing to bind visual features into meaningful words.
中文标题/摘要
标题：唇读差距：视觉语音识别模型是否像人类唇读者那样感知视觉语音？
视觉语音识别（VSR）模型在基准测试中已超越人类唇读者，但这些提升是否意味着实现了类人水平的视觉语音感知？为探究此问题，我们在MaFI词级唇读数据集上，将三个VSR系统与人类基线进行比较，使用词、字符、音素和视觉音素级指标。尽管模型整体准确率更高，但其成功与失败的词与人类不同。仅凭少量初始音素的纯文本n-gram基线已可媲美人类唇读表现。VSR词级错误更由训练词频解释，而非词的视觉信息量。视觉音素准确率、混淆矩阵和人机相关性进一步表明，模型在人类最难识别的视觉音素上表现最佳，且对视觉清晰度依赖较弱。本研究证明，VSR系统主要依赖训练数据中的语言线索，而非视觉感知，未能将视觉特征整合为有意义的词。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether visual speech recognition (VSR) models achieve human-like perception of visual speech, despite outperforming human lipreaders on benchmarks. By comparing three VSR systems against human performance on the MaFI dataset using word, character, phoneme, and viseme metrics, the research finds that while models achieve higher overall accuracy, they make errors on different words than humans. A text-only n-gram baseline using only initial phonemes matches human performance, suggesting VSR models rely more on linguistic patterns than visual cues. Analysis of viseme accuracy, confusion matrices, and human-model correlations reveals models excel on visemes humans find hardest and show minimal dependence on visual clarity, indicating that VSR systems primarily leverage language priors from training data rather than true visual perception.
鉴于视觉语音识别（VSR）模型在基准测试中超越人类唇读者，但其是否具备类人视觉感知能力仍存疑，本研究在MaFI数据集上对比三类VSR模型与人类表现，采用词、字符、音素及视觉音素级指标。尽管模型整体准确率更高，但其错误模式与人类不同，常在人类易识别的词上出错。仅依赖初始音素的纯文本n-gram基线可媲美人类表现，表明模型依赖语言线索而非视觉特征。视觉音素准确率、混淆矩阵和人机相关性分析显示，模型在人类最易混淆的视觉音素上表现最佳，且对视觉清晰度依赖弱，说明其成功源于训练数据中的语言模式而非真实视觉感知。
RISE: Single Static Radar-based Indoor Scene Understanding
Authors: Kaichen Zhou, Laura Dodds, Sayed Saad Afzal, Fadel Adib
First: 2025-11-18T00:56:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:31:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robust and privacy-preserving indoor scene understanding remains a fundamental open problem. While optical sensors such as RGB and LiDAR offer high spatial fidelity, they suffer from severe occlusions and introduce privacy risks in indoor environments. In contrast, millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar preserves privacy and penetrates obstacles, but its inherently low spatial resolution makes reliable geometric reasoning difficult. We introduce RISE, the first benchmark and system for single-static-radar indoor scene understanding, jointly targeting layout reconstruction and object detection. RISE is built upon the key insight that multipath reflections-traditionally treated as noise-encode rich geometric cues. To exploit this, we propose a Bi-Angular Multipath Enhancement that explicitly models Angle-of-Arrival and Angle-of-Departure to recover secondary (ghost) reflections and reveal invisible structures. On top of these enhanced observations, a simulation-to-reality Hierarchical Diffusion framework transforms fragmented radar responses into complete layout reconstruction and object detection. Our benchmark contains 50,000 frames collected across 100 real indoor trajectories, forming the first large-scale dataset dedicated to single, static, radar-based indoor scene understanding. Extensive experiments show that RISE reduces the Chamfer Distance by 60% (down to 16 cm) compared to the state of the art in mmWave layout reconstruction, and delivers the first mmWave-based object detection, achieving 58% IoU. These results establish RISE as a new foundation for geometry-aware and privacy-preserving indoor scene understanding using a single static radar. Our website and code are available at https://rise-cvpr.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RISE：基于单静态雷达的室内场景理解
鲁棒且隐私保护的室内场景理解仍是一个基础性开放问题。虽然RGB和LiDAR等光学传感器提供高空间保真度，但在室内环境中易受严重遮挡并引入隐私风险。相比之下，毫米波（mmWave）雷达具有隐私保护和穿透障碍物的能力，但其固有的低空间分辨率使得可靠的几何推理困难。我们提出了RISE，这是首个针对单静态雷达室内场景理解的基准与系统，同时兼顾布局重建与物体检测。RISE基于一个关键洞察：多径反射——传统上被视为噪声——蕴含丰富的几何线索。为此，我们提出双角度多径增强方法，显式建模入射角与离射角以恢复二次（鬼影）反射并揭示不可见结构。在这些增强观测基础上，我们构建了一种从仿真到现实的分层扩散框架，将碎片化的雷达响应转化为完整的布局重建与物体检测。我们的基准数据集包含100条真实室内轨迹采集的5万帧，是首个专为单静态雷达室内场景理解设计的大规模数据集。大量实验表明，与现有毫米波布局重建方法相比，RISE将Chamfer距离降低60%（降至16厘米），并首次实现基于毫米波的物体检测，达到58%的IoU。这些成果确立了RISE作为单静态雷达实现几何感知与隐私保护室内场景理解的新基础。我们的网站和代码可在https://rise-cvpr.github.io获取。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for privacy-preserving and robust indoor scene understanding without occlusions, RISE introduces a novel system and benchmark using single-static mmWave radar, which inherently lacks spatial resolution but can penetrate obstacles. The method leverages multipath reflections—traditionally dismissed as noise—by employing a Bi-Angular Multipath Enhancement to recover ghost reflections and reveal hidden structures, followed by a Hierarchical Diffusion framework that transforms these enhanced radar signals into complete layout reconstructions and object detections. Evaluated on a 50,000-frame real-world dataset, RISE achieves a 60% reduction in Chamfer Distance (down to 16 cm) for layout reconstruction and delivers the first mmWave-based object detection with 58% IoU, establishing a new standard for radar-driven indoor scene understanding.
为解决室内场景理解中隐私保护与鲁棒性需求，RISE提出首个基于单静态毫米波雷达的系统与基准。其核心方法是将传统视为噪声的多径反射建模为几何线索，通过双角度多径增强技术（Bi-Angular Multipath Enhancement）重建隐藏结构。在此基础上，采用仿真到现实的分层扩散框架，实现完整室内布局重建与物体检测。在包含5万帧、100条真实轨迹的大型数据集上，RISE将布局重建的Chamfer距离降低60%（降至16厘米），并首次实现毫米波物体检测（IoU达58%），为雷达驱动的隐私保护室内场景理解奠定新基础。
Stream3D: Sequential Multi-View 3D Generation via Evidential Memory
Authors: Kaichen Zhou, Zeyang Bai, Xinhai Chang, Mengyu Wang, Paul Liang, Fangneng Zhan
First: 2026-05-20T17:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:30:58+00:00
Comments: Multi-view 3D Generation, Streaming 3D Generation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
View-conditioned 3D generators such as SAM 3D, TRELLIS, and Hunyuan3D produce high-quality object reconstructions from a single view, but real-world visual observation often arrives as long monocular streams. Naively applying these generators to each streaming frame independently leads to severe temporal inconsistency in the generated results. To address this problem, we propose Stream3D, the first training-free streaming mechanism that turns a frozen view-conditioned 3D generator into a streaming generator with constant cross-chunk memory. Stream3D achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory, which selectively caches the most informative historical frames based on a proposed evidence score mechanism. As the stream progresses, the memory dynamically updates to retain a fixed number of informative frames, preventing the memory footprint from growing linearly with sequence length. This also prevents degradation over long sequences and keeps the underlying generator completely unchanged without retraining, architectural modifications, or auxiliary losses. Evaluated on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks, Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines, including KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing, across both photometric and geometric metrics. More details can be found at: https://stream-3d.github.io/stream3d.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Stream3D：通过证据记忆实现顺序多视角3D生成
如SAM 3D、TRELLIS和Hunyuan3D等基于视角的3D生成器可从单视角生成高质量物体重建，但现实世界视觉观测常以长时单目流形式到达。若将这些生成器直接应用于每个流帧，会导致生成结果严重的时间不一致性。为解决此问题，我们提出Stream3D，这是首个无需训练的流式机制，可将冻结的视角条件3D生成器转化为具有恒定跨块记忆的流式生成器。Stream3D通过维护一个紧凑的证据记忆，根据所提证据评分机制选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧。随着流的推进，记忆动态更新以保留固定数量的信息帧，避免记忆占用随序列长度线性增长，同时防止长序列退化，且无需重新训练、架构修改或辅助损失，保持底层生成器完全不变。在真实与合成流式基准上评估，Stream3D在光度与几何指标上均优于包括KV缓存复用和基于流的特征编辑在内的潜在传输基线。详情见：https://stream-3d.github.io/stream3d.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to generate temporally consistent 3D reconstructions from long monocular video streams, where existing view-conditioned 3D generators fail due to frame-by-frame independence, Stream3D introduces a training-free streaming mechanism that adapts frozen 3D generators without retraining. It achieves this by maintaining a compact evidential memory that selectively caches informative historical frames using an evidence score, dynamically updating to retain only the most relevant frames while keeping memory size constant. Experimental results on both realistic and synthetic streaming benchmarks show that Stream3D outperforms latent-transport baselines like KV-cache reuse and flow-based feature editing across photometric and geometric metrics, preserving generator integrity and avoiding degradation over long sequences.
为解决从长单目视频流中生成时序一致3D重建的问题，Stream3D提出一种无需训练的方法，通过维护一个紧凑的证据记忆模块，将冻结的视图条件3D生成器适配为流式生成器。该记忆模块基于证据评分机制选择性缓存最具信息量的历史帧，并动态更新以保持固定帧数，避免内存随序列长度线性增长。在真实与合成流式基准测试中，Stream3D在光度与几何指标上均优于KV缓存复用与流式特征编辑等基线方法，且无需重训练、架构修改或辅助损失，保持原生成器性能不变。
Extracting Recurring Vulnerabilities from Black-Box LLM-Generated Software
Authors: Tomer Kordonsky, Amit LeVi, Maayan Yamin, Noam Benzimra, Avi Mendelson
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-02T22:23:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:30:46+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026, Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond (AIWILD)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
LLMs are increasingly used for code generation, but their outputs often follow recurring templates that can induce predictable vulnerabilities. We study vulnerability persistence in LLM-generated software and introduce Feature--Security Table (FSTab) with two components. First, FSTab enables a black-box attack that predicts likely backend vulnerabilities from observable frontend features and knowledge of the source LLM, without access to the backend or source code. Second, FSTab provides a model-centric evaluation that quantifies how consistently a model reproduces the same vulnerabilities across programs, semantics-preserving rephrasings, and application domains. We evaluate FSTab on state-of-the-art code LLMs, including GPT-5.2, Claude-4.5 Opus, and Gemini-3 Pro, across diverse application domains. Our results show strong cross-domain transfer: even when the target domain is excluded from training, FSTab achieves up to 94% attack success and 93% vulnerability coverage on Internal Tools (Claude-4.5 Opus). These findings expose an underexplored attack surface in LLM-generated software and highlight the security risks of code generation. Our code is available at https://github.com/fstabicml2026/FSTab
中文标题/摘要
标题：从黑盒LLM生成的软件中提取重复性漏洞
大型语言模型（LLM）越来越多地用于代码生成，但其输出常遵循重复模板，可能引发可预测的漏洞。我们研究了LLM生成软件中漏洞的持续性，并引入了特征-安全表（FSTab），包含两个组件。首先，FSTab支持一种黑盒攻击，仅通过可观察的前端特征和对源LLM的知识，即可预测后端漏洞，无需访问后端或源代码。其次，FSTab提供以模型为中心的评估方法，量化模型在不同程序、语义保持的重述和应用领域中重复产生相同漏洞的一致性。我们在包括GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5 Opus和Gemini-3 Pro在内的最先进代码LLM上评估FSTab，覆盖多样化的应用领域。结果表明存在强跨域迁移能力：即使目标领域未参与训练，FSTab在内部工具（Claude-4.5 Opus）上仍可实现高达94%的攻击成功率和93%的漏洞覆盖率。这些发现揭示了LLM生成软件中尚未充分探索的攻击面，并突显了代码生成的安全风险。代码开源于https://github.com/fstabicml2026/FSTab
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that LLM-generated code may repeatedly introduce predictable vulnerabilities due to template-like outputs, this work introduces Feature--Security Table (FSTab), a black-box attack framework that predicts backend vulnerabilities from frontend features without source code access, and a model-centric evaluation metric to measure vulnerability consistency across rephrasings and domains. Evaluated on GPT-5.2, Claude-4.5 Opus, and Gemini-3 Pro across diverse domains, FSTab demonstrates strong cross-domain transfer, achieving up to 94% attack success and 93% vulnerability coverage on Internal Tools when the target domain is unseen during training, revealing a significant security risk in LLM-generated software.
鉴于大语言模型生成的代码常出现重复性漏洞模式，本文提出Feature--Security Table（FSTab）框架，可在不接触源代码或后端的情况下，通过前端特征预测后端漏洞。FSTab同时支持对模型漏洞再现一致性的评估。在GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5 Opus和Gemini-3 Pro等主流模型上跨多个应用领域测试，FSTab即使在目标领域未参与训练时，仍能实现高达94%的攻击成功率和93%的漏洞覆盖率（针对Internal Tools），揭示了大语言模型生成代码中被忽视的攻击面与安全风险。
GEM-4D: Geometry-Enhanced Video World Models for Robot Manipulation
Authors: Kaichen Zhou, Yuzhen Chen, Fangneng Zhan, Hang Hua, Grace Chen, Xinhai Chang, Ao Qu, Yilun Du, Zhuang Liu, Paul Pu Liang, Mengyu Wang
First: 2026-05-20T21:36:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:29:58+00:00
Comments: Robotic World Model, Video Generative Model
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video world models can generate realistic futures from a single instruction, but they often fail to track the same physical points consistently across time. As a result, the generated videos appear plausible, yet lack the physical grounding required for reliable action execution, such as robot manipulation. We present GEM-4D, a geometry-grounded video world model that resolves this limitation by injecting dense 4D correspondence supervision distilled from a pretrained geometry foundation model into the video generative backbone during training. This supervision enables the model to jointly capture appearance and geometric structure while retaining a single-stream architecture with no additional inference cost. We further introduce an inverse dynamics module that converts correspondence-consistent video rollouts into executable robot trajectories, enabling direct deployment in both real-world and simulated manipulation. GEM-4D achieves state-of-the-art performance on both video prediction and geometric consistency across both simulation and realistic scenarios and improves real-world manipulation success from 61% to 81%. Additional results are available at https://gem-4d.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GEM-4D：面向机器人操作的几何增强视频世界模型
视频世界模型可从单一指令生成逼真的未来场景，但常无法在时间上持续追踪同一物理点。因此，生成的视频虽看似合理，却缺乏机器人操作所需的物理基础。我们提出GEM-4D，一种基于几何的视频世界模型，通过在训练中将从预训练几何基础模型蒸馏出的密集4D对应监督注入视频生成主干网络，解决此问题。该监督使模型能同时捕捉外观与几何结构，且保持单流架构，无额外推理开销。我们进一步引入逆动力学模块，将对应一致的视频 rollout 转换为可执行的机器人轨迹，支持在真实与仿真环境中直接部署。GEM-4D在仿真与真实场景的视频预测与几何一致性上均达到最先进水平，并将真实世界操作成功率从61%提升至81%。更多结果见 https://gem-4d.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically grounded video generation for robot manipulation, GEM-4D integrates dense 4D geometric correspondence supervision from a pretrained geometry model into a video generative framework to ensure consistent tracking of physical points over time. This approach enables the model to jointly learn appearance and geometry within a single-stream architecture without extra inference overhead. Experimental results show GEM-4D achieves state-of-the-art video prediction and geometric consistency in both simulation and real-world settings, and significantly boosts real-world manipulation success from 61% to 81%.
为解决视频生成缺乏物理一致性的问题，GEM-4D提出一种几何增强的视频世界模型，通过在生成器训练中注入来自预训练几何基础模型的密集4D对应监督，使模型同时保持外观与几何结构的一致性，且无需额外推理开销。该方法进一步引入逆动力学模块，将几何一致的视频轨迹转化为可执行的机器人动作，支持真实与仿真环境部署。实验表明，GEM-4D在视频预测与几何一致性上达到当前最优，且将真实世界操作成功率从61%提升至81%。
Watch, Remember, Reason: Human-View Video Understanding with MLLMs
Authors: Jiahao Meng, Yue Tan, Qi Xu, Kuan Gao, Weisong Liu, Yanwei Li, Jason Li, Lingdong Kong, Haochen Wang, Qianyu Zhou, Jiangning Zhang, Guangliang Cheng, Yunhai Tong, Lu Qi, Minghsuan Yang
First: 2026-06-05T16:29:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:29:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video understanding is being rapidly transformed by multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as research moves from short clips to long, multimodal, and knowledge-intensive video scenarios. These scenarios require models to handle sparse evidence, long-range dependencies, multimodal alignment, and reliable inference under limited computational budgets. This work presents a human-view perspective on LLM-based video understanding, organized around three functional abilities: watching, remembering, and reasoning. Rather than treating video tasks as isolated benchmarks, this view provides a unified structure for analyzing how video MLLMs acquire evidence, preserve context, and produce grounded outputs. We introduce a formulation that characterizes video understanding systems by their perceptual representations, memory states, reasoning traces, and final predictions. Based on this formulation, we identify challenges in spatio-temporal perception, efficient long-video processing, memory modeling, streaming understanding, and faithful reasoning. Representative methods are organized by their roles in video MLLM systems. Watching covers fine-grained, comprehensive, audio-visual, and efficient perception. Remembering includes offline and streaming memory, while reasoning covers text-only reasoning and thinking with videos. We further examine application domains such as egocentric, sports, instructional, medical, and narrative videos, and cover training datasets and evaluation benchmarks across task types, supervision formats, modalities, and capability dimensions. Finally, we outline open problems and future directions for scalable, memory-aware, and evidence-grounded video intelligence. Related works will be continuously traced at https://github.com/marinero4972/Awesome-HumanView-VideoUnderstanding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：观看、记忆、推理：基于MLLM的人类视角视频理解
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正迅速改变视频理解领域，研究正从短片段转向长、多模态、知识密集型视频场景。这些场景要求模型处理稀疏证据、长程依赖、多模态对齐及有限计算预算下的可靠推理。本文从人类视角出发，围绕观看、记忆、推理三大功能能力，提出一种统一框架，用于分析视频MLLM如何获取证据、保持上下文并生成具身化输出。我们提出一种系统表征方法，通过感知表征、记忆状态、推理轨迹和最终预测来刻画视频理解系统。基于此框架，我们识别出时空感知、高效长视频处理、记忆建模、流式理解及忠实推理等挑战。代表性方法按其在视频MLLM系统中的角色分类：观看涵盖细粒度、全面、视听及高效感知；记忆包括离线与流式记忆；推理涵盖纯文本推理与视频辅助推理。我们进一步考察了自拍、体育、教学、医疗及叙事类视频等应用场景，并覆盖任务类型、监督格式、模态及能力维度的训练数据与评估基准。最后，我们概述了可扩展、记忆感知、证据驱动的视频智能的开放问题与未来方向。相关工作将持续追踪于https://github.com/marinero4972/Awesome-HumanView-VideoUnderstanding。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to systematically analyze how multimodal large language models (MLLMs) handle complex video understanding tasks, this work introduces a human-view framework that decomposes video comprehension into three core abilities: watching, remembering, and reasoning. It proposes a unified characterization of video MLLMs through perceptual representations, memory states, reasoning traces, and final predictions, identifying key challenges such as spatio-temporal perception, efficient long-video processing, memory modeling, and faithful reasoning. The study organizes existing methods by their roles in these three functional components and surveys applications across domains like egocentric, sports, and medical videos, while mapping training datasets and evaluation benchmarks across modalities and task types. Experimental findings reveal that current models struggle with sparse evidence, long-range dependencies, and streaming understanding, highlighting the need for scalable, memory-aware architectures that ground outputs in multimodal evidence.
该论文从人类视角出发，提出视频理解应建模为模型如何感知、记忆与推理视频证据，以应对长视频、多模态、知识密集型场景中的挑战。文章将视频理解划分为三个核心能力：观看（感知）、记忆（存储）与推理（生成），构建统一框架分析模型在不同任务中的表现。作者通过感知表征、记忆状态、推理轨迹和最终输出四个维度刻画视频MLLM系统，指出关键挑战包括时空感知、长视频高效处理、记忆建模、流式理解与可靠推理。实验梳理现有方法按功能角色分类，覆盖如第一人称、体育、医疗等视频领域，并指出训练数据、评估基准、模态与能力维度上的不足，最后提出可扩展、记忆感知与证据驱动的未来研究方向。
OpenGlass: Open-Source Smart Glasses for On-Device Event-Based Gesture Recognition
Authors: Pietro Bonazzi, Julian Moosmann, Ahmet Celik, Philipp Mayer, Michele Magno
First: 2026-06-05T16:27:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:27:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Smart eyewear enables unobtrusive, context-aware interaction through multimodal sensors and on-device intelligence, but is severely limited by power, memory, and compute constraints in a compact form factor. Open-hardware platforms supporting event-based vision and embedded ML at this scale are rare. This work introduces an open-source smart glasses platform for rapid prototyping of novel sensors and algorithms. Its modular design uses a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras without full PCB redesign. A hardware-software co-designed power management system combines a configurable PMIC with event-driven wake-up via an nRF5340 coordinator, keeping the GAP9 RISC-V SoC powered down between inferences. The prototype achieves up to 11.8 hours of continuous on-device ML from a 200 mAh battery. As a demonstration, an egocentric hand gesture recognition pipeline was evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee GENX320 camera. R(2+1)D achieved the best cross-subject accuracy of 83.94\% (macro F1 = 0.781) under leave-two-subjects-out validation, with 33.9 ms end-to-end latency on the GAP9. Temporal augmentation and removal of ambiguous classes provided the largest gains (+8.9 pp). All hardware designs, firmware, and models are released open source.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OpenGlass：面向设备端事件驱动手势识别的开源智能眼镜
智能眼镜通过多模态传感器和设备端智能实现无侵入式、情境感知交互，但受限于紧凑外形下的功耗、内存和计算能力。支持该规模事件驱动视觉与嵌入式机器学习的开源硬件平台极为罕见。本工作提出一种开源智能眼镜平台，用于快速原型化新型传感器与算法。其模块化设计采用柔性FPC转接板，支持事件驱动与帧式相机，无需完整PCB重设计。软硬件协同设计的电源管理系统结合可配置PMIC与nRF5340协调器的事件驱动唤醒机制，使GAP9 RISC-V SoC在推理间保持关机状态。原型机在200mAh电池下实现长达11.8小时的连续设备端机器学习。作为演示，基于Prophesee GENX320相机的极性分离事件直方图，在LynX数据集上评估了以自我为中心的手势识别管道。R(2+1)D在留出两被试交叉验证下达到最佳跨被试准确率83.94%（宏F1=0.781），GAP9端到端延迟33.9ms。时序增强与移除模糊类别带来最大增益（+8.9个百分点）。所有硬件设计、固件及模型均已开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for low-power, on-device gesture recognition in smart glasses constrained by size and resources, this work presents OpenGlass: an open-source platform enabling rapid prototyping of event-based vision and embedded ML. It employs a modular design with a flexible FPC interposer to support both event-based and frame-based cameras, and a co-designed power system using an nRF5340 coordinator and GAP9 SoC to minimize power consumption. The prototype delivers up to 11.8 hours of continuous ML inference from a 200 mAh battery. Evaluated on the LynX dataset using polarity-separated event histograms from a Prophesee camera, the R(2+1)D model achieved 83.94% cross-subject accuracy (macro F1 = 0.781) with 33.9 ms latency, with temporal augmentation and class simplification yielding the largest performance gains.
为在紧凑型智能眼镜上实现低功耗、本地化的手势识别，本研究提出OpenGlass——一个开源平台，支持事件驱动视觉与嵌入式机器学习的快速原型开发。其模块化设计通过柔性FPC排线适配事件型与帧型相机，配合软硬件协同设计的电源管理系统（使用nRF5340与GAP9 SoC），在推理间保持休眠，实现200mAh电池下长达11.8小时的连续运行。在LynX数据集上，基于Prophesee相机的极性分离事件直方图，R(2+1)D模型在交叉受试者验证下达到83.94%准确率（宏F1=0.781），端到端延迟33.9毫秒，其中时序增强与模糊类别剔除带来8.9个百分点的性能提升。
Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus
Authors: Hanqiao Yu, Shusen Yang, Xuebin Ren, Cong Zhao
First: 2026-06-05T16:21:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:21:12+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 5 figures; Supplementary Information available as an ancillary file (79 pages)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过神经引导的λ演算发现复杂系统中的多尺度深度公式
科学中的一个基本问题是识别复杂系统中以简洁数学公式形式存在的底层模式。当前基于人工智能的方法在单尺度系统中表现优异，但在多尺度复杂系统中识别尺度特异性公式方面仍存在局限。我们提出了Deflex，一种端到端的人工智能方法，可自动从复杂系统中提取形式可能不同的多尺度公式，包括不变量和分布。Deflex由两个子系统组成：Deflexformer和Deflexpressor。Deflexpressor是一种用于高阶公式的λ演算符号回归模型；Deflexformer是一种可分解的深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示。Deflexpressor生成合成数据以预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来引导公式发现。在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统中，Deflex的效率比现有最先进方法高出7倍，同时实现了多尺度自动发现。我们的工作可作为跨学科科学发现的有用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to uncover concise mathematical patterns across multiple scales in complex systems, this work introduces Deflex, an end-to-end AI framework that automatically discovers multiscale formulas with varying forms such as invariants and distributions. Deflex combines Deflexformer, a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified cross-scale representations, with Deflexpressor, a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for generating higher-order formulas. Deflexpressor pre-trains Deflexformer using synthetic data, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Experiments across six diverse complex systems show Deflex achieves up to seven times higher efficiency than existing state-of-the-art methods while enabling fully automated multiscale formula extraction.
为了解决现有AI方法在多尺度复杂系统中难以发现尺度特异性数学公式的难题，作者提出了Deflex框架，结合Deflexformer与Deflexpressor两个子系统。Deflexpressor基于lambda演算进行符号回归，生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者是一种可分解的深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表示。通过这种协同机制，Deflex能够解耦并引导多尺度公式的自动发现，包括不变量与分布等不同形式。在六个具有多样行为的复杂系统实验中，Deflex效率较现有最先进方法提升最高达七倍，实现了多尺度公式的自动化提取。
Rapid co-design of Buoyancy-assisted robots for Challenging Locomotion using Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists
Authors: Ankit Sinha, Nitish Sontakke, Dennis Hong, Yusuke Tanaka, Sehoon Ha
First: 2026-06-05T16:18:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:18:15+00:00
Comments: Submitted to RA-L
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing high-performance legged robots requires jointly optimizing morphology and control. Model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers an alternative to model-predictive control for developing robust controllers without explicitly specifying robot dynamics. Thus, we have seen theuse of RL to train controllers and evaluate designs for robot morphology optimization. While RL has shown success inlocomotion, using it in the co-design inner loop is expensive due to repeated policy training. Universal policies conditioned on morphology offer a promising alternative, but suffer from behavioral diversity collapse, converging to a single strategy that performs sub-optimally across designs. On the other hand, end-to-end Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures fail due to a collapse in its representation. We propose Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES), a framework that decouples design-space partitioning from policy learning to capture diverse behaviors explicitly. GES assigns specialist policies to evolving Gaussian regions and iteratively refines them via training, probing, and territory expansion. The resulting specialists are integrated into a design sampling loop, replacing costly re-training with direct evaluation. When tested on the Buoyancy-Assisted Light Legged Unit (BALLU), GES discovers designs with 5 - 25% higher performance than naive universal policies. On hardware, a GES optimized design overcomes a 24 cm tall obstacle - 3x improvement over the baseline BALLU design. Moreover, GES curtails design optimization time by 37%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于高斯进化专家的浮力辅助机器人在复杂运动中的快速协同设计
设计高性能腿式机器人需同时优化形态与控制。无模型强化学习（RL）为开发无需显式指定机器人动力学的鲁棒控制器提供了替代方案。因此，RL被用于训练控制器并评估机器人形态优化设计。尽管RL在运动控制中已取得成功，但在协同设计内循环中使用它因需重复策略训练而代价高昂。条件于形态的通用策略虽具潜力，却因行为多样性崩溃，收敛至单一策略，跨设计表现次优。另一方面，端到端专家混合架构因表示坍塌而失效。我们提出高斯进化专家（GES）框架，将设计空间划分与策略学习解耦，显式捕捉多样化行为。GES将专家策略分配至演化高斯区域，并通过训练、探测与区域扩展迭代优化。所得专家集成至设计采样循环，以直接评估替代昂贵重训练。在浮力辅助轻型腿式单元（BALLU）测试中，GES发现性能比朴素通用策略高5%-25%。在硬件上，GES优化设计可跨越24厘米障碍——较基线BALLU设计提升3倍。此外，GES将设计优化时间缩短37%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of retraining policies in RL-based co-design of legged robots, this work introduces Gaussian Evolutionary Specialists (GES), a method that decouples morphology design from policy learning by assigning specialist policies to evolving Gaussian regions in design space. These specialists are iteratively refined through training, probing, and territory expansion, enabling direct evaluation without costly retraining. Tested on the Buoyancy-Assisted Light Legged Unit (BALLU), GES yielded designs 5–25% more performant than universal policies and enabled a hardware robot to clear a 24 cm obstacle—three times higher than the baseline—while reducing optimization time by 37%.
为解决机器人形态与控制联合优化中反复训练策略的成本问题，本文提出高斯进化专家（GES）框架，将设计空间划分与策略学习解耦，显式捕捉多样化的运动行为。GES 将专家策略分配给设计空间中演化中的高斯区域，通过训练、探测与区域扩展迭代优化策略，并将其集成到设计采样循环中，以直接评估替代昂贵重训练。在浮力辅助轻型腿式机器人（BALLU）上的实验表明，GES 设计性能比通用策略高 5–25%，可跨越 24 厘米障碍物（比基线提升 3 倍），且优化时间缩短 37%。
The Masked Advantage: Uncovering Local-Language Access to Cultural Knowledge in LLMs
Authors: Yang Zhang, Xiao Fei, Amr Mohamed, Sarah Almeida Carneiro, Mersin Konomi, Mingmeng Geng, Ahmed Asaad, Guokan Shang, Michalis Vazirgiannis
First: 2026-06-05T16:16:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:16:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used to answer culturally grounded questions across languages, yet it remains unclear whether local cultural knowledge is better accessed through English or the local language. Existing evaluations face two key limitations: many rely on parallel template-based questions that may not reflect how cultural knowledge naturally appears, and raw accuracy conflates general language proficiency with language-conditioned knowledge access. We address these issues with a controlled framework built on real-world cultural questions collected from regional benchmarks and local sources. By crossing question type (culture-agnostic vs. culture-specific) with query language (English vs. local language), and estimating ability with a shared 1PL item response theory model, we separate proficiency from localized knowledge access. Across 13 locales and roughly 80 models, we find a consistent English advantage on culture-agnostic questions, indicating stronger English proficiency. However, after accounting for this proficiency gap, local languages show a positive knowledge-access advantage in nearly all locale-model settings. This advantage is often masked in raw accuracy but becomes more visible for frontier, regionally aligned, or language-adapted models. Our results suggest that weaker local-language performance does not necessarily imply weaker cultural knowledge; rather, local cultural knowledge may be more accessible through the local language but hidden by limited language proficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面具优势：揭示大语言模型中本地语言对文化知识获取的影响
大语言模型越来越多地用于跨语言回答文化相关的提问，但尚不清楚本地文化知识是通过英语还是本地语言更易获取。现有评估存在两大局限：许多评估依赖平行模板式问题，可能无法反映文化知识自然呈现的方式；而原始准确率混淆了通用语言能力与语言条件下的知识获取能力。我们通过基于真实世界文化问题的受控框架解决这些问题，这些问题来自区域基准和本地来源。通过交叉问题类型（文化无关 vs. 文化特定）与查询语言（英语 vs. 本地语言），并使用共享的1PL项目反应理论模型评估能力，我们分离了语言能力与本地化知识获取。在13个地区和约80个模型中，我们发现文化无关问题上英语具有稳定优势，表明英语能力更强。但在扣除这一能力差距后，本地语言在几乎所有地区-模型设置中均显示出积极的知识获取优势。这一优势在原始准确率中常被掩盖，但在前沿、区域对齐或语言适配模型中更为明显。我们的结果表明，本地语言表现较弱并不一定意味着文化知识较弱；相反，本地文化知识可能更易通过本地语言获取，但被有限的语言能力所掩盖。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models access local cultural knowledge more effectively through English or the local language, addressing limitations in prior evaluations that conflated language proficiency with cultural knowledge access. Using a controlled framework based on real-world cultural questions from 13 locales, the researchers cross-analyzed question types (culture-agnostic vs. culture-specific) and query languages (English vs. local), estimating model ability via a shared 1PL item response theory model to isolate proficiency from knowledge access. Results show an English advantage on culture-agnostic questions, reflecting stronger English proficiency, but after controlling for this, local languages consistently outperformed English in accessing culture-specific knowledge across nearly all locale-model combinations, especially in frontier, regionally aligned, or language-adapted models, suggesting local cultural knowledge is more accessible in the local language despite weaker overall performance.
本研究探讨大语言模型在英语与本地语言中获取文化知识的优劣，针对现有评估方法依赖人工模板且混淆语言能力与文化知识的问题，构建了基于13个地区真实文化问题的控制实验框架，通过文化无关与文化相关问题类型与查询语言的交叉设计，并采用1PL项目反应理论模型分离语言能力与知识获取能力。结果表明，英语在文化无关问题上表现更优，反映更强的语言能力，但在控制该能力差异后，本地语言在文化相关问题上普遍优于英语，尤其在前沿或本地化模型中优势更明显，说明本地文化知识在本地语言中更易被访问，但因语言能力不足而被掩盖。
Model Context Protocols in Adaptive Transport Systems: A Survey
Authors: Gaurab Chhetri, Shriyank Somvanshi, Md Monzurul Islam, Shamyo Brotee, Mahmuda Sultana Mimi, Dipti Koirala, Biplov Pandey, Subasish Das
First: 2025-08-26T17:58:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:15:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid expansion of interconnected devices, autonomous systems, and AI applications has created severe fragmentation in adaptive transport systems, where diverse protocols and context sources remain isolated. This survey provides the first systematic investigation of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) as a unifying paradigm, highlighting its ability to bridge protocol-level adaptation with context-aware decision making. Analyzing established literature, we show that existing efforts have implicitly converged toward MCP-like architectures, signaling a natural evolution from fragmented solutions to standardized integration frameworks. We propose a five-category taxonomy covering adaptive mechanisms, context-aware frameworks, unification models, integration strategies, and MCP-enabled architectures. Our findings reveal three key insights: traditional transport protocols have reached the limits of isolated adaptation, MCP's client-server and JSON-RPC structure enables semantic interoperability, and AI-driven transport demands integration paradigms uniquely suited to MCP. Finally, we present a research roadmap positioning MCP as a foundation for next-generation adaptive, context-aware, and intelligent transport infrastructures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应传输系统中的模型上下文协议：一项综述
互联设备、自主系统和AI应用的快速扩展，导致自适应传输系统出现严重碎片化，其中多样化的协议和上下文源仍相互隔离。本综述首次系统性研究了模型上下文协议（MCP）作为统一范式的能力，强调其在协议级自适应与上下文感知决策之间的桥梁作用。通过分析现有文献，我们表明当前研究已隐含收敛于MCP类架构，预示着从碎片化解决方案向标准化集成框架的自然演进。我们提出一个涵盖自适应机制、上下文感知框架、统一模型、集成策略及MCP支持架构的五类分类法。研究发现揭示三个关键洞见：传统传输协议已达到孤立自适应的极限；MCP的客户端-服务器与JSON-RPC结构支持语义互操作性；AI驱动的传输需求亟需与MCP独特适配的集成范式。最后，我们提出研究路线图，将MCP定位为下一代自适应、上下文感知和智能传输基础设施的基础。

由于互联设备、自主系统和AI应用的快速增长，适应性传输系统面临协议与上下文源碎片化的问题，本综述首次系统性地提出模型上下文协议（MCP）作为统一范式，弥合协议自适应与上下文感知决策之间的鸿沟。通过对现有文献的分析，作者发现当前研究已隐含向MCP架构收敛，并提出涵盖自适应机制、上下文框架、统一模型、集成策略和MCP架构的五类分类体系。主要发现表明，传统传输协议已接近自适应极限，MCP的客户端-服务器与JSON-RPC结构支持语义互操作性，且AI驱动的传输需求亟需MCP特有的集成范式，从而确立MCP作为下一代智能传输基础设施的基础地位。
PAGE-4D: VGGT-4D Perception via Disentangled Pose and Geometry Estimation
Authors: Kaichen Zhou, Yuhan Wang, Grace Chen, Xinhai Chang, Gaspard Beaudouin, Fangneng Zhan, Paul Pu Liang, Mengyu Wang
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-10-20T14:17:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:14:58+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026, VGGT-4D, Dynamic VGGT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent 3D feed-forward models, such as the Visual Geometry Grounded Transformer (VGGT), have shown strong capability in inferring 3D attributes of static scenes. However, since they are typically trained on static datasets, these models often struggle in real-world scenarios involving complex dynamic elements, such as moving humans or deformable objects like umbrellas. To address this limitation, we introduce PAGE-4D, a feedforward model that extends VGGT to dynamic scenes, enabling camera pose estimation, depth prediction and point cloud reconstruction - all without post-processing. A central challenge in multitask 4D reconstruction is the inherent conflict between tasks: accurate camera pose estimation requires suppressing dynamic regions, while geometry reconstruction requires modeling them. To resolve this tension, we propose a dynamics aware aggregator that disentangles static and dynamic information by predicting a dynamics-aware mask - suppressing motion cues for pose estimation while amplifying them for geometry reconstruction. Extensive experiments show that PAGE-4D consistently outperforms the original VGGT in dynamic scenarios, achieving superior results in camera pose estimation, monocular and video depth estimation, and dense point map reconstruction. Necessary code and additional demos are available at Link: https://page4d.github.io/, including both the training-and-inference masking variant and the training-only masking variant (= VGGT architecture at inference). Keywords: VGGT-4D, 4D Perception, Dynamic Scene Reconstruction.

标题：PAGE-4D：通过解耦姿态与几何估计实现VGGT-4D感知
近期的3D前馈模型（如视觉几何锚定变换器VGGT）在推断静态场景的3D属性方面表现出强大能力。然而，由于它们通常在静态数据集上训练，这些模型在涉及复杂动态元素（如移动的人或可变形物体如雨伞）的真实世界场景中往往表现不佳。为解决这一局限，我们提出了PAGE-4D，一种扩展VGGT以支持动态场景的前馈模型，可实现相机姿态估计、深度预测和点云重建——且无需后处理。多任务4D重建的核心挑战在于任务间的固有冲突：精确的相机姿态估计需要抑制动态区域，而几何重建则需要建模这些区域。为解决此矛盾，我们提出一种动态感知聚合器，通过预测动态感知掩码来解耦静态与动态信息——在姿态估计中抑制运动线索，而在几何重建中增强其影响。大量实验表明，PAGE-4D在动态场景中持续优于原始VGGT，在相机姿态估计、单目与视频深度估计及稠密点图重建方面均取得更优结果。必要代码及附加演示可在链接：https://page4d.github.io/获取，包括训练与推理掩码变体及仅训练掩码变体（=推理时采用VGGT架构）。关键词：VGGT-4D，4D感知，动态场景重建。

Motivated by the limitation of static 3D models like VGGT in handling dynamic scenes, PAGE-4D extends VGGT to support 4D perception without post-processing, enabling camera pose estimation, depth prediction, and point cloud reconstruction. It addresses task conflict by introducing a dynamics-aware aggregator that disentangles static and dynamic content using a predicted mask: suppressing motion cues for pose estimation while enhancing them for geometry reconstruction. Experiments demonstrate consistent performance gains over VGGT in dynamic scenarios, including improved camera pose accuracy, monocular and video depth estimation, and dense point cloud reconstruction.
为了解决VGGT等静态3D模型在动态场景中表现不佳的问题，PAGE-4D扩展了VGGT以支持无需后处理的4D感知，实现动态场景下的相机位姿估计、深度预测和点云重建。该方法引入动态感知聚合器，通过预测掩码分离静态与动态信息，在位姿估计中抑制运动线索而在几何重建中增强其影响。实验表明，PAGE-4D在相机位姿估计、单目深度估计和稠密点云重建等多个指标上均显著优于VGGT。
DisPOSE: Projected Polystochastic Diffusion for Self-Supervised Multi-View 3D Human Pose Estimation
Authors: Tony Danjun Wang, Tolga Birdal, Nassir Navab
First: 2026-06-05T16:14:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:14:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recovering 3D human poses for multiple individuals from different camera views is a fundamental bottleneck for analyzing interacting behaviors. Existing self-supervised approaches leverage synthetic catalogues of 3D poses; however, this leads to poor generalization in real-world scenarios due to distribution shifts. We therefore introduce DisPOSE, a self-supervised framework that approximates the inherently discrete multi-view person-assignment problem as a generative diffusion process over the space of polystochastic tensors. By employing differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, our model learns to guide solutions toward valid and feasible assignments based on 2D image priors. The complete 3D skeletons of localized individuals are then regressed using a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that explicitly models relational structures and articulated joints across multiple views. The proposed approach outperforms current state-of-the-art self-supervised methods on standard datasets and demonstrates strong performance on a newly proposed benchmark featuring highly occluded scenes from surgical operating rooms. Our diffusion-based localization demonstrates high label efficiency, retaining 99% of its performance with only 10% of the pseudo-labels. Notably, disentangling the assignment and root regression components while maintaining differentiability makes DisPOSE nearly agnostic to different camera arrangements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DisPOSE：用于自监督多视角3D人体姿态估计的投影多随机扩散
从不同摄像机视角恢复多人的3D姿态是分析交互行为的基本瓶颈。现有自监督方法依赖合成3D姿态数据集，但在真实场景中因分布偏移导致泛化能力差。为此，我们提出DisPOSE，一种自监督框架，将固有的离散多视角人物分配问题近似为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程。通过在去噪过程中使用可微Sinkhorn投影，模型学习基于2D图像先验引导解向有效且可行的分配。随后，采用超图卷积解码器回归局部化个体的完整3D骨架，显式建模多视角下的关系结构与关节运动。该方法在标准数据集上超越当前最先进的自监督方法，并在新提出的手术室高遮挡场景基准上表现优异。我们的扩散式定位方法具有高标签效率，仅用10%伪标签即可保持99%性能。值得注意的是，通过解耦分配与根回归组件并保持可微性，DisPOSE几乎对不同摄像机布局不敏感。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately estimating 3D human poses across multiple camera views without labeled data, the authors propose DisPOSE, a self-supervised method that models the discrete person-assignment problem as a diffusion process over polystochastic tensors. Using differentiable Sinkhorn projections during denoising, the model learns to assign individuals to views based on 2D image priors, then reconstructs full 3D skeletons via a Hypergraph-Convolutional Decoder that encodes relational and joint structures. Experiments show DisPOSE surpasses prior self-supervised methods on standard benchmarks and excels in occluded surgical scenes, maintaining 99% performance with just 10% of pseudo-labels, and remains robust to varying camera configurations due to its decoupled, differentiable architecture.
为解决真实场景中多视角3D人体姿态估计因合成数据导致泛化能力差的问题，DisPOSE将离散的多人分配问题建模为多随机张量空间上的生成扩散过程，通过可微Sinkhorn投影结合2D图像先验引导有效分配，并用超图卷积解码器显式建模多视角下关节结构与关系。实验表明，该方法在标准数据集上超越现有自监督方法，在高遮挡手术场景新基准上表现优异，仅用10%伪标签即可保持99%性能，且因解耦设计对相机布局变化几乎不敏感。
SERNF: Sample-Efficient Real-World Dexterous Policy Fine-Tuning via Action-Chunked Critics and Normalizing Flows
Authors: Chenyu Yang, Denis Tarasov, Davide Liconti, Romain Guntz, Hehui Zheng, Robert K. Katzschmann
First: 2026-02-10T09:28:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:14:20+00:00
Comments: https://srl-ethz.github.io/SERNF/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-world fine-tuning of dexterous manipulation policies remains challenging due to limited real-world interaction budgets and highly multimodal action distributions. Diffusion-based policies, while expressive, do not permit conservative likelihood-based updates during fine-tuning because action probabilities are intractable. In contrast, conventional Gaussian policies collapse under multimodality, particularly when actions are executed in chunks, and standard per-step critics fail to align with chunked execution, leading to poor credit assignment. We present SERFN, a sample-efficient off-policy fine-tuning framework with normalizing flow (NF) to address these challenges. The normalizing flow policy yields exact likelihoods for multimodal action chunks, allowing conservative, stable policy updates through likelihood regularization and thereby improving sample efficiency. An action-chunked critic evaluates entire action sequences, aligning value estimation with the policy's temporal structure and improving long-horizon credit assignment. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of a likelihood-based, multimodal generative policy combined with chunk-level value learning on real robotic hardware. We evaluate SERFN on two challenging dexterous manipulation tasks in the real world: cutting tape with scissors retrieved from a case, and in-hand cube rotation with a palm-down grasp -- both of which require precise, dexterous control over long horizons. On these tasks, SERFN achieves stable, sample-efficient adaptation where standard methods struggle.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SERNF：通过分段动作批评者与归一化流实现样本高效的现实世界灵巧策略微调
由于现实世界交互预算有限且动作分布高度多模态，灵巧操作策略的现实世界微调仍具挑战性。扩散策略虽表达能力强，但在微调中无法进行保守的似然更新，因动作概率不可计算。相比之下，传统高斯策略在多模态下尤其在分段动作执行时会失效，且标准每步批评者无法与分段执行对齐，导致信用分配不佳。我们提出SERNF，一种结合归一化流（NF）的样本高效离策略微调框架。归一化流策略可为多模态动作分段提供精确似然，通过似然正则化实现保守、稳定的策略更新，从而提升样本效率。分段动作批评者评估完整动作序列，使价值估计与策略的时间结构对齐，改善长时信用分配。据我们所知，这是首次在真实机器人硬件上将基于似然的多模态生成策略与分段级价值学习相结合。我们在两个具有挑战性的现实世界灵巧操作任务上评估SERNF：从盒中取出剪刀剪胶带，以及以掌心向下抓取方式旋转手中立方体——两者均需在长时域内实现精确灵巧控制。在这些任务中，SERNF实现了稳定且样本高效的适应，而标准方法则难以胜任。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of fine-tuning dexterous manipulation policies in real-world settings with limited interaction data and multimodal action distributions, the authors propose SERNF, an off-policy framework that combines normalizing flows for exact likelihood-based policy updates and action-chunked critics for improved credit assignment. The normalizing flow policy enables stable, conservative updates by modeling multimodal action chunks with tractable likelihoods, while the chunked critic evaluates entire action sequences to align value estimation with the policy’s temporal structure. Evaluated on two real robotic tasks—tape cutting and in-hand cube rotation—SERNF demonstrates superior sample efficiency and stability compared to standard methods, achieving successful adaptation where conventional approaches fail.
为了解决在有限真实交互预算下对灵巧操作策略进行高效微调的问题，SERNF提出了一种基于归一化流的离策略微调框架，支持多模态动作块的精确似然更新，并结合动作块级批评者以改善长时序信用分配。在真实机器人上的两个任务——剪胶带和掌心向下握持的立方体旋转中，SERNF实现了稳定且高效的样本利用，显著优于传统方法在这些任务中的表现。
Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying
Authors: Abhijeet Praveen, Sareh Soleimani, Cormac Cureton, Aman Sidhu, Kintak Raymond Yu, Cristian Cojocaru, Narges Armanfard
First: 2026-06-05T16:09:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:09:39+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ECML PKDD 2026 (Applied Data Science Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

标题：基于视频的等离子喷涂飞行粒子特性预测
大气等离子喷涂（APS）是一种广泛使用的涂层工艺，其中飞行粒子的温度和速度对涂层质量有显著影响。然而，这些粒子特性在操作过程中难以持续监测，因此推动了非侵入式数据驱动诊断方法的发展。本研究探讨了高速视频观测等离子射流以估计APS中飞行粒子特性的预测潜力。我们引入了三种不同的视频衍生特征表示方法，并使用表格式先验数据拟合网络（TabPFN）、卷积神经网络（CNN）及经典回归基线方法（包括随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归和XGBoost）进行评估。实验在63次APS喷涂运行的126组标注前后喷涂视频记录上采用分组留一交叉验证进行。在所有工程特征实验中，TabPFN在温度预测方面表现最稳定，使用组合特征表示时R²达0.86；CNN模型在速度预测方面表现更强，R²达0.81。此外，我们评估了直接在原始视频帧上操作的模型，使用预训练CNN并添加回归头，温度和速度预测的R²分别达到0.90和0.82。结果表明，视频衍生的射流信息为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监控提供了有前景且可扩展的基础。

Three video-derived feature representations were engineered and evaluated using TabPFN, CNNs, and classical regression methods across 126 labeled video recordings. A Human-Sensitive Controller: Adapting to Human Musculoskeletal Disorder-Related Constraints via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Vitor Martins, Sara M. Cerqueira, Mercedes Balcells, Elazer R Edelman, Cristina P. Santos
First: 2025-04-14T11:09:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:09:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Work-Related Musculoskeletal Disorders continue to be a major challenge in industrial environments, leading to reduced workforce participation, increased healthcare costs, and long-term disability. This study introduces a human-sensitive robotic system aimed at reintegrating individuals with a history of musculoskeletal disorders into standard job roles, while simultaneously optimizing ergonomic conditions for the broader workforce. This research leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to develop a human-aware control strategy for collaborative robots, focusing on optimizing ergonomic conditions and preventing pain during task execution. Two RL approaches, Q-Learning and Deep Q-Network (DQN), were implemented and tested to personalize control strategies based on individual user characteristics. Although experimental results revealed a simulation-to-real gap, a fine-tuning phase successfully adapted the policies to real-world conditions. DQN outperformed Q-Learning by completing tasks faster while maintaining zero pain risk and safe ergonomic levels, achieving on average 38% shorter task completion times across all tested anthropometries. The structured testing protocol confirmed the system's adaptability to diverse human anthropometries, underscoring the potential of RL-driven cobots to enable safer, more inclusive workplaces.

标题：一种人性化的控制器：通过强化学习适应与肌肉骨骼障碍相关的约束

为应对工业环境中持续存在的职业性肌肉骨骼疾病问题，本研究采用强化学习构建一种人性化的机器人控制系统，以适应个体的生理限制并预防疼痛。研究实现了Q-Learning与深度Q网络（DQN）两种强化学习方法，旨在根据用户特征个性化机器人控制策略，提升任务效率与人体工学安全性。尽管存在仿真与现实环境的差距，通过微调策略成功适配真实场景，DQN表现更优：平均任务完成时间缩短38%，且全程无疼痛风险、保持安全人体工学水平，验证了该系统在包容性工作场所中的应用潜力。
Sparsely gated tiny linear experts
Authors: Simon Schug
First: 2026-06-05T16:06:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:06:47+00:00
Comments: Code available at https://github.com/smonsays/sparsely-gated-linear
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏门控的微型线性专家
稀疏性允许在不按比例增加计算成本的情况下扩展模型参数。虽然混合专家（MoE）模型正变得越来越稀疏，但单个专家通常仍保持较大且密集。本文证明，通过将每个专家缩小为单个神经元，并仅选择众多可用神经元中的极小比例，可进一步提升计算效率和可解释性。反直觉的是，实现这两者的关鍵在于移除通常应用于专家的非线性，从而形成稀疏门控线性神经元（sgatlin）网络。在等计算量比较中，我们发现将所有Transformer前馈层替换为sgatlin可提升不同计算预算下语言模型的困惑度。同时，由此产生的前馈电路的稀疏性和线性特性为模型可解释性提供了新机遇。在小型案例研究中，我们证明无需训练额外替代模型即可解释sgatlin中的前馈电路。我们发现它们形成语义结构化的簇，并在事实回忆中具有因果关联。我们的发现描绘了一条通向计算高效且可解释的Transformer前馈层的可能路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost, this work explores sparsity in transformer feedforward layers by replacing dense experts with sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin), each consisting of a single neuron. By removing the nonlinearity typically applied to experts, the method achieves both compute efficiency and interpretability. Experiments show that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity across different compute budgets in language models, while also enabling direct interpretation of feedforward circuits without additional training, revealing semantically structured clusters causally linked to factual recall.
为在不显著增加计算成本的前提下扩展模型参数，作者提出稀疏门控微小线性专家（sgatlin）架构，每个专家仅由单个神经元构成，仅激活极少数可用神经元。通过移除专家通常使用的非线性激活函数，实现了计算效率与可解释性的双重提升。实验表明，将Transformer前馈层替换为sgatlin可提升不同算力预算下语言模型的困惑度，同时其线性与稀疏结构使前馈电路可直接解释，形成语义结构化簇并因果关联事实记忆。
Robust Instruction Compliance in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Wo Wei Lin, Ethan Rathbun, Enrico Marchesini, Xiang Zhi Tan
First: 2026-05-12T19:01:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:06:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in real-world use cases may need to adapt to external natural language instructions that interrupt ongoing behavior and conflict with long-horizon objectives. However, conditioning rewards on instructions introduces a fundamental failure mode as Bellman updates couple value estimates across instruction contexts, leading to inconsistent values when instructions interrupt macro-actions. We propose Macro-Action Value Correction for Instruction Compliance (MAVIC), which corrects Bellman backups at instruction boundaries by correcting the incoming instruction objective and restoring the continuation value under the current objective. Unlike reward shaping, MAVIC modifies the bootstrapping target itself, enabling consistent value estimation under stochastic instruction switching within a unified policy. We provide theoretical analysis and an actor-critic implementation, and show that MAVIC achieves high instruction compliance while preserving base task performance in increasingly complex cooperative multi-agent environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协作多智能体强化学习中的鲁棒指令合规性
现实世界中的多智能体强化学习（MARL）可能需要适应外部自然语言指令，这些指令会中断正在进行的行为并与其长期目标冲突。然而，将奖励条件化于指令会引入根本性失效模式，因为贝尔曼更新会在不同指令上下文间耦合价值估计，导致指令中断宏操作时出现不一致的价值。我们提出宏操作价值校正指令合规性（MAVIC），通过校正进入指令目标并恢复当前目标下的延续价值，来在指令边界处修正贝尔曼备份。与奖励塑造不同，MAVIC直接修改引导目标本身，从而在统一策略下实现随机指令切换时的一致价值估计。我们提供了理论分析和演员-评论家实现，并表明MAVIC在日益复杂的协作多智能体环境中实现了高指令合规性，同时保持基础任务性能。
