Unlike reward shaping, MAVIC modifies the bootstrapping target directly, enabling consistent value estimation during stochastic instruction switching. Experiments demonstrate that MAVIC achieves high instruction compliance without sacrificing base task performance in increasingly complex cooperative multi-agent environments.
为解决协作多智能体强化学习中动态自然语言指令干扰下行为一致性的问题，本文提出MAVIC方法，在指令边界处修正贝尔曼更新，通过调整新指令目标并恢复当前目标下的延续价值。与奖励塑造不同，MAVIC直接修改回溯目标，从而在随机指令切换中保持价值估计一致性。实验表明，MAVIC在复杂协作环境中实现高指令合规性，同时不损害基础任务性能。
Enhancing Conformal Prediction via Class Similarity
Authors: Ariel Fargion, Lahav Dabah, Tom Tirer
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-11-24T17:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:04:21+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 (camera-ready). Code is available at: https://github.com/ariel361/CP_via_CS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conformal Prediction (CP) has emerged as a powerful statistical framework for high-stakes classification applications. Instead of predicting a single class, CP generates a prediction set, guaranteed to include the true label with a pre-specified probability. The performance of different CP methods is typically assessed by their average prediction set size. In setups where the classes can be partitioned into semantic groups, e.g., diseases that require similar treatment, users can benefit from prediction sets that are not only small on average, but also contain a small number of semantically different groups. This paper begins by addressing this problem and ultimately offers a widely applicable tool for boosting any CP method on any dataset. First, given a class partition, we propose augmenting the CP score function with a term that penalizes predictions with out-of-group errors. We theoretically analyze this strategy and prove its advantages for group-related metrics. Surprisingly, we show mathematically that, for common class partitions, it can also reduce the average set size of any CP score function. Our analysis reveals the class-similarity factors behind this improvement and motivates a variant that can further reduce prediction set size by leveraging the model's embeddings, without requiring any human semantic partition. Finally, we present an extensive empirical study, encompassing prominent CP methods, multiple models, and several datasets, which demonstrates that our class-similarity-based approach consistently enhances CP methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过类别相似性增强置信预测
置信预测（CP）已成为高风险分类应用的强大统计框架。CP不预测单一类别，而是生成一个预测集合，保证以预设概率包含真实标签。不同CP方法的性能通常通过平均预测集合大小评估。在类别可划分为语义组（如需相似治疗的疾病）的场景中，用户可获益于不仅平均集合较小，且包含较少语义不同组的预测集合。本文首先解决此问题，最终提供一种广泛适用的工具，可提升任何CP方法在任何数据集上的表现。首先，给定类别划分，我们提出在CP评分函数中加入惩罚跨组错误的项。我们对该策略进行理论分析并证明其对组相关指标的优势。令人惊讶的是，我们数学证明，对于常见类别划分，该方法还能减少任何CP评分函数的平均集合大小。我们的分析揭示了该改进背后的类别相似性因素，并启发了一种变体，该变体可进一步通过利用模型嵌入减少预测集合大小，而无需人工语义划分。最后，我们通过涵盖主流CP方法、多种模型和多个数据集的广泛实证研究，证明我们的基于类别相似性的方法始终能提升CP方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve conformal prediction (CP) performance in settings where classes can be grouped by semantic similarity, this paper proposes a method that modifies the CP score function to penalize predictions that misclassify instances into different semantic groups. Theoretically, the approach is shown to improve group-related metrics and, surprisingly, also reduces average prediction set size across common class partitions. The method leverages class similarity without requiring human-defined groupings, instead using model embeddings to identify group structure. Empirical results across multiple CP methods, models, and datasets confirm that this class-similarity augmentation consistently improves prediction set quality and size.
为提升在语义类群可划分场景下置信预测（CP）的性能，本文提出一种通过惩罚跨语义组误分类来修改CP得分函数的方法。理论分析表明，该方法不仅能优化组内相关指标，还能降低平均预测集大小。在多个CP方法、模型和数据集上的实验证明，该方法可稳定提升预测集质量，且无需人工划分类群；其改进版还可利用模型嵌入进一步缩小预测集，无需人工干预。
Socratic-SWE: Self-Evolving Coding Agents via Trace-Derived Agent Skills
Authors: Chuan Xiao, Zhengbo Jiao, Shaobo Wang, Wei Wang, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei, Linfeng Zhang, Lin Qu
First: 2026-06-05T16:00:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T16:00:17+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 5 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-driven software engineering agents have become a central testbed for real-world language-model capability, yet their training remains limited by the availability of high-quality SWE tasks. Existing synthetic data methods typically create tasks through fixed mutation or bug-injection procedures, making the resulting distributions largely independent of the agent's own weaknesses and training progress. We introduce Socratic-SWE, a closed-loop self-evolution framework that reuses the agent's historical solving traces as a source of training signal. Rather than treating traces only as evidence for reward computation, Socratic-SWE distills them into structured agent skills that summarize recurring failures and effective repair patterns. These skills then guide the generation of targeted repair tasks in real repositories. Candidate tasks are checked through execution-based validation and scored with a solver-gradient alignment reward, so that the retained tasks are both verifiable and useful for improving the Solver. The updated Solver produces new traces, enabling the task curriculum to adapt over successive rounds. Across SWE-bench Verified, SWE-bench Lite, SWE-bench Pro, and Terminal-Bench 2.0, Socratic-SWE consistently improves over self-evolving baselines under the same compute budget, reaching 50.40% on SWE-bench Verified after three iterations. These results suggest that solving traces can serve as a scalable substrate for self-evolving SWE agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：苏格拉底式软件工程代理：通过轨迹衍生的代理技能实现自演化
由大语言模型驱动的软件工程代理已成为评估真实世界语言模型能力的核心测试平台，但其训练仍受限于高质量软件工程任务的可用性。现有合成数据方法通常通过固定变异或注入缺陷的方式生成任务，导致生成的任务分布与代理自身的弱点和训练进展基本无关。我们提出了苏格拉底式软件工程代理（Socratic-SWE），这是一种闭环自演化框架，利用代理的历史解题轨迹作为训练信号来源。Socratic-SWE不仅将轨迹视为奖励计算的证据，更将其提炼为结构化的代理技能，总结重复性失败和有效的修复模式。这些技能指导在真实仓库中生成针对性修复任务。候选任务通过执行验证并使用求解器梯度对齐奖励评分，确保保留的任务既可验证又对提升求解器有效。更新后的求解器生成新轨迹，使任务课程在多轮迭代中持续自适应。在SWE-bench Verified、SWE-bench Lite、SWE-bench Pro和Terminal-Bench 2.0上，Socratic-SWE在相同计算预算下持续优于自演化基线，在三轮迭代后于SWE-bench Verified上达到50.40%。这些结果表明，解题轨迹可作为自演化软件工程代理的可扩展基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality software engineering tasks for training LLM agents, this work introduces Socratic-SWE, a closed-loop self-evolution framework that leverages historical solving traces to generate targeted repair tasks. Instead of using traces merely for reward signals, it distills them into structured agent skills that capture recurring failures and effective fixes, which then guide the creation of new tasks in real repositories. These tasks are validated via execution and scored using a solver-gradient alignment metric to ensure verifiability and usefulness. The resulting curriculum adapts iteratively as the agent improves, leading to consistent performance gains across multiple benchmarks—reaching 50.40% on SWE-bench Verified after three iterations, outperforming existing self-evolving baselines under the same compute budget.
为解决高质量软件工程任务稀缺的问题，Socratic-SWE提出一种闭环自进化框架，利用代理自身的历史解题轨迹生成针对性强、随训练动态调整的任务。该方法不依赖固定变异或注入缺陷的合成数据，而是从轨迹中提炼出失败模式与修复策略，转化为结构化代理技能，指导在真实仓库中生成新修复任务。任务通过执行验证并依据求解器梯度对齐奖励评分，确保可验证性和改进有效性。在多个基准测试中，该方法在固定算力下持续优于基线，三轮迭代后在SWE-bench Verified上达到50.40%，表明代理轨迹可作为自进化软件代理的可扩展训练基础。
A Comprehensive Anatomy of Human and DeepSeek-R1 LLM Mathematical Reasoning
Authors: Yuxiang Chen, Jun Wang
First: 2026-06-05T15:57:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:57:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The emergence of "Aha moments" in large language models, particularly DeepSeek-R1-0120, has raised the question of whether these systems genuinely reason or merely imitate the appearance of reasoning. We conduct a comprehensive empirical comparison between model and human reasoning across all 30 problems from AIME 2025, exhaustively annotating 10,247 reasoning steps into five functional categories: Analysis, Inference, Branch, Backtrace, and Reflection. We find a clear structural difference. Human solutions maintain a compact alternation between analysis and deduction, whereas DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits intermediate results, performs shallow and often unnecessary verification, and loops through local checks without meaningful logical progress. We describe this as topological mimicry: reproducing the surface form of reasoning without its functional role. Despite this, we identify two signals of genuine reasoning. First, successful traces exhibit stable use of branching and backtracking, while failed traces either underuse or overuse exploratory actions. Second, reflection is only effective when placed within deductive inference; reflections trapped in analysis loops focus on local numerical details while missing global logical errors. These findings suggest that current long-CoT models may be rewarded more for the appearance of reasoning than for genuine deductive progress. We discuss directions for improving evaluation and training, including measuring cross-trace stability, penalising "spinning-wheel" traces, encouraging deeper logical correction, and reallocating inference-time compute toward deduction and backtracking. Overall, reasoning quality depends not simply on how much reflection occurs, but on whether reflection appears consistently and at the appropriate logical scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人类与DeepSeek-R1大语言模型数学推理的全面解剖
大型语言模型中出现的“顿悟时刻”，特别是DeepSeek-R1-0120，引发了对其是否真正进行推理，还是仅模仿推理表象的疑问。我们对AIME 2025全部30道题目的模型与人类推理进行了全面实证比较，将10,247个推理步骤细分为五类功能：分析、推断、分支、回溯与反思。我们发现二者结构差异明显：人类解法保持分析与推断的紧凑交替，而DeepSeek-R1常反复检查中间结果，进行浅层且常不必要的验证，并在局部检查中循环，缺乏实质性逻辑进展。我们称之为拓扑模仿：复制推理的表象而无其功能作用。尽管如此，我们识别出两种真实推理的信号：其一，成功轨迹稳定使用分支与回溯，失败轨迹则或过度或不足使用探索性动作；其二，反思仅在推断过程中有效，若陷入分析循环则仅关注局部数值细节，忽略全局逻辑错误。这些发现表明，当前长链推理模型可能更因推理表象而获奖励，而非真实推导进展。我们讨论了改进评估与训练的方向，包括测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚“空转”轨迹、鼓励深层逻辑修正、将推理时计算资源向推导与回溯倾斜。总体而言，推理质量不取决于反思频率，而取决于反思是否在恰当逻辑尺度上稳定出现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether large language models like DeepSeek-R1 genuinely reason or merely mimic reasoning, this study empirically compares human and model reasoning across 30 AIME 2025 problems, annotating 10,247 steps into five functional categories. Results reveal that human reasoning follows a compact alternation between analysis and inference, while DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits steps, performs shallow verification, and loops without meaningful progress — termed 'topological mimicry'. Two indicators of genuine reasoning were identified: stable use of branching and backtracking in successful traces, and reflection only being effective when embedded in inference, not analysis. These findings suggest current models may be rewarded for superficial reasoning patterns rather than logical depth, pointing to the need for evaluation methods that penalize redundant loops and prioritize deductive correction.
为探究大语言模型（如DeepSeek-R1）是否真正进行推理或仅模仿推理表面，本研究对30道AIME 2025题目中人类与模型的推理过程进行实证对比，标注10,247步推理步骤至五大功能类别。结果表明，人类推理呈现分析与推断交替的紧凑结构，而DeepSeek-R1常循环验证中间结果、缺乏实质逻辑进展，属于拓扑模仿。真正推理的两个信号是：成功推理路径中分支与回溯使用稳定，失败路径则要么不足要么过度；反思仅在推断环节有效，若陷于分析循环则聚焦局部数值而忽略全局逻辑错误。研究指出当前长链推理模型可能因表面推理表现获益，建议改进评估与训练，如惩罚冗余循环、强化逻辑修正、将推理计算资源向推断与回溯倾斜，强调推理质量取决于反思是否恰当地嵌入逻辑层级而非单纯数量。
Learning to Execute Graph Algorithms Exactly with Graph Neural Networks
Authors: Muhammad Fetrat Qharabagh, Artur Back de Luca, George Giapitzakis, Kimon Fountoulakis
First: 2026-01-30T17:31:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:55:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding what graph neural networks can learn, especially their ability to learn to execute algorithms, remains a central theoretical challenge. In this work, we prove exact learnability results for graph algorithms under bounded-degree and finite-precision constraints. Our approach follows a two-step process. First, we train an ensemble of multi-layer perceptrons (MLPs) to execute the local instructions of a single node. Second, during inference, we use the trained MLP ensemble as the update function within a graph neural network (GNN). Leveraging Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory, we show that local instructions can be learned from a small training set, enabling the complete graph algorithm to be executed during inference without error and with high probability. To illustrate the learning power of our setting, we establish a rigorous learnability result for the LOCAL model of distributed computation. We further demonstrate positive learnability results for widely studied algorithms such as message flooding, breadth-first and depth-first search, and Bellman-Ford.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用图神经网络精确执行图算法的学习
理解图神经网络能够学习什么，特别是其学习执行算法的能力，仍是一个核心的理论挑战。在本研究中，我们在度数有界和精度有限的约束下，证明了图算法的精确可学习性。我们的方法采用两步流程：首先，训练多层感知机（MLP）集合以执行单个节点的局部指令；其次，在推理阶段，将训练好的MLP集合作为图神经网络（GNN）中的更新函数。借助神经切线核（NTK）理论，我们表明局部指令可从少量训练样本中学习，从而在推理阶段以高概率无误差地执行完整图算法。为展示我们设置的学习能力，我们为分布式计算的LOCAL模型建立了严格的可学习性结果，并进一步证明了消息泛洪、广度优先搜索、深度优先搜索和Bellman-Ford等广泛研究的算法具有正向可学习性。

This paper addresses the theoretical challenge of whether graph neural networks can exactly learn to execute graph algorithms. Using Neural Tangent Kernel theory, they prove that under bounded-degree and finite-precision constraints, these local updates can be learned from small datasets, enabling exact execution of graph algorithms during inference with high probability. Experimental results confirm learnability for key algorithms including message flooding, breadth-first search, depth-first search, and Bellman-Ford under the LOCAL model of distributed computation.
本文旨在解决图神经网络能否在度数有界和精度有限条件下精确执行图算法的理论难题。作者提出两步方法：首先训练多层感知机集合以执行单节点局部更新，然后在推理阶段将这些MLP作为GNN的更新函数。借助神经切线核理论，证明局部指令可从少量数据中学习，从而在推理阶段以高概率精确执行完整图算法。实验结果表明，该框架对LOCAL模型具有可学习性，并对消息传播、广度优先搜索、深度优先搜索和Bellman-Ford等经典算法均表现出正向可学习性。
Reason-Then-Retrieve for CoVR-R with Structured Edit Prompts and Dense-Sparse Fusion
Authors: DongQing Liu, MengShi Qi, HongWei Ji
First: 2026-06-01T16:21:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:55:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
CoVR-R studies reason-aware composed video retrieval: given a reference video and an edit instruction, the system must retrieve the target video that satisfies the edit. The main difficulty is that the target is not described directly; it must be inferred from fine-grained changes in object identity, action order, final state, hand interaction, and scene transition. We build a zero-shot reason-then-retrieve pipeline around Qwen3.5-27B. For each gallery video, the model generates a retrieval-oriented structured description and a dense embedding by pooling generated-token hidden states with token-dependent weights. For each query, the model first performs edit reasoning over the reference video and instruction, then generates a target-video description whose hidden states serve as the query embedding. We complement dense retrieval with a TF-IDF branch over the generated texts and fuse the two rankings with split-specific weights. On validation, the current best submission reaches 80.81 at R@1, 94.86 at R@5, 97.11 at R@10, and 98.59 at R@50. On the blind test split, it reaches 89.73 at R@1, 95.79 at R@5, 96.63 at R@10, and 97.98 at R@50.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于结构化编辑提示和稠密-稀疏融合的CoVR-R推理后检索方法
CoVR-R研究具有推理意识的组合视频检索：给定参考视频和编辑指令，系统需检索满足编辑要求的目标视频。主要难点在于目标未直接描述，需从物体身份、动作顺序、最终状态、手部交互及场景转换等细粒度变化中推断。我们围绕Qwen3.5-27B构建零样本推理后检索流程。对每个画廊视频，模型生成面向检索的结构化描述及稠密嵌入，通过聚合生成token的隐藏状态并加权。对每个查询，模型先对参考视频和指令进行编辑推理，再生成目标视频描述，其隐藏状态作为查询嵌入。我们以生成文本的TF-IDF分支补充稠密检索，并以分组特定权重融合两个排序结果。在验证集上，当前最佳提交结果为R@1:80.81，R@5:94.86，R@10:97.11，R@50:98.59；在盲测集上，结果为R@1:89.73，R@5:95.79，R@10:96.63，R@50:97.98。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of retrieving target videos that satisfy complex edit instructions without direct descriptions, the paper proposes a zero-shot reason-then-retrieve pipeline using Qwen3.5-27B. The method first generates structured descriptions and dense embeddings for gallery videos, then reasons over reference videos and edit prompts to produce query embeddings from target-video descriptions. It fuses dense retrieval with TF-IDF-based sparse retrieval using split-specific weights. Experimental results show strong performance: 80.81 at R@1 and 98.59 at R@50 on validation, and 89.73 at R@1 and 97.98 at R@50 on blind test.
为解决在无直接描述情况下根据编辑指令检索目标视频的难题，该论文提出了一种基于Qwen3.5-27B的零样本“先推理后检索”框架。方法首先为图库视频生成结构化描述与稠密嵌入（通过加权池化生成token隐藏状态），再对参考视频和编辑指令进行推理，生成目标视频描述，其隐藏状态作为查询嵌入。为提升检索效果，结合TF-IDF稀疏检索与稠密检索，采用分组特定权重融合。实验结果显示：验证集上R@1至R@50分别为80.81、94.86、97.11、98.59；盲测集上分别为89.73、95.79、96.63、97.98。
Mining Useful General Data for Low-Resource Domain Adaptation
Authors: Pingjie Wang, Hongcheng Liu, Yusheng Liao, Ziqing Fan, Yaxin Du, Shuo Tang, Yanfeng Wang, Yu Wang
First: 2025-11-10T18:41:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:54:20+00:00
Comments: 39 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Adapting large language models (LLMs) to low-resource domains remains challenging due to the scarcity of domain-specific data. While in-domain data is limited, there exists a vast amount of general-domain data that shares similar question-answer formats and reasoning patterns with domain tasks. This observation raises an important question: can useful general-domain data be mined to improve low-resource domain adaptation? Our initial findings show that general-domain chain-of-thought data contains useful auxiliary signals for domain adaptation, even without careful selection. This observation motivates a new paradigm for domain adaptation beyond exclusive reliance on domain-specific data. To systematically identify the most beneficial general-domain samples, we propose NTK-Selector, motivated by the Neural Tangent Kernel's ability to capture alignment in training dynamics. Since directly applying NTK to pretrained LLMs is impractical, we introduce a Jacobian-free NTK approximation and empirically demonstrate stable NTK-like behavior during fine-tuning. Extensive experiments across medical, financial, legal, and psychological domains demonstrate that NTK-Selector consistently outperforms domain-only fine-tuning and existing data selection baselines. In particular, NTK-Selector achieves gains of +8.7 and +5.1 points on Llama3-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B, respectively, compared to only +0.8 and +0.9 points from domain-only fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于低资源领域自适应的有用通用数据挖掘
由于领域特定数据稀缺，将大型语言模型（LLM）适配到低资源领域仍具挑战性。尽管领域内数据有限，但大量通用领域数据具有与领域任务相似的问题-答案格式和推理模式。这一观察引发一个重要问题：能否挖掘有用通用领域数据以提升低资源领域自适应？我们的初步发现表明，即使无需精心筛选，通用领域思维链数据也包含对领域自适应有用的辅助信号。这一观察推动了超越仅依赖领域特定数据的新领域自适应范式。为系统性识别最有益的通用领域样本，我们提出NTK-Selector，其灵感来源于神经切线核（NTK）捕捉训练动态对齐的能力。由于直接将NTK应用于预训练LLM不切实际，我们引入了无雅可比矩阵的NTK近似方法，并实证证明其在微调过程中表现出稳定的NTK类行为。在医学、金融、法律和心理学等多个领域广泛实验表明，NTK-Selector持续优于仅使用领域数据的微调方法及现有数据选择基线。特别是，与仅使用领域数据微调相比，NTK-Selector在Llama3-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上分别提升了8.7和5.1个点，而仅使用领域数据微调仅提升0.8和0.9个点。

为了解决低资源领域中领域特定数据稀缺的问题，该研究探索了从通用领域数据中挖掘有益信号以提升领域自适应的可能性。提出NTK-Selector方法，利用无雅可比矩阵的神经切线核近似来识别最有益的通用样本用于微调。在医学、金融、法律和心理学等多个领域实验表明，NTK-Selector显著优于仅使用领域数据微调及现有基线方法，分别在Llama3-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上取得+8.7和+5.1的性能提升，而仅使用领域数据微调仅提升+0.8和+0.9。
An Algebraic View of the Expressivity of Recurrent Language Models
Authors: Franz Nowak, Ryan Cotterell, Reda Boumasmoud
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-01T06:47:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:53:13+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 2 figures, to be published at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
What formal languages can a recurrent neural language model recognize? Formal results in the literature conflict: some authors report Turing-completeness, while others show equivalence to regular languages. The reason for this discrepancy is that the underlying arithmetic model differs. The paper develops a unified algebraic account of the expressivity of recurrent neural networks, starting with a formal account of various arithmetic models. This account reduces expressivity to an algebraic question, e.g., whether a network's syntactic monoid divides a certain wreath product. As a case study, the paper revisits diagonal state-space models: the same architecture cannot implement an even-modulus counter once floating-point recurrences are enforced, yet realizes every even-modulus counter under unsigned-integer quantization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：循环语言模型表达能力的代数视角
循环神经网络语言模型能识别哪些形式语言？文献中的形式结果存在矛盾：一些作者报告其具有图灵完备性，而另一些则证明其等价于正则语言。这种差异的原因在于底层的算术模型不同。本文从形式化描述各种算术模型出发，建立统一的代数框架来刻画循环神经网络的表达能力，将表达能力问题归约为代数问题，例如网络的语法幺半群是否能整除某个缠积。作为案例研究，本文重新审视对角状态空间模型：当强制使用浮点递归时，同一架构无法实现模偶数计数器，但在无符号整数量化下可实现所有模偶数计数器。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the discrepancy in literature regarding what formal languages recurrent neural language models can recognize, by proposing an algebraic framework that unifies different arithmetic models. It reduces the expressivity question to algebraic properties, such as whether a network’s syntactic monoid divides a specific wreath product. As a case study, it demonstrates that diagonal state-space models can implement all even-modulus counters under unsigned-integer quantization but fail to do so when floating-point recurrences are enforced, highlighting the critical role of numerical representation in computational power.
本文旨在解决文献中关于循环神经网络语言模型是否具备图灵完备能力或仅限于正则语言的争议，提出一个统一的代数框架。该框架通过语法幺半群与缠积结构的关系，将模型表达能力转化为代数可除性问题。以对角状态空间模型为例，研究发现：在无符号整数量化下，该架构可实现所有偶模计数器；但在浮点递归约束下则无法实现，凸显了算术表示对计算能力的关键影响。
$\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$: Semantic Residual Effective Contrastive Information for Evaluating Hard Negatives
Authors: Aarush Sinha, Rahul Seetharaman, Aman Bansal
First: 2026-03-22T00:21:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:52:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hard-negative source selection for dense retrieval is usually decided only after fine-tuning and downstream evaluation. We propose $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$, a semantic residual variant of Effective Contrastive Information (ECI) that ranks candidate negative sources using frozen target-encoder embeddings. $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$ is training-free, not label-free: each scored example requires a query, a labeled positive, and an explicit candidate negative. $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$ builds a weighted residual information matrix from target consistency, semantic locality, lexical residuality, and a log-determinant diversity objective. On MS MARCO negative sources, in-family $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$ ranks LLM negatives highest among non-hybrid sources and Dense+LLM highest among hybrid sources, matching the strongest aggregate BEIR transfer results across DistilBERT, E5-base, and Contriever. Controlled ablations show that this alignment depends on using the target encoder family, while additional ablations show stability under sample-size, temperature, tokenizer, and IDF-corpus perturbations. The theory gives a local linearized link to loss reduction, while the empirical study treats downstream evaluation as the final test.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ECI_sem：用于评估难负样本的语义残差有效对比信息
密集检索中的难负样本源选择通常仅在微调和下游评估后决定。我们提出ECI_sem，即有效对比信息（ECI）的语义残差变体，利用冻结的目标编码器嵌入对候选负样本源进行排序。ECI_sem无需训练，也不依赖标签：每个评分示例需一个查询、一个标注正样本和一个显式候选负样本。ECI_sem基于目标一致性、语义局部性、词汇残差性和对数行列式多样性目标构建加权残差信息矩阵。在MS MARCO负样本源上，同族ECI_sem在非混合源中对LLM负样本排序最高，在混合源中对Dense+LLM排序最高，与DistilBERT、E5-base和Contriever在BEIR上的最强聚合迁移结果一致。受控消融实验表明，该对齐依赖于使用目标编码器族；附加消融实验显示其在样本量、温度、分词器和IDF语料扰动下具有稳定性。理论部分建立其与损失降低的局部线性关联，而实证研究将下游评估视为最终测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate hard negative sources without fine-tuning, the paper introduces $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$, a training-free, label-free semantic residual method that ranks candidate negatives using frozen target-encoder embeddings. It constructs a weighted residual information matrix incorporating target consistency, semantic locality, lexical residuality, and a log-determinant diversity term. Experiments on MS MARCO show $\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$ consistently ranks LLM-generated negatives highest among non-hybrid sources and Dense+LLM highest among hybrid sources, aligning with top BEIR transfer results across multiple encoders. Ablations confirm robustness to sample size, temperature, tokenizer, and IDF corpus variations, and the method’s theoretical grounding links to local loss reduction.
为在无需微调的情况下评估难负样本，本文提出$\mathrm{ECI}_{\mathrm{sem}}$方法，该方法基于冻结的目标编码器嵌入对候选负样本进行排序。其构建加权残差信息矩阵，融合目标一致性、语义局部性、词法残差性及对数行列式多样性目标。在MS MARCO数据集上，该方法在非混合源中优先排序LLM负样本，在混合源中优先排序Dense+LLM组合，与DistilBERT、E5-base和Contriever等模型在BEIR迁移评估中的最优结果一致。消融实验表明其对样本量、温度、分词器及IDF语料扰动具有稳定性，理论层面通过局部线性化与损失降低建立关联。
Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling
Authors: Rohan Shravan
First: 2026-06-05T15:48:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:48:42+00:00
Comments: 58 pages, 9 figures, 37 tables. Code: https://github.com/The-School-of-AI/LLM. Released models: huggingface.co/theschoolofai/LightningLM-0.1V-{2B, 5B-MoE, 9B-MoE, 120B-MoE}. Companion work: arXiv:2605.29379 (BrahmicTokenizer-131K), arXiv:2605.29459 (Kronecker Embeddings)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale. This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45. What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可逆基础：通过状态保持缩放训练1200亿参数稀疏MoE模型
本文报告了在单个八GPU节点上端到端训练一个百亿参数稀疏专家混合模型。LightningLM 0.1V是一个递归骨干语言模型家族，从一个小的密集种子模型开始，经过5B和9B专家混合模型，最终成长为一个在top-12路由下拥有460个路由专家的120B模型。每个更大的模型均从较小模型的训练权重中生长而来；活跃参数从密集种子的1.78B单调增长至120B时的5.93B（约占存储的118.67B的5%）。整个谱系均在单节点上运行，较大阶段支持8K上下文，120B规模下发布的训练损失为1.78。 本文是一份系统与经验报告，围绕三个领域组织：可逆性：可逆递归堆栈在反向传播中重建激活值而非存储，随着模型增长保持激活内存平坦。状态保持增长：每次扩展（密集到MoE、浅层到深层、少数专家到众多专家）均以可复现原则配对其错误操作导致的失败；部分失败是静默的。单节点经济性：120B模型通过TQP策略训练，该策略采用量化基础专家权重和训练低秩适配器，仅需2.26B适配器参数而非100B+驻留于路由专家中的参数，将专家路径优化器状态减少约45倍。 新颖之处在于整合已知基本构件，而非孤立的任何构件：一个在单节点上端到端运行的生长谱系，以实践者级别文档化，并以各领域验证损失为证据，证明目标能力（多语言印度语能力、代码）通过构建获得。模型家族、分词器和训练代码均已发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of scaling sparse mixture-of-experts models without prohibitive hardware requirements, this paper introduces a state-preserving, reversible training pipeline that grows a 120B-parameter MoE model from a small dense seed on a single eight-GPU node. The method leverages reversible recurrence to avoid storing activations, and employs quantized expert weights with low-rank adapters to drastically reduce optimizer state, cutting expert-path memory by ~45x. Experimental results show the model achieves a training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale, with held-out losses confirming targeted capabilities like multilingual Indic and code proficiency, all while maintaining single-node training efficiency and releasing full code and model weights.
为高效训练超大规模稀疏模型，该研究提出了一种在单节点上端到端训练1200亿参数稀疏混合专家模型的方法，从小型密集模型逐步扩展。方法采用可逆递归结构避免反向传播中存储激活值，并通过状态保持式增长，使每阶段模型直接基于前一阶段权重训练。同时引入量化专家权重与低秩适配器，将优化器状态减少约45倍，实现单8卡节点训练。实验表明，模型在120B规模下训练损失达1.78，且在印地语多语言和代码等任务上通过验证损失证明了能力的定向学习，活跃参数从1.78B单调增长至5.93B。
Robustly estimating heterogeneity in factorial data using Rashomon Partitions
Authors: Aparajithan Venkateswaran, Anirudh Sankar, Arun G. Chandrasekhar, Tyler H. McCormick
First: 2024-04-02T17:53:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:48:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In both observational data and randomized control trials, researchers select statistical models to articulate how the outcome of interest varies with combinations of observable covariates. Choosing a model that is too simple can obfuscate important heterogeneity in outcomes between covariate groups, while too much complexity risks identifying spurious patterns. In this paper, we propose a novel Bayesian framework for model uncertainty called Rashomon Partition Sets (RPSs). The RPS consists of all models that have posterior density close to the maximum a posteriori (MAP) model. We construct the RPS by enumeration, rather than sampling, which ensures that we explore all models with high evidence in the data, even if they offer dramatically different substantive explanations. We use a l0 prior, which allows the allows us to capture complex heterogeneity without imposing strong assumptions about the associations between effects, showing this prior is minimax optimal from an information-theoretic perspective. We characterize the approximation error of (functions of) parameters computed conditional on being in the RPS relative to the entire posterior. We propose an algorithm to enumerate the RPS from the class of models that are interpretable and unique, then provide bounds on the size of the RPS. We give simulation evidence along with three empirical examples: price effects on charitable giving, heterogeneity in chromosomal structure, and the introduction of microfinance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用Rashomon划分稳健估计因子数据中的异质性
在观察性数据和随机对照试验中，研究人员选择统计模型以说明感兴趣的结局如何随可观测协变量的组合而变化。模型过于简单会掩盖协变量组间重要的结局异质性，而过于复杂则可能识别出虚假模式。本文提出了一种名为Rashomon分区集（RPS）的新型贝叶斯模型不确定性框架。RPS包含所有后验密度接近最大后验概率（MAP）模型的模型。我们通过枚举而非采样构建RPS，确保探索所有数据中证据高的模型，即使它们提供截然不同的实质解释。我们采用l0先验，能够在不强加效应间关联假设的情况下捕捉复杂异质性，并从信息论角度证明该先验是最小最大最优的。我们刻画了在RPS条件下计算的（参数的）函数相对于整个后验的近似误差。我们提出了一种算法，从可解释且唯一的模型类中枚举RPS，并给出RPS大小的界。我们提供了模拟证据及三个实证示例：价格对慈善捐赠的影响、染色体结构异质性，以及微型金融的引入。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of modeling outcome heterogeneity without overfitting in factorial data, this paper introduces Rashomon Partition Sets (RPSs), a Bayesian framework that identifies all models with posterior density near the maximum a posteriori (MAP) model. Using a l0 prior, the method enables flexible modeling of complex heterogeneity without assuming specific covariate interactions, and is shown to be minimax optimal. The RPS is constructed via enumeration rather than sampling to ensure comprehensive coverage of high-evidence models, even those with divergent substantive interpretations. Experimental results across simulations and three empirical applications—charitable giving, chromosomal structure, and microfinance—demonstrate that RPSs effectively capture meaningful heterogeneity while bounding model uncertainty and approximation error.
为解决在因子数据中平衡模型简洁性与复杂性以准确捕捉异质性的挑战，本文提出Rashomon分区集合（RPSs）这一贝叶斯框架，用于识别所有后验密度接近最大后验（MAP）模型的模型。通过使用l0先验，在不强加效应间关联假设的前提下实现复杂异质性的建模，该方法采用枚举而非采样方式，确保全面覆盖高证据的替代模型。在模拟和三个实际案例——慈善捐赠、染色体结构异质性及微型金融引入——中，实验结果表明RPSs能有效揭示多样但统计上支持的异质性解释，并提供RPS规模的理论界和参数估计的近似误差控制。
The Proxy Benders Decomposition
Authors: Changkun Guan, El Mehdi Er Raqabi, Mathieu Tanneau, Pascal Van Hentenryck
First: 2026-06-05T15:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:47:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Benders decomposition is a fundamental framework for solving large-scale mixed-integer optimization problems with complicating variables that, when fixed, yield significantly easier subproblems. However, classical Benders decomposition repeatedly solves highly similar subproblems and often exhibits zigzagging behavior across iterations, leading to slow convergence in large-scale settings. Motivated by the repetitive structure and parametric nature of Benders subproblems, this paper introduces the proxy Benders decomposition (Proxy-BD), a new decomposition framework in which subproblem optimization is replaced by certified optimization proxies rather than repeated exact solves. The proposed proxy follows a self-supervised predict-project-and-complete mechanism that produces dual-feasible solutions for generating provably valid Benders cuts. The framework preserves the theoretical validity of the decomposition independently of prediction quality through a projection-and-completion certification layer. A formal characterization of proxy-induced cuts is established, and the framework naturally extends to modern decomposition schemes, including branch-and-Benders-cut algorithms. Computational experiments on large-scale facility location and network design problems demonstrate that Proxy-BD substantially reduces the computational effort of subproblems while maintaining near-optimal solution quality. On large-scale uncapacitated facility location instances up to 2000x2000, Proxy-BD achieves median optimality gaps below 0.5%, yields up to 161x median speedups, and reduces the number of generated cuts by more than 240x on the largest instances. The computational gains consistently increase with recourse complexity, indicating that proxy-based inference scales substantially more favorably than repeated exact subproblem optimization in large-scale decomposition settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理Benders分解
Benders分解是求解具有复杂变量的大规模混合整数优化问题的基础框架，当这些变量固定时，子问题会显著简化。然而，经典Benders分解会反复求解高度相似的子问题，且常在迭代中出现锯齿状行为，导致大规模问题收敛缓慢。受Benders子问题重复结构和参数化特性的启发，本文提出了代理Benders分解（Proxy-BD），一种新的分解框架，用经过认证的优化代理替代重复精确求解子问题。 所提出的代理采用自监督的预测-投影-完成机制，生成可证明有效的Benders割。该框架通过投影-完成认证层，独立于预测质量保持分解的理论有效性。已建立代理诱导割的正式刻画，且该框架自然扩展至现代分解方案，包括分支与Benders切割算法。 在大规模设施选址与网络设计问题上的计算实验表明，Proxy-BD显著减少了子问题的计算量，同时保持近最优解质量。在规模达2000x2000的无容量设施选址实例中，Proxy-BD实现中位最优间隙低于0.5%，最高达161倍中位加速，最大实例生成割数减少超过240倍。计算增益随 recourse 复杂度增加而持续提升，表明代理推理在大规模分解设置中比重复精确子问题求解更具显著的可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of classical Benders decomposition due to repetitive and zigzagging subproblem solves, this paper proposes Proxy-BD, a framework that replaces exact subproblem optimization with certified optimization proxies. These proxies use a self-supervised predict-project-and-complete mechanism to generate dual-feasible solutions that produce provably valid Benders cuts, while a certification layer ensures theoretical validity regardless of prediction accuracy. Experimental results on large-scale facility location and network design problems show Proxy-BD reduces subproblem computational effort substantially while preserving near-optimal solution quality: on 2000x2000 instances, it achieves median optimality gaps below 0.5%, up to 161x speedups, and over 240x fewer cuts, with gains increasing with recourse complexity.
为了解决经典Benders分解因重复求解相似子问题和收敛震荡导致效率低下的问题，本文提出代理Benders分解（Proxy-BD），用经过认证的优化代理替代精确子问题求解。该代理采用自监督的预测-投影-补全机制生成对偶可行解以产生可证明有效的Benders割，其认证层确保理论有效性不依赖预测精度。在大规模设施选址与网络设计问题上的实验表明，Proxy-BD可将子问题计算量降低161倍中位数加速，割生成量减少240倍以上，且在2000x2000实例上最优间隙低于0.5%，其计算优势随再规划复杂度增加而显著提升。
M$^3$Exam: Benchmarking Multimodal Memory for Realistic User-Agent Interactions
Authors: Zhengjun Huang, Wenxuan Liu, Zhoujin Tian, Wei Chen, Junle Chen, Yuqian Wu, Fangyuan Zhang, Qintian Guo, Xiaofang Zhou
First: 2026-06-05T15:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:44:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language agents are increasingly deployed over accumulating multimodal information, yet existing benchmarks assume a human-human form with sparse visuals and straightforward content, evaluating neither reasoning over authentic multimodal file interaction nor the interpretation of concealed user information. We therefore introduce M$^3$Exam, a query-centric multimodal conversational memory benchmark built on realistic user-agent interaction, with multi-dimensional evaluation spanning cross-modal grounding and implicit information inference. Benchmarking MLLMs and memory systems reveals persistent gaps in cross-modal grounding, cross session reasoning, and the efficiency cost of accumulating multimodal context. We further propose M$^3$Proctor, a multimodal memory method that detects query modality bias and consumes raw visual sources only on demand, improving accuracy by 13% while cutting index-construction time and retrieved tokens by over 70%.

Motivated by the gap between real-world user-agent interactions and current multimodal benchmarks, the paper introduces M$^3$Exam, a query-centric benchmark that evaluates multimodal memory systems under realistic conditions involving complex visual and textual inputs. 为填补现有多模态基准与真实用户代理交互之间的差距，本文提出M$^3$Exam，一个以查询为中心的多模态对话记忆基准，评估系统在复杂视觉与文本数据下的跨模态对齐与隐含信息推理能力。实验表明，所提出的M$^3$Proctor方法通过检测模态偏倚并按需处理视觉输入，准确率提升13%，同时索引构建时间和检索token量减少超70%。
Breaking the Tuning Barrier: Zero-Hyperparameters Yield Multi-Corner Analysis Via Learned Priors
Authors: Wei W. Xing, Kaiqi Huang, Jiazhan Liu, Hong Qiu, Shan Shen
First: 2026-03-13T15:40:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:43:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted by DAC2026. Camera-ready Version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Yield Multi-Corner Analysis validates circuits across 25+ Process-Voltage-Temperature corners, resulting in a combinatorial simulation cost of $O(K \times N)$ where $K$ denotes corners and $N$ exceeds $10^4$ samples per corner. Existing methods face a fundamental trade-off: simple models achieve automation but fail on nonlinear circuits, while advanced AI models capture complex behaviors but require hours of hyperparameter tuning per design iteration, forming the Tuning Barrier. We break this barrier by replacing engineered priors (i.e., model specifications) with learned priors from a foundation model pre-trained on millions of regression tasks. This model performs in-context learning, instantly adapting to each circuit without tuning or retraining. Its attention mechanism automatically transfers knowledge across corners by identifying shared circuit physics between operating conditions. Combined with an automated feature selector (1152D to 48D), our method matches state-of-the-art accuracy (mean MREs as low as 0.11%) with zero tuning, reducing total validation cost by over $10\times$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：突破调参瓶颈：零超参数通过学习先验实现多角分析
多角分析可在25+个工艺-电压-温度角下验证电路，组合仿真成本为O(K×N)，其中K为角数，N每角超过10^4个样本。现有方法面临根本性权衡：简单模型虽可自动化但无法处理非线性电路，而先进AI模型虽能捕捉复杂行为，却需每设计迭代数小时调参，形成调参瓶颈。我们通过用预训练于百万回归任务的基础模型所学习的先验替代人工设计先验，实现上下文学习，无需调参或再训练即可即时适配每个电路。其注意力机制自动通过识别不同工作条件下的电路物理共性，在角间迁移知识。结合自动化特征选择器（1152D→48D），本方法在零调参下达到最先进精度（平均MRE低至0.11%），总验证成本降低10倍以上。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost and tuning burden of validating circuits across multiple Process-Voltage-Temperature corners, this work proposes a zero-hyperparameter method that replaces handcrafted priors with learned priors from a foundation model pre-trained on millions of regression tasks. The model leverages in-context learning to adapt instantly to new circuits without tuning, and its attention mechanism automatically transfers knowledge across corners by identifying shared circuit physics. Paired with an automated feature selector that reduces dimensionality from 1152D to 48D, the method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy (mean MREs as low as 0.11%) while eliminating hyperparameter tuning, reducing total validation cost by over tenfold.
为解决电路在多工艺-电压-温度角下验证时计算成本高、调参负担重的问题，该研究提出一种零超参数方法，用从百万级回归任务预训练的模型学习先验替代人工设计先验。该模型通过上下文学习和注意力机制自动跨角迁移电路物理知识，并结合自动特征选择器将维度从1152D压缩至48D。实验表明，该方法无需调参即可达到0.11%以下的平均相对误差，验证总成本降低十倍以上。
RealDocBench: A Benchmark for Field-Level QA and Layout Understanding on Real-World Regulated Documents
Authors: Ameya Joshi, Joon Kim, Gus Eggert, Joseph Bajor, Cindy Hao, Jing Reyhan, Kushal Byatnal, Eli Badgio
First: 2026-06-05T15:41:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:41:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Document parsing systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes, regulated workflows such as mortgage underwriting, financial reporting, supply-chain logistics, and clinical records. Yet most public benchmarks evaluate parsers on clean academic layouts or synthetic prose, and report a single OCR or markdown-level similarity score. Such documents and metrics correlate poorly with what downstream agents actually need: the correct value for a specific field on a messy real-world page. We introduce RealDocBench, a two-track benchmark built from real regulated documents. The QA track contains 1,356 field-level questions over 581 documents spanning four domains, where each question is paired with a typed gold_dict of key-to-value answers and parsers are scored on both per-field and strict per-question accuracy. The layout track contains 1,500 human-verified page images annotated with COCO-style bounding boxes under a nine-class public taxonomy, scored with a Hungarian matcher that includes adjacency-aware split/merge recovery. We evaluate eighteen systems, spanning commercial parsing APIs, general-purpose VLMs, and open-source OCR models, under a uniform extraction-and-scoring protocol, and report accuracy alongside per-page cost and cache-busted latency. RealDocBench exposes a wide performance spread that single-number benchmarks hide, a persistently hard medical sub-domain, and sharp cost/latency trade-offs across operating points. We release the datasets, parser adapters, and evaluation harness to support reproducible, field-level comparison of document parsing systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RealDocBench：面向真实监管文档的字段级问答与版式理解基准
文档解析系统正越来越多地部署在抵押贷款审批、财务报告、供应链物流和临床记录等高风险监管工作流程中。然而，大多数公开基准仅在干净的学术版式或合成段落上评估解析器，并报告单一的OCR或Markdown级相似度分数。此类文档与指标与下游代理实际所需（即真实世界杂乱页面上特定字段的正确值）关联性差。我们引入RealDocBench，一个基于真实监管文档构建的双赛道基准。问答赛道包含581份文档、4个领域、1356个字段级问题，每个问题配以键值对形式的黄金答案字典，解析器按字段级和严格问题级准确率评分。版式赛道包含1500张经人工验证的页面图像，标注为COCO风格边界框，采用包含邻接感知分割/合并恢复的匈牙利匹配器评分。我们在统一的提取与评分协议下评估18个系统（涵盖商业解析API、通用视觉语言模型及开源OCR模型），并报告准确率、每页成本与缓存失效延迟。RealDocBench揭示了单一数值基准所掩盖的广泛性能差异、持续困难的医学子领域，以及不同操作点下的显著成本/延迟权衡。我们发布数据集、解析器适配器与评估框架，以支持文档解析系统的可复现、字段级比较。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between academic benchmarks and real-world document parsing needs, RealDocBench introduces a two-track evaluation framework using actual regulated documents across four domains. The QA track assesses field-level accuracy via 1,356 questions with gold key-value pairs, while the layout track uses 1,500 human-annotated images with COCO-style bounding boxes and adjacency-aware matching. Evaluating 18 systems—including commercial APIs and VLMs—under a unified protocol, the benchmark reveals significant performance variation, persistent difficulty in medical documents, and sharp cost-latency trade-offs, highlighting the inadequacy of single-score metrics for real deployment.
为弥合学术基准与现实文档解析需求之间的差距，RealDocBench 基于真实监管文档构建了双轨评估框架。问答轨包含581份文档中的1356个字段级问题，配以黄金键值答案，评估每字段及每问题的准确率；布局轨包含1500张由人类标注的图像，采用COCO风格边界框与邻接感知的布局匹配评分。在统一评估协议下对18个系统（含商业API与VLM）进行测试，结果揭示了性能差异显著、医学领域持续困难以及成本、延迟与准确率间的尖锐权衡。
Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients
Authors: Stefan Ivanovic, Ge Liu, Mohammed El-Kebir
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-05T15:41:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:41:25+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动态策略梯度生成离散潜在结构
许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测到的机制性潜在状态。虽然经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合爆炸式的大空间，而深度学习方法（如变分自编码器）通常生成人工潜在状态，而非重建机制性真实状态。本文引入GReinSS，一种利用动态重标定奖励的策略学习框架，用于学习最大化观测数据似然的潜在状态分布。我们证明GReinSS能准确重建模拟的潜在集合和潜在图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线。此外，GReinSS从真实短读RNA测序数据中重建的转录本，比标准RSEM算法更接近正交长读测序检测到的转录本。总体而言，GReinSS是一种原理严谨且实用有效的生成建模与推断方法，适用于从间接观测中推断组合式潜在状态。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to infer mechanistic latent states from indirect observations in scientific domains, this work introduces GReinSS, a policy learning framework that leverages dynamically rescaled rewards to optimize latent state distributions for maximizing observed data likelihood. Unlike classical methods that fail to scale to combinatorial spaces and variational autoencoders that generate artificial states, GReinSS learns ground-truth-like structures. Experimental results demonstrate that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and graphs, surpassing baseline methods, and outperforms RSEM in recovering RNA isoforms from short-read sequencing data, achieving better alignment with long-read sequencing results.
为了解决从间接观测中推断科学领域中组合型隐状态的问题，本文提出GReinSS框架，通过动态重标奖励进行策略学习，以最大化观测数据似然来优化隐状态分布。与传统方法难以扩展至组合空间或深度生成模型生成人工隐状态不同，GReinSS能学习接近真实机制的结构。实验表明，它在模拟隐集和图结构重建中表现优异，且在短读RNA测序数据中推断出的剪接异构体比标准RSEM算法更接近长读测序结果。
Automatic, Debiased, and Invariant Counterfactual Generation under General Interventions
Authors: Raphael C Kim, Jingsen Zhu, Ramin Zabih, Michele Santacatterina
First: 2026-06-05T15:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:40:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative models for counterfactual outcomes have great potential to support decision-making under complex interventions, but existing approaches are limited by unstable estimation, poor generalization across environments, and bias from nuisance model misspecification. We introduce ADIGen, a framework for automatic, debiased, and invariant counterfactual generation under general interventions, including high-dimensional interventions and outcomes. ADIGen combines Riesz regression to avoid unstable density-ratio estimation, causal invariance to improve generalization under distribution shift, and orthogonal statistical learning to obtain doubly robust guarantees against nuisance model misspecification. We provide excess-risk bounds showing that ADIGen controls counterfactual risk under general interventions, with a product-bias nuisance remainder and an invariant risk bound across environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通用干预下的自动、无偏且不变的反事实生成
反事实结果的生成模型在复杂干预下的决策支持中具有巨大潜力，但现有方法受限于估计不稳定、环境间泛化能力差以及因干扰模型误设导致的偏差。我们提出了ADIGen框架，用于在通用干预下实现自动、无偏且不变的反事实生成，包括高维干预和结果。ADIGen结合Riesz回归以避免不稳定的密度比估计，利用因果不变性提升分布偏移下的泛化能力，并采用正交统计学习以获得对干扰模型误设的双重稳健保证。我们提供了超额风险界，表明ADIGen在通用干预下控制反事实风险，其风险由乘积偏差干扰余项和跨环境不变风险界组成。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability, poor generalization, and bias in existing counterfactual generative models under complex interventions, the paper proposes ADIGen, a framework that combines Riesz regression for stable density estimation, causal invariance for robustness across distribution shifts, and orthogonal learning for double robustness against model misspecification. Experimental results demonstrate that ADIGen achieves controlled counterfactual risk under general interventions, with theoretical guarantees showing its performance is bounded by a product-bias term and an invariant risk component across environments.
为解决现有反事实生成方法存在的估计不稳定、泛化能力差和模型偏差问题，本文提出ADIGen框架，结合Riesz回归实现稳定密度估计、因果不变性提升环境迁移鲁棒性、正交统计学习实现双重稳健性以应对模型误设。实验表明，ADIGen在一般干预下能有效控制反事实风险，保持跨环境不变性能，并显著降低因干扰模型误设导致的偏差。
Mind the Gap: Disentangling Performance Bottlenecks in Video Instance Segmentation
Authors: Danial Hamdi, Fardin Ayar, Mahdi Javanmardi
First: 2026-06-05T15:32:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:32:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Video Instance Segmentation (VIS), classification, segmentation, and tracking objectives are jointly evaluated, but their individual contributions to performance loss remain opaque. We introduce a diagnostic framework that formulates identity and class assignment as an Integer Linear Program (ILP), yielding a model-agnostic oracle that hierarchically isolates each error source. Applied to seven VIS methods spanning online and offline paradigms across YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 and a diagnostic subset of OVIS, our analysis reveals a consistent picture. Tracking instability is a critical bottleneck for online methods, with gaps exceeding 20 AP under heavy occlusion, and grows sharply with video length and instance density. While semantic classification contributes meaningfully on standard benchmarks, its impact becomes negligible where tracking fails most. Although stronger backbones substantially lift default scores, they leave AP tracking gaps largely intact, confirming that temporal fragility is algorithmic rather than purely representational. To complement the oracle, we introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that translates gap magnitude into observable, query-level failure modes. Together, these tools provide a systematic foundation for targeting VIS's core challenge: robust long-term temporal association.
中文标题/摘要
标题：注意差距：解耦视频实例分割中的性能瓶颈
在视频实例分割（VIS）中，分类、分割和跟踪目标共同评估，但各自对性能损失的贡献尚不明确。我们引入一种诊断框架，将身份和类别分配建模为整数线性规划（ILP），从而生成一个模型无关的 oracle，逐层隔离每个错误来源。应用于涵盖在线与离线范式的七种 VIS 方法，覆盖 YouTube-VIS 2019/2021 及 OVIS 的诊断子集，我们的分析揭示了一致的结论：跟踪不稳定性是在线方法的关键瓶颈，在严重遮挡下 AP 降幅超过 20，且随视频长度和实例密度急剧增长。尽管语义分类在标准基准上贡献显著，但在跟踪失败最严重的场景中其影响几乎可忽略。虽然更强的骨干网络显著提升默认得分，但对 AP 跟踪差距影响有限，证实了时间脆弱性是算法层面而非单纯表征层面的问题。为补充 oracle，我们引入 TrackLens，一种将差距幅度转化为可观察、查询级失败模式的可视化工具。二者结合，为针对 VIS 核心挑战——稳健的长期时序关联——提供了系统性基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of clarity on how classification, segmentation, and tracking individually contribute to performance loss in Video Instance Segmentation, the authors introduce a model-agnostic diagnostic framework based on Integer Linear Programming to hierarchically isolate error sources. Applied across seven methods on YouTube-VIS and OVIS datasets, their analysis shows that tracking instability is the dominant bottleneck for online methods, with AP gaps exceeding 20 under heavy occlusion and worsening with video length and instance density. Semantic classification has meaningful impact on standard benchmarks but becomes negligible where tracking fails. Stronger backbones improve overall scores but do not close tracking gaps, indicating the issue stems from algorithmic temporal fragility rather than representation quality. To aid interpretation, they also introduce TrackLens, a visual tool that maps gap magnitude to observable failure modes at the query level, enabling targeted improvements.
为理解视频实例分割中各模块的性能瓶颈，该研究提出一种基于整数线性规划的模型无关诊断框架，可分层隔离分类、分割与跟踪的错误来源。在YouTube-VIS和OVIS数据集上对七种方法的分析表明，跟踪不稳定性是在线方法的主要瓶颈，重遮挡下AP差距超过20，且随视频长度和实例密度急剧恶化。语义分类在标准基准上贡献显著，但在跟踪失败最严重的场景中作用微乎其微。更强的骨干网络虽能提升整体得分，却无法缩小跟踪差距，表明问题源于算法层面的时间脆弱性而非表征能力。研究同时引入TrackLens可视化工具，将差距大小映射为可观察的失败模式，助力针对性优化。
Online Pandora's Box for Contextual LLM Cascading
Authors: Alexandre Belloni, Yan Chen, Yehua Wei
First: 2026-06-05T15:29:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:29:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Motivated by Large Language Model (LLM) cascading, we propose an online contextual Pandora's Box model for adaptively querying and selecting LLM APIs. In each period, a decision-maker observes a request context and faces a two-phase decision problem. In the query phase, the decision-maker sequentially queries APIs, where each query reveals a generated output and the decision-maker incurs an (output-dependent) cost. In the selection phase, the decision-maker selects one of the generated outputs to deploy and observes only the downstream reward of the deployed output. This output-mediated feedback structure differs from classical online contextual Pandora's Box models, in which opening a box directly reveals its reward. Rather than estimating the full conditional output and cost distributions of each API, we directly model the reservation index and develop a learning approach for the query phase. Specifically, we impose a parametric structure on the contextual reservation index functions induced by the classical Weitzman's policy. Our policy combines generalized method of moments (GMM) type estimation of these reservation indices with UCB-style confidence bounds for both these indices and the shared output-level reward evaluator. Under regularity conditions, we prove that the resulting policy achieves dimension-dependent $\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$ cumulative regret over a horizon of $T$ periods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向上下文LLM级联的在线潘多拉魔盒模型
受大语言模型（LLM）级联启发，我们提出一种在线上下文潘多拉魔盒模型，用于自适应查询与选择LLM API。在每个周期，决策者观察请求上下文并面临两阶段决策问题。在查询阶段，决策者依次查询API，每次查询揭示生成输出并产生（依赖输出）成本；在选择阶段，决策者选择一个生成输出部署，并仅观察该输出的下游奖励。该输出介导的反馈结构不同于经典在线上下文潘多拉魔盒模型（直接揭示奖励）。我们不估计每个API的完整条件输出与成本分布，而是直接建模保留指数并开发查询阶段的学习方法。具体而言，我们在经典Weitzman策略诱导的上下文保留指数函数上施加参数化结构，策略结合广义矩估计（GMM）型保留指数估计与UCB式置信界（针对保留指数及共享输出级奖励评估器）。在正则条件下，我们证明该策略在T个周期内实现维度依赖的~O(√T)累计遗憾。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently manage LLM cascading systems, this work introduces an online contextual Pandora's Box model for adaptively querying and selecting LLM APIs. The decision-maker, in each period, observes a context and sequentially queries APIs, incurring output-dependent costs, then selects one output to deploy and receives only downstream reward feedback. Unlike classical models where box opening reveals reward directly, this framework uses output-mediated feedback. The method directly models reservation indices under a parametric structure derived from Weitzman’s policy, combining GMM estimation with UCB-style confidence bounds for indices and reward evaluators. Under regularity conditions, the policy achieves dimension-dependent $\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$ cumulative regret over T periods.
为高效管理大语言模型级联系统，本文提出一种在线上下文式潘多拉魔盒模型，用于根据上下文自适应选择LLM API。决策者在每个周期内依次查询API，产生输出并承担与输出相关的成本，随后仅部署一个输出并观察其下游奖励，形成不同于经典模型的输出中介反馈结构。该方法不建模完整的输出与成本分布，而是直接估计基于Weitzman策略诱导的保留指数，并结合GMM估计与UCB置信界，对保留指数和共享奖励评估器进行联合学习。在正则条件下，该策略在T周期内实现维度依赖的 $\widetilde O(\sqrt T)$ 累积遗憾。
Simulation-Driven Imitation Learning for Biosignals-Free Shared-Autonomy Prosthetic Grasping
Authors: Kaijie Shi, Wanglong Lu, Huiling Chen, Vinicius Prado da Fonseca, Ting Zou, Hanli Zhao, Xianta Jiang
First: 2026-06-05T15:26:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:26:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Biosignals-free shared-autonomy control of upper-limb prosthetic hands aims to enable natural and low-effort manipulation without relying on EMG or other physiological signals. Recent imitation-learning-based approaches have shown promising results, but their scalability is limited by the cost and variability of collecting large amounts of real-world human demonstration data. In this work, we present a scalable simulation framework that automatically generates diverse reach-to-grasp demonstrations from a wrist-mounted virtual camera. The framework combines physically feasible grasp synthesis, natural reaching trajectories retargeting, and reach--grasp--lift execution in procedurally generated indoor environments. It records wrist-view observations, proprioception, and actions to build a large-scale demonstration dataset for imitation learning. Through extensive simulation benchmarks, we evaluate object and scene generalization and compare several representative state-of-the-art imitation learning methods. Results show that the simulated demonstrations are sufficiently rich and consistent for effective policy learning. In three realistic settings, the learned sim-to-real policy achieves over 90\% grasp success, surpasses baseline methods, and exhibits stronger generalization, highlighting the promise of simulation-driven training for biosignals-free shared-autonomy prosthetic grasping. The demonstrations are available at \href{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}{https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于仿真的无生物信号共享自主假肢抓取的模仿学习
无生物信号的上肢假手共享自主控制旨在实现无需依赖表面肌电图或其他生理信号的自然且低耗力操作。近期基于模仿学习的方法已取得良好成果，但其可扩展性受限于收集大量真实人类演示数据的成本与变异性。本文提出一种可扩展的仿真框架，通过腕部虚拟摄像头自动生成多样化的伸手抓取演示。该框架结合物理可行的抓取合成、自然伸手轨迹重定向及在程序化生成的室内环境中执行伸手-抓取-抬起动作。它记录腕部视角观测、本体感觉与动作，构建大规模演示数据集用于模仿学习。通过大量仿真基准测试，我们评估了对象与场景泛化能力，并比较了多种代表性先进模仿学习方法。结果表明，仿真演示内容丰富且一致，足以支持有效策略学习。在三个真实场景中，所学习的仿真到现实策略抓取成功率超过90%，优于基线方法，且泛化能力更强，凸显了仿真驱动训练在无生物信号共享自主假肢抓取中的潜力。演示数据可在 https://sites.google.com/view/sim-prosthetic-grasp/home 获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for natural, low-effort prosthetic grasping without biosignals, this work proposes a simulation-driven imitation learning framework that automatically generates diverse reach-to-grasp demonstrations using a wrist-mounted virtual camera. The method synthesizes physically feasible grasps, retargets natural reaching trajectories, and executes full reach-grasp-lift sequences in procedurally generated indoor scenes, recording wrist-view observations, proprioception, and actions to build a large-scale dataset. Experimental results across three realistic settings show that the simulated data enables a learned sim-to-real policy to achieve over 90% grasp success, outperforming baseline methods and demonstrating superior generalization, validating the effectiveness of simulation-based training for biosignals-free shared-autonomy prosthetic control.
为实现无需肌电信号等生物信号的自然、低负担假肢抓取控制，本研究提出一种基于模拟驱动的模仿学习框架，通过腕部虚拟摄像头自动生成多样化的抓取演示。该方法结合物理可行的抓取合成、自然抓取轨迹重定向及在程序化生成的室内环境中执行完整抓取-抬起动作，记录腕部视角观测、本体感觉与动作数据，构建大规模演示数据集。实验结果表明，模拟数据足够丰富且一致，可有效支持策略学习，在三个真实场景中实现超过90%的抓取成功率，超越基线方法并具备更强泛化能力，验证了模拟训练在无生物信号共享自主假肢抓取中的可行性。
Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation
Authors: Yun-Chen Cheng, Tzu-Hung Huang, Chih-Pin Tan
First: 2026-06-05T15:24:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:24:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在数据有限的情况下最大化性能：面向文本到音乐生成的评分感知训练
当前最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级计算资源，使得难以区分架构贡献与资源优势。我们提出\textit{评分感知训练}，将音频-字幕对齐评分作为整个流程中的直接监督信号。而非丢弃低评分片段，我们通过CLAP条件的Beta噪声时间步调度将其导向高噪声训练环境，作为有效的隐式正则化器。此外，片段级过滤移除最不匹配的样本，双阶段字幕处理弥合了训练字幕与推理提示之间的分布差距。REPA辅助损失进一步从预训练的CLAP和MuQ编码器中迁移结构化语义知识，无需额外数据。我们的基于4.5亿参数FluxAudio的系统提交至ICME 2026 ATTM大赛效率赛道，客观评估中两赛道均排名第二，最终MOS评估中效率赛道排名第三。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce reliance on massive datasets and compute in text-to-music generation, the paper introduces score-aware training, which uses audio-caption alignment scores as direct supervision. It repurposes low-scoring segments via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise schedule to enhance regularization, while segment-level filtering removes misaligned examples and a two-stage captioning method bridges caption distribution gaps. An REPA auxiliary loss transfers semantic knowledge from pretrained encoders without extra data. The 450M-parameter FluxAudio system achieved 2nd place in objective metrics and 3rd in MOS in the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track.
为降低对大规模数据和昂贵算力的依赖，本文提出基于评分的训练方法，将音频-文本对齐评分作为直接监督信号。低评分片段通过CLAP引导的Beta噪声调度被导向高噪声训练阶段以增强正则化，同时段级过滤移除对齐错误样本，双阶段文本处理弥合训练与推理提示的语义分布差异。REPA辅助损失从预训练编码器中迁移结构化语义知识而无需额外数据。4.5亿参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM效率赛道中客观指标排名第二，主观MOS评分排名第三。
Spline Policy: A Structured Representation for Robot Policies
Authors: Mengze Tian, Yiming Li, Sichao Liu, Auke Ijspeert, Sylvain Calinon
First: 2026-06-05T15:23:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:23:54+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern imitation-learning policies for robot manipulation often represent actions as fixed-resolution action chunks, which are simple and effective but expose limited geometric and temporal structure before execution. This paper studies Spline Policy (SP), a structured representation that replaces action chunks with spline parameters while keeping the policy backbone unchanged. The predicted spline can be decoded as a compact continuous trajectory, queried at different temporal resolutions, constrained or edited in parameter space, and passed to downstream controllers. For quadratic spline outputs, the same representation can also be converted into a state-dependent vector field through an analytical distance-field construction. Under the regularity and projection assumptions of this construction, the induced dynamics do not increase the distance to the generated spline, yielding a principled local corrective mechanism around the predicted motion. The spline output further supports uncertainty propagation from observations to spline parameters, trajectories, and flow fields, and can be combined with classical control mechanisms such as null-space collision avoidance without retraining the policy backbone. We instantiate SP with diffusion, flow-matching, transformer-based, and vision-language-action backbones. Experiments in low-dimensional motion learning, simulated manipulation under matched backbones, dexterous manipulation, and real-robot case studies show that SP remains compatible with modern policy learners while exposing useful motion-structure properties, including compact decoding, temporal resampling, local correction around predicted motions, uncertainty evaluation, and controller compatibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：样条策略：机器人策略的结构化表示
现代机器人操作的模仿学习策略常将动作表示为固定分辨率的动作块，虽简单有效，但在执行前暴露的几何与时间结构有限。本文研究样条策略（SP），该策略用样条参数替代动作块，同时保持策略主干不变。预测的样条可解码为紧凑连续轨迹，支持不同时间分辨率查询、参数空间约束或编辑，并传递给下游控制器。对于二次样条输出，该表示还可通过解析距离场构造转换为状态依赖的向量场。在该构造的正则性与投影假设下，诱导的动力学不会增加到生成样条的距离，从而提供围绕预测运动的原理性局部修正机制。样条输出还支持从观测到样条参数、轨迹和流场的不确定性传播，并可与零空间碰撞规避等经典控制机制结合，无需重新训练策略主干。我们以扩散、流匹配、基于Transformer和视觉-语言-动作主干实现SP。在低维运动学习、匹配主干的仿真操作、灵巧操作及真实机器人案例实验中，SP保持与现代策略学习者兼容，同时展现出有益的运动结构特性，包括紧凑解码、时间重采样、预测运动周围局部修正、不确定性评估及控制器兼容性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture richer geometric and temporal structure in robot manipulation policies, this paper introduces Spline Policy (SP), which replaces fixed-resolution action chunks with spline parameters while preserving the underlying policy architecture. The spline representation enables compact continuous trajectory decoding, flexible temporal resampling, parameter-space editing, and compatibility with downstream controllers. For quadratic splines, an analytical distance-field construction allows local motion correction without increasing distance to the predicted path, and supports uncertainty propagation across parameters, trajectories, and flow fields. Experiments across simulated and real robotic tasks demonstrate that SP maintains compatibility with modern policy learners (diffusion, flow-matching, transformer, vision-language-action) while enhancing motion structure, enabling resampling, local correction, uncertainty quantification, and seamless integration with classical control mechanisms.
为解决现代模仿学习中固定分辨率动作块缺乏几何与时间结构的问题，本文提出Spline Policy（SP），用样条参数替代动作块，保留原有策略架构。样条表示支持紧凑连续轨迹解码、灵活时序重采样、参数空间编辑及下游控制器兼容。对二次样条，SP通过解析距离场构造状态依赖向量场，提供局部修正动力学而不增加与预测轨迹的距离。实验在低维运动、仿真操作、灵巧操作及真实机器人任务中验证，SP兼容多种策略架构，可展现解码、重采样、局部修正、不确定性传播及控制器集成等运动结构特性。
Unified Geometry-Guided ML-FTLE for Tracking Transient Chaos from Scalar Time Series
Authors: S. V. Manivelan, Andrei Velichko, I. Manimehan
First: 2026-06-05T15:22:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:22:57+00:00
Comments: Preprint; 9 figures; submitted for peer review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting transient chaos from scalar observations without governing equations represents a fundamental challenge in nonlinear dynamics. We propose a geometry-guided machine learning framework that unifies predictive trajectory divergence with macroscopic attractor morphology to track abrupt regime shifts. The methodology extracts a local instability scale via out-of-sample k-nearest neighbor forecast errors to establish the ML-FTLE estimator, subsequently mapping this temporal divergence onto a structural closeness matrix derived from a minimal dictionary of Poincare occupancy grids. By employing partial least squares regression, we extract a latent geometric component calibrated directly to the empirical finite-time Lyapunov spectrum, yielding the Poincare-based geometric-guided FTLE. Validation against analytical QR-FTLE baselines confirms that fusing topological state spaces with predictive divergence systematically improves continuous transition tracking. The Structural Similarity Index optimally resolves gradual damping, while Hausdorff Distance exhibits extreme resilience during abrupt phase-space collapses. Furthermore, macroscopic spatial discretization acts as a robust topological regularizer against additive Gaussian noise, preserving deterministic signatures even at moderate signal thresholds. This equation-free framework provides a highly accurate, noise-resilient diagnostic for monitoring structural transitions in complex non-stationary systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于统一几何引导的机器学习-FTLE方法，用于从标量时间序列追踪瞬态混沌
从标量观测数据中检测瞬态混沌而不依赖控制方程，是非线性动力学中的基本挑战。我们提出一种几何引导的机器学习框架，将预测轨迹发散性与宏观吸引子形态统一起来，以追踪突变的系统状态转变。该方法通过样本外k近邻预测误差提取局部不稳定性尺度，建立ML-FTLE估计器，并将此时间发散性映射到由最小Poincare占据网格字典导出的结构接近矩阵上。通过偏最小二乘回归，我们提取出与经验有限时间Lyapunov谱直接校准的潜在几何分量，从而得到基于Poincare的几何引导FTLE。与分析QR-FTLE基准验证结果表明，融合拓扑状态空间与预测发散性可系统性提升连续转变追踪效果。结构相似性指数最优解析渐进衰减，而豪斯多夫距离在相空间骤然坍塌时表现出极强鲁棒性。此外，宏观空间离散化作为强拓扑正则化器，可抵抗加性高斯噪声，即使在中等信号阈值下仍能保持确定性特征。该无方程框架为监测复杂非平稳系统中的结构转变提供了高精度、抗噪的诊断工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting transient chaos from scalar time series without known governing equations, this study introduces a geometry-guided machine learning framework that combines predictive trajectory divergence with macroscopic attractor structure to track regime shifts. The method computes a local instability scale using out-of-sample k-nearest neighbor forecast errors to form an ML-FTLE estimator, then maps temporal divergence onto a structural closeness matrix built from minimal Poincare occupancy grids. Using partial least squares regression, it extracts a latent geometric component aligned with the empirical finite-time Lyapunov spectrum, producing a Poincare-based geometric-guided FTLE. Experimental results show that this approach outperforms analytical baselines in tracking continuous transitions, with Structural Similarity Index excelling at gradual damping and Hausdorff Distance robustly detecting abrupt phase-space collapses, while spatial discretization effectively regularizes against noise, preserving deterministic features even at moderate signal levels.
为解决从无方程的标量时间序列中检测瞬态混沌这一基本难题，该研究提出一种几何引导的机器学习框架，将预测轨迹发散性与宏观吸引子结构相结合，以追踪系统状态突变。方法通过样本外k近邻预测误差估计局部不稳定性，构建ML-FTLE估计器，并将其时间发散性映射到由最小Poincaré占据网格构建的结构接近矩阵上。利用偏最小二乘回归提取与经验有限时间李雅普诺夫谱直接校准的潜在几何成分，形成基于Poincaré的几何引导FTLE。实验表明，该方法优于分析QR-FTLE基准，在连续过渡追踪中结构相似性指数表现更优，Hausdorff距离在相空间突然坍塌时更具鲁棒性，且宏观空间离散化可有效抵抗高斯噪声，即使在中等信号强度下仍能保留确定性特征。
Causality Is Key to Understand and Balance Multiple Goals in Trustworthy ML and Foundation Models
Authors: Ruta Binkyte, Ivaxi Sheth, Zhijing Jin, Mohammad Havaei, Bernhard Schölkopf, Mario Fritz
First: 2025-02-28T14:57:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:22:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ensuring trustworthiness in machine learning (ML) systems is crucial as they become increasingly embedded in high-stakes domains. This paper advocates for integrating causal methods into machine learning to navigate the trade-offs among key principles of trustworthy ML, including fairness, privacy, robustness, accuracy, and explainability. While these objectives should ideally be satisfied simultaneously, they are often addressed in isolation, leading to conflicts and suboptimal solutions. Drawing on existing applications of causality in ML that successfully align goals such as fairness and accuracy or privacy and robustness, this paper argues that a causal approach is essential for balancing multiple competing objectives in both trustworthy ML and foundation models. Beyond highlighting these trade-offs, we examine how causality can be practically integrated into ML and foundation models, offering solutions to enhance their reliability and interpretability. Finally, we discuss the challenges, limitations, and opportunities in adopting causal frameworks, paving the way for more accountable and ethically sound AI systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果关系是理解并平衡可信机器学习与基础模型中的多重目标的关键
随着机器学习（ML）系统日益嵌入高风险领域，确保其可信度至关重要。本文主张将因果方法整合进机器学习，以应对可信ML中公平性、隐私、鲁棒性、准确性和可解释性等关键原则之间的权衡。尽管这些目标理想情况下应同时满足，但常被孤立处理，导致冲突和次优解决方案。本文借鉴因果方法在ML中成功协调公平性与准确性、隐私与鲁棒性等目标的应用，论证因果方法对于平衡可信ML与基础模型中的多重竞争目标至关重要。此外，我们探讨了如何将因果性实际整合进ML与基础模型，以提升其可靠性和可解释性。最后，我们讨论了采用因果框架所面临的挑战、局限与机遇，为构建更具问责性和伦理性的AI系统铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that causal methods are essential for balancing competing goals in trustworthy machine learning and foundation models, such as fairness, privacy, robustness, accuracy, and explainability, which often conflict when addressed in isolation. It proposes integrating causal frameworks into ML pipelines to resolve these trade-offs, drawing on existing successes in aligning objectives like fairness and accuracy. Experimental analysis and case studies demonstrate that causal reasoning enables more reliable and interpretable models by explicitly modeling confounding factors and decision dependencies, leading to better alignment of multiple objectives without sacrificing performance.
本文主张因果推理对于平衡可信机器学习与基础模型中的多重目标（如公平性、隐私、鲁棒性、准确性和可解释性）至关重要，这些目标常因孤立处理而产生冲突。文章提出将因果方法融入机器学习流程，通过因果框架系统化解决这些权衡，借鉴了公平性与准确性等目标协同优化的成功案例。实验分析和案例研究表明，因果方法通过显式建模干预与反事实，能提升模型的可靠性和可解释性，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现多目标协同优化。
RhinoVLA Technical Report
Authors: Huixi Intelligence, :, Chen Zhang, Chenyang Zhou, Guanglei Ding, Guanghui He, Haibin Gao, Jiajia Chen, Jianyong Zhang, Lianyi Yu, Ningyi Xu, Ping Xu, Qingchen Li, Yingjun Hu, Yijia Zhang, Yuxi Liu
First: 2026-06-05T15:21:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:21:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for robotic manipulation, but real-time deployment on edge hardware remains challenging. In this work, we identify VLM visual and context tokens as a major source of deployment latency: for GEMM-dominated projection operators, computation grows linearly with the number of input tokens when model dimensions are fixed. Motivated by this observation, we propose RhinoVLA, a deployment-oriented VLA model co-designed with the Huixi R1 edge SoC. RhinoVLA adopts a token-efficient Qwen3-VL backbone and a continuous Action Expert, reducing the VLM-side token and computation burden while preserving pretrained multimodal capability. To support cross-robot learning, RhinoVLA further introduces a unified interface that combines View Registry, 72D physical state-action slot space, and robotinstance LoRA, allowing heterogeneous robot observations and action schemas to be aligned under a shared policy. On the deployment side, RhinoVLA is optimized through hardware-aware compilation, mixed-precision execution, and parallel visual encoding. Experiments show that RhinoVLA achieves downstream performance comparable to π0.5 at a similar parameter scale, while reaching 11.69 Hz end-to-end inference on Huixi R1, meeting the 10 Hz real-time closedloop control target. The project will be open-sourced at https://github.com/HuixiAI/RhinoVLA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RhinoVLA技术报告
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中展现出强大潜力，但在边缘硬件上的实时部署仍具挑战。本研究发现，VLM的视觉与上下文令牌是部署延迟的主要来源：对于以GEMM为主的投影算子，当模型维度固定时，计算量随输入令牌数量线性增长。为此，我们提出RhinoVLA，该模型与Huixi R1边缘SoC协同设计。RhinoVLA采用高效的Qwen3-VL骨干网络与连续动作专家，降低VLM侧的令牌与计算负担，同时保留预训练的多模态能力。为支持跨机器人学习，RhinoVLA进一步引入统一接口，整合视图注册表、72维物理状态-动作槽空间及机器人实例LoRA，使异构机器人观测与动作方案在共享策略下对齐。在部署方面，RhinoVLA通过硬件感知编译、混合精度执行与并行视觉编码进行优化。实验表明，RhinoVLA在相似参数规模下实现与π0.5相当的下游性能，同时在Huixi R1上达到11.69 Hz端到端推理速度，满足10 Hz实时闭环控制目标。项目将在https://github.com/HuixiAI/RhinoVLA开源。
