Motivated by the high deployment latency caused by VLM visual and context tokens in VLA models, RhinoVLA introduces a token-efficient architecture co-designed with the Huixi R1 edge SoC, combining a Qwen3-VL backbone and a continuous Action Expert to reduce computational load while preserving multimodal capability. It further enables cross-robot learning via a unified interface integrating View Registry, 72D state-action slots, and robotinstance LoRA. Through hardware-aware compilation, mixed-precision execution, and parallel visual encoding, RhinoVLA achieves 11.69 Hz end-to-end inference on Huixi R1, matching π0.5’s downstream performance at a similar parameter scale and meeting the 10 Hz real-time control requirement.
为解决视觉语言模型中视觉与上下文token导致的实时部署延迟问题，RhinoVLA提出与Huixi R1边缘SoC协同设计的轻量架构，采用Qwen3-VL骨干网络与连续动作专家，降低token负担并保留多模态能力。通过视图注册表、72维状态动作槽与机器人实例LoRA构建统一接口，支持跨机器人学习。结合硬件感知编译、混合精度执行与并行视觉编码优化部署效率。实验表明，RhinoVLA在参数规模相近下性能媲美π0.5，于Huixi R1上实现11.69Hz端到端推理，满足10Hz实时闭环控制需求。
Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation
Authors: Mathieu Chalvidal, Florentin Coeurdoux, Eric Vanden-Eijnden
First: 2026-06-05T15:21:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:21:27+00:00
Comments: 18 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过随机插值进行协方差收缩
我们将高维协方差估计器的经典收缩方法重新表述为在源分布与目标分布之间的参数化随机插值上的经验风险最小化。该形式化方法可恢复已知的收缩估计器作为特例，并揭示三种降低统计风险的机制：(i) 调度：插值调度决定了可接受协方差的类别，从而决定可实现的风险。(ii) 流映射与耦合：虽然朴素构造假设分布之间相互独立，但特定耦合结构（如最优传输问题的解）可降低经验风险。此外，实现此类耦合的非线性流映射可使插值协方差脱离经验估计的特征基，从而实现特征向量正则化。(iii) 早期停止：通过积分回归向量场定义的估计器，可通过逼近真实插值分布，提供额外的偏差-方差权衡。随后，我们提出一种插值的神经网络估计器，并给出其二次风险相对于插值近似误差的上界，且在合成实验中验证了两者。最后，我们将该估计器应用于真实神经影像数据，展示了该方法在实践中提供的额外正则化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve covariance estimation in high-dimensional settings, this work reinterprets classical shrinkage methods as empirical risk minimization over a stochastic interpolant between source and target distributions. The authors introduce three key mechanisms—scheduling, flow maps with optimal transport couplings, and early stopping—that jointly reduce statistical risk by constraining the covariance space, enabling eigenvector regularization, and introducing bias-variance trade-offs. They propose a neural estimator for the interpolant and provide a theoretical bound on its quadratic risk, validated on synthetic data. Applied to real neuroimaging data, the method demonstrates enhanced regularization and improved performance over standard approaches.
为提升高维环境下协方差估计的性能，本文将经典收缩方法重新表述为在源分布与目标分布之间随机插值的实证风险最小化问题。该方法包含三种机制：通过调度定义可接受的协方差类，利用最优传输耦合结构降低风险并实现特征向量正则化，以及通过早期停止平衡偏差与方差。合成数据实验验证了理论界值并展示了更优的风险表现，真实神经影像数据应用也证实了该方法在实践中的额外正则化能力。
Impact of Synthetic Lesional MR Images in Automated Focal Cortical Dysplasia Detection in Low-Data Scenarios
Authors: Prabhjot Kaur, Hakim Ouaalam, Sedat Kandemirli, Sanjay P. Prabhu, Simon K. Warfield
First: 2026-06-05T15:21:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:21:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background and Purpose: Automated detection of focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) requires large volumes of voxelwise lesion-delineated MRI data, which are difficult to acquire. This study aims to generate synthetic MRI data exhibiting FCD, assess their realism, and evaluate their impact on automated FCD detection, particularly in reducing the need for manual annotations. Methods: T1-weighted (T1w) and T2-weighted Fluid-Attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) MRI scans from 131 FCD patients and 90 healthy controls from multiple (3) sites were retrospectively studied. Synthetic MRIs were generated by conditioning a generative network on binary FCD masks. Two neuroradiologists identified real images from a random set of 14 real and 14 synthetic scans. Three nnU-Net models were trained to detect FCD using: (i) real-only (35 FCD / 35 controls), (ii) real (35 FCD / 35 controls) plus synthetic augmentation, and (iii) expanded real data (70 FCD / 70 controls). Results: Experts showed limited ability to distinguish real from synthetic images, with classification accuracy of 60% for T1w and 70% for FLAIR (inter-rater agreement kappa = 0.86). Augmenting automated FCD detection with synthetic data increased sensitivity by 8.14% (p = 0.12) and improved model confidence at true lesion sites (0.83 +/- 0.11 to 0.89 +/- 0.12; p = 0.02). The expanded real-data model further improved sensitivity to 73.8% (p < 0.001) and confidence to 0.90 +/- 0.14 (p = 0.01). Conclusion: Conditional generative networks can generate realistic synthetic FCD-MRIs, reducing labeled data needs by approximately 20% while maintaining equivalent sensitivity. Equivalent amounts of real data, when available, remain more effective than synthetic augmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合成病灶MRI图像在低数据场景下自动化局灶性皮质发育不良检测中的影响
背景与目的：自动化检测局灶性皮质发育不良（FCD）需要大量逐体素标注病灶的MRI数据，获取困难。本研究旨在生成表现出FCD的合成MRI数据，评估其真实性，并评估其对自动化FCD检测的影响，特别是减少人工标注需求。 方法：回顾性研究了来自3个不同中心的131例FCD患者和90例健康对照者的T1加权（T1w）和T2加权液体衰减反转恢复（FLAIR）MRI扫描。通过将生成网络条件化于二值FCD掩膜生成合成MRI。两名神经放射科医生从14张真实和14张合成图像的随机集合中识别真实图像。训练了三个nnU-Net模型以检测FCD，分别使用：(i) 仅真实数据（35例FCD/35例对照），(ii) 真实数据（35例FCD/35例对照）加合成数据增强，(iii) 扩展真实数据（70例FCD/70例对照）。 结果：专家对真实与合成图像的区分能力有限，T1w分类准确率为60%，FLAIR为70%（组内一致性Kappa=0.86）。在自动化FCD检测中加入合成数据，敏感性提高8.14%（p=0.12），并在真实病灶处提升模型置信度（0.83±0.11至0.89±0.12；p=0.02）。扩展真实数据模型进一步将敏感性提升至73.8%（p<0.001），置信度提升至0.90±0.14（p=0.01）。 结论：条件生成网络可生成逼真的合成FCD-MRI图像，在保持等效敏感性前提下减少约20%的标注数据需求。若真实数据充足，其效果优于合成数据增强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of labeled MRI data for automated focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) detection, this study used conditional generative networks to create synthetic T1w and FLAIR MRIs conditioned on binary FCD masks. Two neuroradiologists could only classify real vs. synthetic images with moderate accuracy (60-70%), indicating high realism. Three nnU-Net models were trained on real-only, synthetic-augmented, and expanded real datasets; synthetic augmentation improved sensitivity by 8.14% and confidence at lesion sites, while expanded real data achieved the highest sensitivity (73.8%) and confidence (0.90), demonstrating that real data remains superior when available.
为缓解FCD检测中标注数据稀缺的问题，本研究利用条件生成网络从二值化FCD掩膜生成逼真合成MRI图像，以减少人工标注依赖。方法训练了三个nnU-Net模型，分别使用纯真实数据、真实数据加合成数据增强和扩充真实数据，两名放射科医生评估合成图像的真实性。结果显示，专家仅能以60–70%准确率区分真实与合成图像，而合成数据增强使检测敏感性提升8.14%、模型置信度提高，扩充真实数据组则达到最高敏感性73.8%和置信度0.90。
Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests
Authors: Thanawat Lodkaew, Johannes Ackermann, Soichiro Nishimori, Nontawat Charoenphakdee, Masashi Sugiyama, Takashi Ishida
First: 2026-06-05T15:20:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:20:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编码代理会欺骗我们吗？通过带随机测试的受限评估检测与防止作弊
在代理评估与训练中，一个日益严重的失败模式是模型可通过利用捷径而非解决实际任务来获得高评估分数，从而产生欺骗性表现。这使得评估分数无法可靠反映模型的真实任务解决能力。我们提出CapCode框架，用于构建包含随机测试的编码数据集，其最佳非作弊表现被刻意限制在1以下。这种受限表现设计使评估分数更具可解释性：显著高于上限的分数是不可信的，从而提供作弊证据。为防止作弊，我们提出基于CapCode原则的CapReward奖励设计，以抑制超出上限的优化行为。在多个数据集上的实验表明，CapCode能检测作弊同时保持模型性能排序，而CapReward可减少作弊行为，使模型更贴近任务规范。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem that models often achieve high scores by exploiting shortcuts rather than solving tasks genuinely, the paper introduces CapCode, a framework that constructs coding datasets with randomized tests to cap the best non-cheating performance below one, making scores above this cap indicative of cheating. It further proposes CapReward, a reward mechanism based on this capped design to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that CapCode successfully detects cheating while preserving model ranking, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, resulting in models that more faithfully follow task specifications.
鉴于模型常通过捷径获得高分而非真正解决任务，本文提出CapCode框架，通过设计随机化测试使非作弊情况下最佳表现被人为限制在低于1的水平，从而让高于该阈值的分数被视为作弊证据。同时提出CapReward奖励机制，以抑制超出该上限的优化行为。实验表明，CapCode能有效检测作弊且保持模型性能排序准确性，CapReward可显著减少作弊行为，使模型更忠实地遵循任务规范。
You Only Landmark Once: Lightweight U-Net Face Super Resolution with YOLO-World Landmark Heatmaps
Authors: Riccardo Carraro, Anna Briotto, Endi Hysa, Marco Fiorucci, Lamberto Ballan
First: 2026-05-13T22:41:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:19:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at IEEE AVSS 2026 (Notification date: June 5, 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Face image super-resolution aims to recover high-resolution facial images from severely degraded inputs. Under extreme upscaling factors, fine facial details are often lost, making accurate reconstruction challenging. Existing methods typically rely on heavy network architectures, adversarial training schemes, or separate alignment networks, increasing model complexity and computational cost. To address these issues, we propose a lightweight U-Net based-architecture designed to reconstructs $128{ \times }128$ facial images from severely degraded $16{ \times }16$ inputs, achieving an $8 \times $ magnification. A key contribution is a novel auxiliary-training-free supervision strategy that leverages heatmaps generated by YOLO-World, an open-vocabulary object detector, to localize key facial features such as eyes, nose, and mouth. These heatmaps are converted into spatial weights to form a heatmap-guided loss that emphasizes reconstruction errors in semantically important regions. Unlike prior methods that require dedicated landmark or alignment networks, our approach directly reuses detector outputs as supervision, maintaining an efficient training and inference pipeline. Experiments on the aligned CelebA dataset demonstrate that the proposed loss consistently improves quantitative metrics and produces sharper, more realistic reconstructions. Overall, our results show that lightweight networks can effectively exploit detection-driven priors for perceptually convincing extreme upscaling, without adversarial training or increased computational cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你只需一次地标：基于YOLO-World地标热图的轻量级U-Net人脸超分辨率方法
人脸图像超分辨率旨在从严重退化的输入中恢复高分辨率人脸图像。在极端放大倍数下，精细面部细节常被丢失，导致精确重建困难。现有方法通常依赖复杂网络架构、对抗训练方案或独立的对齐网络，增加了模型复杂度和计算成本。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种轻量级U-Net架构，可从严重退化的16×16输入重建128×128人脸图像，实现8倍放大。关键贡献是一种新颖的无辅助训练监督策略，利用开放词汇目标检测器YOLO-World生成的热图定位关键面部特征（如眼睛、鼻子和嘴巴）。这些热图转换为空间权重，形成热图引导损失，强调语义重要区域的重建误差。与以往需专用地标或对齐网络的方法不同，我们的方法直接复用检测器输出作为监督，保持高效训练和推理流程。在对齐的CelebA数据集上的实验表明，所提损失持续提升定量指标，并生成更锐利、更逼真的重建结果。总体而言，我们的结果表明轻量级网络可有效利用检测驱动先验实现感知上令人信服的极端放大，无需对抗训练或增加计算成本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing high-resolution facial images from severely degraded inputs under extreme upscaling, this work proposes a lightweight U-Net architecture that achieves 8x magnification from 16x16 to 128x128. It avoids heavy networks, adversarial training, and separate alignment modules by using YOLO-World’s landmark heatmaps as supervision, converting them into spatial weights for a heatmap-guided loss that prioritizes reconstruction in semantically critical regions. Experiments on aligned CelebA show consistent improvements in quantitative metrics and perceptual quality, demonstrating that detection-driven priors can enable efficient, high-fidelity super-resolution without added computational burden.
为解决在极端放大倍率下从严重降质输入重建高分辨率人脸图像的难题，本文提出一种轻量级U-Net架构，可将16x16输入放大至128x128，实现8倍放大。该方法引入一种基于YOLO-World检测器输出热图的引导损失，无需额外对齐网络，通过将热图转换为空间权重，强化对关键面部区域（如眼睛、鼻子、嘴巴）的重建精度。实验表明，在CelebA对齐数据集上，该方法在定量指标和视觉质量上均优于现有方法，证明轻量级网络可有效利用检测驱动先验实现高质量超分辨率，且无需对抗训练或增加计算开销。
Beyond Backscatter: InSAR coherence from detected SAR images
Authors: Francescopaolo Sica, Andrea Pulella, Michael Schmitt
First: 2026-06-05T15:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-05T15:18:28+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we propose a deep learning framework for coherence regression directly from detected SAR images, without the need for accurate coregistration. A Residual U-Net is trained using coherence maps derived from precisely coregistered Sentinel-1 SLC data to learn the relationship between backscatter magnitudes and coherence. The model is trained on 12-day SLC pairs and evaluated across different datasets, including coregistered SLC products and open access analysis-ready data, covering diverse radiometric properties, geometries, and locations. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves high-resolution coherence regression with improved accuracy compared to existing intensity-based approaches. The network generalizes well across diverse geographical locations and even across different temporal baselines that were never seen at training time. Additionally, the ability to operate on globally available analysis-ready data, such as ground range detected data, e.g., distributed through Google Earth Engine, enables its large-scale application in mission design, change monitoring, and diverse mapping tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越后向散射：从检测到的SAR图像直接进行InSAR相干性回归
本文提出了一种从检测到的SAR图像直接进行相干性回归的深度学习框架，无需精确配准。使用从精确配准的Sentinel-1 SLC数据导出的相干性图训练残差U-Net，以学习后向散射幅值与相干性之间的关系。模型在12天SLC图像对上训练，并在多种数据集上评估，包括配准SLC产品和公开可访问的分析就绪数据，涵盖多样化的辐射特性、几何结构和地理位置。实验结果表明，该方法实现了高分辨率相干性回归，相比现有基于强度的方法具有更高的精度。该网络在不同地理区域和训练时未见过的时间基线上均表现出良好的泛化能力。此外，其能够处理全球可获取的分析就绪数据（如地面距离检测数据，例如通过Google Earth Engine分发），从而支持其在任务设计、变化监测和多样化制图任务中的大规模应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate coherence estimation without precise coregistration, this study proposes a deep learning framework using a Residual U-Net to regress coherence directly from detected SAR images. Trained on 12-day SLC pairs with ground-truth coherence from coregistered Sentinel-1 data, the model learns the mapping between backscatter magnitudes and coherence. Experimental results show the method achieves high-resolution coherence prediction with superior accuracy over intensity-based approaches, generalizes well across diverse geographic regions and temporal baselines unseen during training, and is applicable to globally available analysis-ready data such as ground range detected products.
为解决无需精确配准即可估计SAR相干性的需求，本研究提出一种基于残差U-Net的深度学习框架，直接从检测后的SAR图像回归相干性。模型在精确配准的Sentinel-1 SLC数据生成的相干性图上训练，并在多种具有不同辐射特性、几何形态和地理位置的数据集上评估。实验表明，该方法在高分辨率相干性估计上优于传统强度方法，具有良好的地理泛化能力，可适应训练时未见的时基间隔，并能利用全球可获取的分析就绪数据（如地面范围检测数据）实现大规模应用。
